### [1] [Complete Bernstein functions and scaled ultraspherical zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19186)
*K. Castillo*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文彻底解决了 Gautschi 提出的三个关于超球多项式正零点的高阶单调性问题，并为第四个猜想构造了反例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gautschi 提出了若干个关于超球多项式 $C_n^\lambda$ 正零点在尺度变换下是否具有完全单调性或完全 Bernstein 性质的高阶单调性问题，这些问题此前未获解决，其中第三个问题的印刷端点 $\lambda=0$ 是奇异的，需要重新表述。

Method: 通过引入参数区间的平移将零点标度函数转化为定义在 $(0,\infty)$ 上的函数，利用完全 Bernstein 函数与完全单调函数的理论，分别分析 $\sqrt{\lambda+1}\,z_{n,j}(\lambda)$、$\sqrt{\lambda}\,z_{n,j}(\lambda)$ 以及最大零点轨迹 $\sqrt{\lambda+\frac{2n^2+1}{4n+2}}\,z_{n,1}(\lambda)$ 的单调性；并对四次情形进行精确计算。

Result: 对于所有 $n\geq3$，$\sqrt{\lambda+1}\,z_{n,j}(\lambda)$ 在参数区间平移后成为完全 Bernstein 函数；对于所有 $n\geq2$，$\sqrt{\lambda}\,z_{n,j}(\lambda)$ 在 $(0,\infty)$ 上为完全 Bernstein 函数；对于所有 $n\geq4$，最大零点轨迹的相应标度具有同样性质，而其余正零点该标度的导数不是完全单调的。此外，四次情形的精确计算给出了关于线性标度 $\lambda z_{n,j}(\lambda)$ 的第四猜想的反例。

Conclusion: 三个高阶单调性问题均得到完全解决，第四个猜想被证伪，完善了超球多项式零点单调性理论。

Abstract: Let $z_{n,j}(λ)$ denote the positive zeros, in decreasing order, of the ultraspherical polynomial $C_n^λ$, $λ>-1/2$, with the reduced limiting interpretation at $λ=0$ specified below. Our principal result settles three higher-monotonicity questions of Gautschi: two as printed and the natural open-interval form of the third, whose printed endpoint $λ=0$ is singular. For every $n\geq3$, $$
  \sqrt{λ+1}\,z_{n,j}(λ) $$ becomes a complete Bernstein function after translation of its parameter interval to $(0,\infty)$. For every $n\geq2$, $$
  \sqrtλ\,z_{n,j}(λ) $$ is a complete Bernstein function on $(0,\infty)$. For every $n\geq4$, the largest-zero trajectory $$
  \sqrt{λ+\frac{2n^2+1}{4n+2}}\,z_{n,1}(λ) $$ has the same property, whilst for every other positive zero the derivative of this scaling fails to be completely monotone. Separately, an exact calculation in degree $4$ provides a counterexample to a fourth conjecture of Gautschi, concerning the linear scaling $λz_{n,j}(λ)$.

</details>


### [2] [Rigorous analysis of giant magnetic vortex strings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19411)
*Ovidiu Avadanei,Wilhelm Schlag*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 研究四元Abelian Higgs模型中轴对称涡旋在大磁通极限下的严格渐近分析，证明磁芯半径与物理文献预测一致，并建立各区域的匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Hakim-Lemaitre-Mallick '01及Dumitrescu-Gaikwad '25的形式大磁通展开提供严格的渐近分析基础。

Method: 对任意小于4的耦合常数，将磁芯与超导区界面等同于Ginzburg-Landau理论中的经典法向-超导转变层（由Chapman-Howison-McLeod-Ockendon '91定理保证其存在性、唯一性与单调性）；用Weber方程将标量场左侧高斯尾部与界面直接联系；将芯部Higgs方程作为半经典Sturm-Liouville问题处理；外部区域用修正Bessel函数作微扰描述；关键步骤是证明线性化界面方程两端可容许Cauchy数据间匹配映射满秩。

Result: 对每个小于4的耦合常数，证明磁芯半径达到物理文献预测的尺度；成功建立各区域（芯部、界面、外部）的渐近匹配结构。

Conclusion: 该工作为四元Abelian Higgs涡旋的大磁通渐近行为提供了严格数学证明，验证了物理文献中的形式展开结果。

Abstract: This paper studies axially symmetric Abrikosov-Nielsen-Olesen vortices in the quartic Abelian Higgs model as the magnetic flux becomes arbitrarily large. The emphasis is on developing the rigorous asymptotic analysis behind the formal large-flux expansions of Hakim, Lemaitre, and Mallick '01 and Dumitrescu, Gaikwad '25. For every coupling constant less than 4 we prove that the radius of the magnetic core is of the size predicted by the physics literature. We identify the interface between then normal and super-conducting regimes with the classical normal-to-superconducting transition layer in Ginzburg-Landau theory. The theorem of Chapman-Howison-McLeod-Ockendon '91 proves its existence, uniqueness and monotonicity. The connection of the Gaussian tail of the scalar field on the left with the interface is made directly with the Weber equation. The core Higgs equation is treated as a semiclassical Sturm-Liouville problem in the left overlap region, where the Higgs field decays like a Gaussian. The exterior region is described perturbatively around modified Bessel functions. A key step is to show that the matching map between the admissible Cauchy data at the two ends of the linearized interface equations has maximal rank.

</details>


### [3] [Product-profile anti-concentration for block-structured multi-affine polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19473)
*Evgeny Abakumov,Omer Friedland,Yosef Yomdin*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文对立方体上的多仿射多项式建立了乘积剖面型的反集中、密度与Remez估计，核心量为由变量分块$\\mathcal{B}$决定的尺度$w_d(\\mathcal{B})$，给出全中心小球估计、$L^p$密度界以及平移不变的商型Remez不等式，并证明这些界在块乘积模型上关于块尺寸向量是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多仿射多项式（即每个变量次数不超1）的小球概率与密度估计在组合、概率与泛函分析中有广泛应用，经典结果往往只给出与总变量数$n$和精确次数$d$相关的通用尺度$n^{d-1/2}$。然而当多项式具有变量分块结构（不同块变量间无同时出现的单项式）时，真实尺度应当更小。本文旨在引入一个能同时刻画分块结构与次数$d$的精确尺度$w_d(\mathcal{B})$，从而给出更精细、且在块乘积模型上最优的反集中、密度与Remez型估计。

Method: 对变量作分块$\\mathcal{B}$，要求任一单项式不含同一块中的两个变量，定义结构尺度$w_d(\\mathcal{B})=\\sum_{\\mathcal{I}\\subseteq\\mathcal{B},|\\mathcal{I}|=d-1}(\\prod_{B\\in\\mathcal{I}}\\sqrt{|B|})\\sqrt{\\sum_{C\\in\\mathcal{B}\\setminus\\mathcal{I}}|C|}$。证明方法结合：加权块选择、仿射立方体切片、降次收缩以及乘积剖面背后的精确递归；由此导出全中心小球估计$\\sup_u \\mathbb{P}\\{|P(X)-u|\\le\\rho\\}\\le\\Phi_d(C_d w_d(\\mathcal{B})\\rho/\\mathrm{osc}(P))$、密度$L^p$界$\\|f_P\\|_{L^p}\\le C_d p^{d-1}(w_d(\\mathcal{B})/\\mathrm{osc}(P))^{1-1/p}$，并应用于平移不变的商型Remez不等式。

Result: 得到全中心小球估计，其中$\Phi_d(t)=t\sum_{j=0}^{d-1}\log^j(1/t)/j!$在$(0,1]$上截断为1。单点分块给出通用尺度$n^{d-1/2}$；分成至多$q$块的可容许分块给出改进尺度$q^{(d-1)/2}n^{d/2}$；若$\mathcal{B}$恰有$d$块，则$w_d(\mathcal{B})=d\prod_{B\\in\\mathcal{B}}\\sqrt{|B|}$，且中心化块平均之积表明小球队列与对完整块尺寸向量的依赖在常数（仅依赖$d$）意义下均最优。密度估计在所有$1<p<\\infty$成立，并在块乘积模型上对$p\\ge2$具有匹配的$p$与块尺度增长。作为应用，导出具有相同结构尺度的平移不变商型Remez不等式。

Conclusion: 多仿射多项式的小球概率与密度可由分块结构尺度$w_d(\\mathcal{B})$精确控制，统一并推广了经典$n^{d-1/2}$型界；该尺度在块乘积模型上达到最优，并为Remez型不等式提供结构性刻画。

Abstract: We establish product-profile anti-concentration, density, and Remez estimates for multi-affine polynomials on the cube. Let $X$ be uniformly distributed on $[0,1]^n$, and let $P:[0,1]^n\to\R$ be a nonconstant multi-affine polynomial of exact degree $d$, with all variables active. For a partition $\cB$ of the variables such that no monomial contains two variables from the same block, define $$ w_d(\cB) = \sum_{\cI\subseteq\cB, \abs{\cI} = d-1} \left(\prod_{B\in\cI}\sqrt{\abs B}\right) \sqrt{\sum_{C\in\cB\setminus\cI}\abs C}. $$ We prove the all-center small-ball estimate $$ \sup_{u\in\R} \Prob\{\abs{P(X)-u}\leρ\} \le Φ_d\left( C_dw_d(\cB)\fracρ{\osc(P)} \right), $$ where $\osc(P)$ is the length of the range of $P$ and $Φ_d(t) = t\sum_{j = 0}^{d-1}\log^j(1/t)/j!$ on $(0,1]$, capped at one. Every multi-affine polynomial admits the singleton partition, which yields the universal scale $n^{d-1/2}$; an admissible partition into at most $q$ blocks yields the improved scale $q^{(d-1)/2}n^{d/2}$. If $\cB$ has exactly $d$ blocks, then $$ w_d(\cB) = d\prod_{B\in\cB}\sqrt{\abs B}, $$ and products of centered block averages show that both the small-ball profile and the dependence on the full block-size vector are optimal, up to constants depending only on $d$.
  We also prove that $P(X)$ has a density $f_P$ satisfying $$ \norm{f_P}_{L^p(\R)} \le C_dp^{d-1} \left( \frac{w_d(\cB)}{\osc(P)} \right)^{1-1/p}, \quad 1<p<\infty, $$ with matching $p$- and block-scale growth for $p\ge2$ on the block-product models. As an application, we derive translation-invariant quotient Remez inequalities with the same structural scale. The proof combines weighted block selection, affine cube slicing, degree-lowering contractions, and the exact recursion underlying the product profile.

</details>


### [4] [The divergence set for the wave equation in higher dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19691)
*Xiumin Du,Terence L. J. Harris,Jianhui Li*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 证明了R^{4+1}中波动方程在0.5<s≤0.55时，解沿时间趋于0时几乎处处点态收敛到初值，例外集Hausdorff维数不超过6-4s，并在小范围内验证了BBCR猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究波动方程低正则初值下解的点态收敛问题，特别是关于Barceló、Bennett、Carbery和Rogers提出的例外集维数猜想，旨在改进已有例外集界。

Method: 针对R^{4+1}波动方程，初值u0∈H^s、u1∈H^{s-1}，通过调和分析/傅里叶限制性估计等技术，估计点态收敛失败的例外集Hausdorff维数；并将方法推广到R^{n+1}，n≥4，1/2<s<n/4。

Result: 在0.5<s≤0.55时，例外集Hausdorff维数上界为6-4s；在一小段s范围内验证了BBCR猜想；对n≥4和1/2<s<n/4，给出了例外集界的部分改进。

Conclusion: 低正则波动方程的点态收敛几乎处处成立，例外集维数受控；结果支持并推进了BBCR猜想，并在更高维情形下部分改进了例外集估计。

Abstract: It is shown that if $u$ solves the wave equation in $\mathbb{R}^{4+1}$ with initial data $u(\cdot,0) = u_0(\cdot) \in H^s$ and $\partial_tu(\cdot,0) = u_1(\cdot ) \in H^{s-1}$, where $0.5 < s \leq 0.55$, then $u(x,t) \to u_0(x)$ and $\partial_tu(x,t) \to u_1(x)$ pointwise as $t \to 0$, for all $x$ outside an exceptional set of Hausdorff dimension at most $6-4s$. In a very small range of $s$, this verifies a conjecture of Barceló, Bennett, Carbery, and Rogers. More generally, a partial improvement to the exceptional set bound in $\mathbb{R}^{n+1}$ is obtained for $n \geq 4$ and $1/2 < s < n/4$.

</details>


### [5] [$L^p$ Decay Estimates for Circular Means of Fractal Measures in $\mathbb{R}^2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20108)
*Zhenbin Cao,Feilong Guo,Junfeng Li*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 研究平面非零曲率曲线Fourier延拓算子的加权$L^p\to L^q$限制估计，并应用于分形测度Fourier变换的圆平均$L^p$衰减估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 加权限制估计可统一刻画延拓算子在分形集上的可积性，并为分形测度Fourier变换的圆平均衰减提供新工具。

Method: 针对平面非零曲率曲线，建立$\|Ef\|_{L^q(X)}\le C_\epsilon R^\epsilon\|f\|_p$形式的加权限制估计（$X$为$\alpha$维集），并构造反例。

Result: 得到新的限制估计；由此导出$\mathbb{R}^2$中分形测度Fourier变换圆平均的新$L^p$衰减估计；对$p\in[1,2]$构造例子给出衰减指数的若干上界。

Conclusion: 加权限制估计为分形测度圆平均衰减提供新途径，且反例揭示了衰减指数的上界限制。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate weighted $L^p\rightarrow L^q$ restriction estimates for the Fourier extension operator associated with planar curves of non-vanishing curvature. More precisely, we establish estimates of the form \begin{equation}
  \|Ef\|_{L^q(X)}\leq C_εR^ε\|f\|_p, \end{equation} where $X$ is an $α$-dimensional set. As an application of this result, we derive a new $L^p$ decay estimate for circular means of Fourier transforms of fractal measures in $\mathbb{R}^2$. We also construct examples that give several upper bounds for the decay exponent when $p\in[1,2]$.

</details>


### [6] [Sharp mixed $A_p$-$A_\infty$ estimates for sparse operators on filtered and nonhomogeneous measure spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20531)
*Francisco Gonçalves,Emiel Lorist*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文在两个非二倍测度（non-doubling）框架下证明了稀疏算子的混合 $A_p$-$A_\infty$ 估计，并给出强型与弱型界，其权重特征的幂次与经典情形一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典混合 $A_p$-$A_\infty$ 估计多建立在二倍测度或二进结构上。作者希望在非二倍情形推广此类估计，并解决若干公开问题，如 Rubio de Francia 平方函数的尖锐弱型 $(2,2)$ 估计以及平衡非二倍测度上 Haar 位移的定量强型界。

Method: 方法分两个设定：其一，利用连续时间下的停时（stopping times）定义稀疏算子；其二，考察允许不同二进立方体在二进距离有界时相互作用的二进稀疏形式。通过发展相应的混合 $A_p$-$A_\infty$ 权重技术进行分析。

Result: 第一设定下获得强型与弱型界，权重特征幂次与经典情形相同；部分弱型界即使对 $\mathbb R^d$ 上二进滤链也是新的，并推出 Rubio de Francia 平方函数的尖锐弱型 $(2,2)$ 估计，解决了 Garg、Roncal 与 Shrivastava (2021) 的公开问题。第二设定下获得强型界，作为一维应用得到平衡非二倍测度上 Haar 位移的强型界，回答了 Conde-Alonso、Pipher 与 Wagner (2025) 的定量问题。

Conclusion: 本文表明混合 $A_p$-$A_\infty$ 估计可推广至非二倍测度与连续时间稀疏算子等非经典框架，并获得与经典情形相同的权重依赖幂次，同时解决了若干已发表文献中的公开问题。

Abstract: We prove mixed $A_p$-$A_\infty$ estimates for sparse operators in two non-doubling settings. In the first setting, we consider sparse operators defined using stopping times in continuous time. We obtain both strong- and weak-type bounds with the same powers of the weight characteristics as in the classical setting. Some of our weak-type bounds are even new for the dyadic filtration on $\mathbb R^d$ and, in particular, imply a sharp weak-type $(2,2)$ estimate for Rubio de Francia square functions, solving a problem left open by Garg, Roncal and Shrivastava [J. Geom. Anal., 31:748-771, 2021].
  In the second setting, we consider dyadic sparse forms in which distinct cubes may interact, provided their dyadic distance is bounded. We obtain strong-type bounds with the same powers of the weight characteristics as in the classical setting. As a one-dimensional application, we obtain strong-type bounds for Haar shifts over balanced non-doubling measures, answering a quantitative question posed by Conde-Alonso, Pipher, and Wagner [Math. Ann., 391:2209-2253, 2025].

</details>


### [7] [Universal completeness of exponentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20805)
*Susanna Bertolini,Enric Florit-Simon,Lukas Liehr,Mitchell A. Taylor*

Main category: math.CA

TL;DR: 本文研究经典Fourier唯一性定理的推广，构造具有完全性的指数系统对应的一致离散频率集，并证明周期性弱间隙谱唯一集的Sobolev正则性条件α>1/2是sharp的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Fourier唯一性定理表明，若函数及其Fourier变换支集测度小于1则函数为零。本文旨在推广该定理，探索指数系统在一般可测集上的完全性，并刻画实现这种普适性所需频率集的密度与结构条件，同时确定相关Sobolev正则性阈值的sharp性。

Method: 构造均匀密度为1的一致离散集Λ⊂ℝ，使指数系统{e^{2πiλx}:λ∈Λ}对任意1≤p<∞及任意|S|<1的可测集S在L^p(S)中完全；证明渐近整数集不具此普适性；对每个v∈(0,1)构造均匀密度v的整数频率集，使其指数系统在|S|<v的S⊂[0,1]上完全；并通过构造性反例证明α>1/2的Sobolev正则性条件sharp；结果推广到高维并用Lean验证。

Result: 存在密度为1的一致离散频率集，其指数系统对所有测度小于1的可测集在L^p中完全，且渐近整数集无此性质；对任意v∈(0,1)存在密度v的整数频率集在测度小于v的[0,1]子集上完全；确认Olevskii与Ulanovskii考虑的周期性弱间隙谱唯一集的Sobolev条件α>1/2为sharp。

Conclusion: 本文推广了经典Fourier唯一性定理，刻画了指数系统在L^p(S)中完全性对应的频率集密度结构，证明了周期弱间隙谱唯一集Sobolev正则性阈值α>1/2的sharp性，结果可推广至高维并经Lean形式化验证。

Abstract: We consider generalizations of the classical Fourier uniqueness theorem. First, we construct a family of uniformly discrete sets $Λ\subset \mathbb{R}$, of uniform density one, such that the exponential system $\{e^{2πiλx} : λ\in Λ\}$ is complete in $L^p(S)$ for every $1 \leq p < \infty$ and every measurable set $S \subset \mathbb{R}$ with $|S| < 1$. We also show that no set that is asymptotically integer can have this universality property. Additionally, for every $v \in (0,1)$, we construct a set of integer frequencies and uniform density $v$ whose exponential system is complete in $L^p(S)$ for every $1 \leq p < \infty$ and every measurable set $S \subset [0,1]$ with $|S| < v$. Finally, we prove that the Sobolev regularity condition $α> \frac12$ for the existence of uniformly discrete uniqueness sets for spectra with periodic weak gaps, considered by Olevskii and Ulanovskii, is sharp. Our findings admit extensions to higher dimensions and are verified in Lean.

</details>

### [8] [$L^2$-regularity of minimizers of anisotropic interaction functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19181)
*Tristan Bullion-Gauthier,Kai Xiao*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 研究各向异性核 $W$ 与一般约束势 $V$ 构成的交互泛函 $\mathscr E$，在 $W$ 及其傅里叶变换的标准假设下，证明极小元具有 $L^2$ 密度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对带有各向异性核与一般约束势的非局部交互泛函，其极小元密度的正则性问题此前缺乏系统性结论，作者希望在此一般框架下建立极小元的 $L^2$ 可积性。

Method: 通过对非局部变分不等式进行细致分析，结合对核 $W$ 及其傅里叶变换的标准假设来证明极小元的 $L^2$ 密度性质。

Result: 在标准假设下证明了交互泛函 $\mathscr E$ 的极小元具有 $L^2$ 密度。

Conclusion: 该结果推广了各向异性核与一般约束势情形下极小元的正则性，且所用的非局部变分不等式分析具有独立价值。

Abstract: We consider interaction functionals of the form \begin{equation*} ν\mapsto {\mathscr E}(ν)=\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N}\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N} W(x-y)\intdν(y)\intdν(x)+\int\_{{\mathbb R}^N} V(x)\intdν(x)\text{,} \end{equation*} involving an anisotropic kernel $W$ and a general confinement potential $V$. Under standard assumptions on $W$ and its Fourier transform, we show that the minimizer of ${\mathscr E}$ has $L^2$ density. Our argument relies on a careful analysis of nonlocal variational inequalities, which may be of independent interest.

</details>

In the spirit of Arnold's formulation of ideal hydrodynamics, a solution is regarded as a path of volume-preserving diffeomorphisms; the proof is then based on the interplay between classical convex integration techniques and geometric constructions at the level of the Lie algebra of this Lie group. Our work substantially extends the recent result of Enciso, Peñafiel-Tomás and Peralta-Salas \cite{EnPePe} and can be used to reprove \cite{GiRa} of Giri and Radu without a Newton iteration.

Abstract: We study global energy minimization for matrix-valued heteroclinic connections whose vacuum sets are continuous conjugacy orbits rather than isolated points. For two distinguished parameter regimes of a one-dimensional $\mathrm{SU}(5)\times\mathbb Z_2$ matrix field model, we first identify the full zero set of the quartic potential as the disjoint union of two compact homogeneous vacuum orbits. We then construct auxiliary potentials controlled by the Frobenius distance to these orbits. Pointwise comparison with the original potential, combined with a calibration inequality and the one-dimensional coarea formula, gives a sharp lower bound for every finite-energy connection joining the two vacuum components; no diagonality, commutativity, or symmetry-reduced ansatz is imposed on the competitors. Two explicit non-Abelian kinks attain the bound and are therefore global minimizers in the full orbit-to-orbit heteroclinic classes. Their exact wall tensions are $4\sqrt2\,μ^3/(3λ)$ and $9\sqrt2\,μ^3/(10h)$, respectively. Equivalently, the corresponding line-segment paths realize the degenerate weighted distance between the vacuum manifolds. We also resolve every fixed-endpoint sector: for arbitrary prescribed representatives on the two vacuum orbits, the energy infimum is the same wall tension, and it is attained if and only if the prescribed representatives form a closest pair of the two orbits. Nonclosest endpoint sectors therefore exhibit non-attainment through increasingly slow tangential motion along the vacuum manifolds. The proof separates a model-independent distance--coarea criterion from the model-specific orbit geometry and polynomial positivity estimates, providing a reusable mechanism for matrix-valued multiwell potentials with symmetry-generated vacua.

Abstract: In this paper, we consider the inequality $Δ_pu+Δ_qu+u^s|\nabla u|^t\leq 0$ on geodesically complete noncompact Riemannian manifolds. Abstract: In this article, we investigate the inverse problem of determining time-dependent first- and zeroth-order coefficients for a wave equation with nonlocal-in-time attenuation on a Riemannian manifold, using boundary measurements. This problem is motivated by applications in imaging and viscoelasticity, where such equations arise naturally, and we aim to characterize the properties of the underlying medium by recovering these coefficients. Our main objective is to exploit the memory properties of solutions induced by nonlocal attenuation, establishing the recovery of a general class of lower-order coefficients from boundary measurements collected over an arbitrarily small time interval. As a byproduct of our analysis, we also prove recovery results for time-dependent coefficients using measurements supported on disjoint time intervals. While such data restrictions are unavailable, and generally impossible, for the classical wave equation, we demonstrate that they become feasible under standard assumptions due to the presence of nonlocal-in-time attenuation. Our analysis combines techniques from differential geometry, the theory of partial differential equations with nonlocal terms, complex analysis, and the theory of nonlocal operators.

Abstract: We give an alternative proof of a theorem of Brena, De Lellis and Franceschini (arXiv:2503.00649): the support of a stationary integral $d$ dimensional varifold in an open subset of $\mathbf{R}^n$ is $(\mathscr{H}^d, d)$ rectifiable of class $(k, α)$ for every positive integer $k$ and every $0 < α< 1$, and in particular of class $\mathscr{C}^{\infty}$. Our proof is independent of theirs and proceeds by different means. It compares the varifold, at every scale and at $\mathscr{H}^d$ almost every point, with the graph of a solution of the Euler--Lagrange system of a smoothed area integrand, and thereby derives an excess-decay estimate that is faster than any power of the scale; the conclusion then follows from Santilli's characterisation of higher order rectifiability together with Whitney's extension theorem.

Abstract: Blow-up in the self-dual Chern--Simons--Higgs equation has a rich multiscale structure, but the detailed theory has largely focused on the simple regime, where one entire profile resolves each concentration point. By contrast, non-simple blow-up is analytically more delicate: several interacting regular cores must be resolved simultaneously as they collapse toward a single vortex while remaining separated on their own scales. We determine their possible total masses and derive a mass-gap criterion that rules out such blow-up for certain vortex configurations. Our quantitative profile and moment estimates for general finite-height clusters guide the design of the approximate solution and clarify the core interactions underlying the finite-height construction. Abstract: The paper studies the weighted dual Minkowski problem for the weighted $q$th dual curvature measure
  of convex bodies in $\mathbb R^n$ first posed by Huang, Lutwak, Yang, and Zhang.
  Most of the results are in the G-invariant setting,
  i.e. when the convex bodies are invariant under a closed subgroup $G$ of $O(n)$.
  They generalize previous results on the existence of solutions to the dual Minkowski problem
  for origin-symmetric convex bodies.
  It is proved:
  (i) a full characterization of the G-invariant weighted dual curvature measure for q\in(0,1],
  (ii) a necessary and sufficient condition for the existence of solutions to the G-invariant weighted
  dual Minkowski problem for q\in(1,n),

Using Fourier-Laplace transform techniques and a direct convolution-kernel construction, we prove the existence and uniqueness of exponentially bounded measure solutions for measure data. Moreover, we identify a necessary and sufficient condition on the source term for conservation of unit mass and provide separate sufficient conditions for non-negativity and weak convergence to $δ_0$.

Abstract: In this paper, we study the existence of nonnegative bound states for the higher-order logarithmic Schrödinger equation $$ -αΔu+V(x)u-βu\ln|u| = γu(\ln|u|)^m, \qquad u\in H^1(\mathbb R^N), $$ where $α,β,γ>0$, $m$ is an odd positive integer and $V$ is a positive bounded potential converging to a constant at infinity. We introduce a family of power-law approximations and derive estimates that are uniform as the approximation exponent tends to the logarithmic limit. Under a structural condition on the autonomous nonlinearity, we obtain uniqueness, up to translations, of the nonnegative autonomous profile with connected positivity set and a corresponding energy gap below the two-profile threshold. The small-amplitude behavior exhibits a maximum-principle/compact-support dichotomy: the case $m=1$ yields positivity, whereas the higher odd orders fall into the compact-support regime for the autonomous profile. We then establish a profile decomposition for constrained Palais-Smale sequences and construct a barycenter-based min-max level below the splitting threshold. This prevents loss of mass through multiple profiles and allows us to pass to the logarithmic limit. More importantly, the dominant factor extraction method developed in this work provides a general framework for the stability analysis of complex coupled fluid systems.

Abstract: Viscosity solutions of eikonal equations need not enjoy regularity beyond Lipschitz continuity. In contrast, differentiable solutions exhibit stronger structural properties. The key stability estimates imply that the error introduced at each stage becomes arbitrarily small when the next approximation time is taken sufficiently far in the future, allowing the construction to be iterated indefinitely.

Moreover, the pathwise trace is not automatically measurable because its exceptional sets may depend on the realization. Abstract: We prove local regularity for axisymmetric suitable weak solutions of the 3D Navier-Stokes equations, which are smooth before the terminal time $t=0$, and satisfy Type~II pointwise bounds at a vanishing length scale $\ell(t)$. We say $\ell(t)$ is an Euler length if it is non-increasing, satisfies a doubling condition, and if $\ell(t)\to0$ and $(-t)/\ell(t)^2\to0$ as $t\to0^-$. This includes power laws $\ell(t)=(-t)^γ$ with $0<γ<1/2$, and logarithmic enlargements of the parabolic length. Our main result shows that local bounds of the type $|u(\cdot,t)| \leq C\ell(t)/(-t)$ and $|\nabla^2 u(\cdot,t)|\leq C/((-t)\ell(t))$ for all $t\in (-1,0)$ imply regularity. The proof adapts the circulation and potential-vorticity argument of our earlier paper~\cite{CIV26} to ancient limits obtained from the rescaled vorticity system by zooming in. We show that the second derivative a priori assumption may be replaced by a Hölder bound on the azimuthal vorticity and a corresponding bound on its potential vorticity.

Abstract: We study a singular Dirichlet problem driven by a regularized $p(x)$-Laplacian under a prescribed modular constraint. Using growth and differentiability estimates for the regularized energy, together with coercivity and modular compactness, we construct nonnegative constrained minimizers for a family of nonsingular approximating functionals. Under the admissibility of signed constrained variations, each minimizer satisfies an approximate Euler--Lagrange equation with a uniquely determined constraint multiplier. An explicit uniform boundary lower barrier and a compatible weight condition, combined with a variable exponent Hardy inequality, provide global dual control of the singular sources and convergence against every zero-trace Sobolev test function. For these approximate minimizing pairs, the singular-limit result gives subsequential strong Sobolev convergence of the states and convergence of their multipliers, while preserving positivity and the prescribed modular. The limiting pair is therefore a positive normalized weak solution of the singular problem, obtained by removing the reaction regularization while keeping the gradient regularization fixed.

</details>

Abstract: OpenAI~\cite{OpenAIManuscript} has recently announced a proof of finite time singularity formation for the 3D Navier-Stokes equations, in the presence of a $C^\infty$-smooth body force. The construction in~\cite{OpenAIManuscript} has a few key features, among which we single out: (i) the angular mean of the solution satisfies specific Type II bounds which are anisotropic; (ii) the solution is exactly axisymmetric in a collapsing core region. In this paper we consider solutions of the 3D Navier-Stokes equations in the presence of a real-analytic body force, and assume that these solutions satisfy properties (i) and (ii) above. We prove that such solutions are in fact regular at the putative singular point. As a consequence, in the construction of~\cite{OpenAIManuscript}, and in any construction with properties (i) and (ii) whose force remains bounded in $C^2$ up to the singular time, the force can neither vanish identically near the singular point, nor be real analytic in the space variables, locally uniformly in time. The main idea of the proof is inspired by our earlier work~\cite{CIV26} and the companion paper~\cite{CIVEulerLength}: zooming-in at the putative singularity using the anisotropic length scales provided by the a priori bounds, we arrive at ancient limits whose PDE evolution imposes additional rigidity.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定Toeplitz算子生成的C*-代数何时是交换的，是Toeplitz算子理论的核心问题之一；在加权Fock空间上，符号的不变性与群表示的分解结构可能决定交换性，需要系统刻画并给出特征值表示。

Conclusion: 径向加权Fock空间上Toeplitz算子生成C*-代数的交换性可由群表示的多重无重性完全刻画，且特征值可用于构造显式积分表示；但一般地，平方根度量下一致连续的有界序列C*-代数未必等于特征值序列的均匀闭包。

Abstract: We study Toeplitz operators acting on radial weighted Fock spaces. We use tools from representation theory to construct commutative families of $C^*$-algebras that are generated by Toeplitz operators whose symbols are invariant under the action of $\U(n)$. For a partition $m=(b_1,...,b_k)$ of an integer $n$, we realize \[\mathbf{\U_m}:=\U(b_1)\times...\times\U(b_k)\] as a block diagonally subgroup of $\U(n)$ and describe the decomposition of the weighted Fock space into irreducible $\mathbf{U_m}$-modules. This allow us to study Toeplitz operators with symbols that are invariant under $\mathbf{U_m}$ and $k$-quasi-radial symbols, and we provide and explicit integral representation of their eigenvalues. More generally, for an arbitrary compact subgroup $H\subseteq\U(n)$, we characterize the commutativity of the $C^*$-algebra generated by $H$-invariant Toeplitz operators in terms of the multiplicity-free property of the representation $π|_H$. Finally, for a logarithmically growing radial weight, we construct a bounded radial symbol for which the corresponding eigenvalue sequence not uniformly continuous with respect to the square root metric. Consequently, the uniform closure of the set of eigenvalue sequences does not coincide with the $C^*$-algebra of bounded sequences that are uniformly continuous with respect to the square root metric.

</details>

Abstract: Christensen and Pedersen proved that every properly infinite $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebra is monotone sequentially complete. We isolate the part of their argument that extends to an arbitrary cardinal. The property of properly infinite $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebras used in their proof is the existence of an orthogonal sequence of projections, each equivalent to $1$, with sum $1$. We generalize this by defining a notion of $κ$-homogeneity, with the case $κ= \aleph_{0}$ recovering the aforementioned property. $κ$-homogeneity supplies the fresh orthogonal space needed at each successor stage of a transfinite projection dilation, and normality of $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebras supplies, at every limit stage and again at the end of the construction, the passage from a join of projections to a supremum in the self-adjoint order. This second point replaces both the addability theorem and the perturbation argument used in the countable case. We prove that every $κ$-homogeneous $\mathrm{AW}^*$-algebra is $κ$-monotone complete, and that $κ$-monotone completeness together with $κ$ ordinary states separating positive elements from zero implies monotone completeness. As an application, an $\mathrm{AW}^*$-factor with a corner admitting a faithful state is monotone complete.

</details>

Main category: math.CO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lo、Treglown 和 Zhao 在研究 $K_4$-free 图族时，将 $f(n,7,4)$ 列为唯一未解决的情形。本文旨在补全这一缺口，确定该函数对所有 $n\ge1$ 的取值。

Method: 首先将对应的三色极值 $\delta(n,7,3)$ 约化为一个 $7\times3$ 整数矩阵问题并精确求解；随后通过结构论证证明：任何最小度 $\delta(G)>\frac{132}{31}n$ 的平衡 $7$-部 $K_4$-free 图都是三色可染的。

Result: 对于所有 $n\ge1$，$f(n,7,4)=\lfloor30n/7\rfloor$，但当 $n\equiv4\pmod7$ 且 $n\ge11$ 时，该值为 $\lfloor30n/7\rfloor-1$。同时精确确定了 $\delta(n,7,3)$。

Conclusion: 本文完整解决了 Lo–Treglown–Zhao 提出的 $K_4$-free 族中最后一个未决情形，给出了 $f(n,7,4)$ 的精确表达式，并揭示了其与三色可染性阈值之间的结构联系。

Abstract: For integers $n,r,t$ with $2\le t\le r-1$, let $f(n,r,t+1)$ denote the largest possible minimum degree of a balanced $r$-partite graph with parts of size $n$ and containing no copy of $K_{t+1}$. Lo, Treglown and Zhao identified $f(n,7,4)$ as the only remaining case in their treatment of the $K_4$-free family. I determine this function for every $n\ge1$. First, the corresponding three-colourable extremum $δ(n,7,3)$ is reduced to a $7\times3$ integer matrix problem and determined exactly. Second, a structural argument shows that every balanced $7$-partite $K_4$-free graph $G$ with $δ(G)>\frac{132}{31}n$ is three-colourable. Consequently, $f(n,7,4)=\lfloor30n/7\rfloor$ except when $n\equiv4\pmod7$ and $n\ge11$, where it is one less.

</details>


### [56] [Lean-Certified Infinite Counterexamples to Written on the Wall II Conjecture 194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19195)

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了一族四参数的反例图，其两参数主子族满足 Written on the Wall II 猜想 194 的不等式（甚至取等号），却不含哈密顿路径，从而推翻该猜想；该无限子族已在 Lean 4 中形式化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Written on the Wall II 猜想 194 断言：若连通图满足 alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G)，则存在哈密顿路径。作者旨在通过构造反例检验并否证这一猜想，并进一步探讨最小度下界是否能补救该猜想。

Result: 该两参数子族恰好使猜想中的不等式取等号，却不存在哈密顿路径；(s,t)=(1,3) 时对应 18 个顶点的具体图。形式化证书是参数化的，而非仅验证单个图。因此任意固定的最小度下界都无法修补该猜想。

Conclusion: 猜想 194 被无限族反例否证；最小度条件无法挽救该命题；形式化验证提升了结果的可靠性。

Abstract: For a finite simple graph G, let alpha(G) denote its independence number and let
  l_avg(G) = (1 / |V(G)|) sum_{v in V(G)} alpha(G[N_G(v)])
  be the average independence number of its open neighbourhoods. Written on the Wall II Conjecture 194 asserts that every simple connected graph on n > 1 vertices satisfying alpha(G) <= 1 + l_avg(G) has a Hamiltonian path. We give a four-parameter family of counterexamples. Its principal two-parameter subfamily satisfies the proposed inequality with equality: for every pair of integers s >= 1 and t >= 3 it has (s + 1)t^2 vertices, independence number t + 1, l_avg(G) = t, and minimum degree s, but has no Hamiltonian path. This entire infinite subfamily is machine-checked in Lean 4: one universally quantified theorem certifies its order, connectivity, independence number, average neighbourhood independence, minimum degree, conjecture hypothesis, and failure of traceability. Thus no fixed lower bound on the minimum degree repairs the conjecture. The case (s,t) = (1,3) has 18 vertices, but the formal certificate is parametric rather than a verification of that one graph alone.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Socolar与Taylor提出是否存在单个、单连通的3维原型块仅凭形状强制非周期性、且不允许弱非周期铺砌的问题。已有的Schmitt-Conway-Danzer双棱柱和3维Socolar-Taylor块分别具有螺旋运动或周期堆叠方向，因此并不完全满足"仅由形状强制非周期"的要求，需要构造更强的不可能周期化的单块。

Method: 构造一个有理多面体3-球Q（Chair44，七立方体椅子），其24个外露单位面板带有微小方锥特征。设计依据Six Birds emergence calculus对非周期单块现象的理解；构造依赖一个有限测试（机器检验）：块自身接触规则在粗化后仍成立，使解码出的父铺砌遵守该块的规则且不遵守其他规则。证明由书面几何论证（特征迫使任何铺砌落在注册格点上）与穷举有限枚举相结合；伴随普查由两个独立实现复现，每个有限关卡在Lean 4中内核检查，书面几何引理与逻辑组装亦为Lean定理。

Result: Q允许用全等副本（允许反射）铺满R^3；任何此类铺砌都没有平移周期，对称群阶至多24；每个铺砌都是同手性的，并携带唯一的无限嵌套超块层级。定理在命名编译器钩子的前提下经Lean 4内核检查，书面证明作为说明。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了一个单连通、仅凭形状强制非周期性的3维单块，回答了Socolar-Taylor问题，并通过Lean 4形式化与双实现普查提高了证明可信度。

Abstract: Socolar and Taylor asked for a single, simply connected three-dimensional prototile that forces nonperiodicity by shape alone, admitting no weakly nonperiodic tiling; the Schmitt-Conway-Danzer biprism and the three-dimensional Socolar-Taylor tile admit screw motions or a periodic stacking direction. We exhibit a rational polyhedral $3$-ball $Q$, which we call Chair44 (R44): a seven-cube chair whose $24$ exposed unit panels carry tiny square-pyramid features, and prove, as a proof submission, that $Q$ admits tilings of $\mathbb{R}^3$ by congruent copies, reflections allowed, and that every such tiling has no translational period and a symmetry group of order at most $24$; every tiling is homochiral and carries a unique infinite hierarchy of nested supertiles. The solid was designed to a reading of the aperiodic-monotile phenomenon reached with the Six Birds emergence calculus (Section 3.3), and the construction turns on a single finite test, checked by machine: the tile's own contact rule survives coarsening, so that the decoded parent tiling obeys the tile's rule and no other. The proof combines a written geometric argument, that the features force every tiling onto a registered lattice, with exhaustive finite enumerations; the companion census is replayed by two independent implementations, every finite gate is kernel-checked in Lean 4 (modulo a named compiler hook per native_decide theorem), and the written geometric lemmas and the logical assembly are Lean theorems as well, so that the theorem is kernel-checked modulo the named compiler hooks; the written proofs remain as exposition.

</details>


### [58] [From one generator to loop order on the three-cube](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19218)
*Jonathan Washburn,Milan Zlatanović*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维立方体图 Q3 上基于闭合游走的幺半群 W，其基本群为自由群 F5。作者证明：任何加性一步读数（边权的阿贝尔群和）都通过有向转移计数分解，但转移计数相等并不意味着约化环字相同；同时刻画了若干非交换识别商、有限与无界记忆的分离，以及占据型约束的矩形性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是探究“交换式聚合”能否决定路径/游走的非交换结构（约化环字），即边权求和、转移计数等阿贝尔不变量是否足以恢复真正携带自由群信息的约化环字。作者希望在 Q3 这一基本群为 F5 的简洁模型上，精确刻画阿贝尔聚合与非交换识别之间的鸿沟，并进一步研究记忆容量、占据约束及四元数时钟等不同识别机制。

Method: 以三维立方体 Q3 的 1-骨架为基础闭合游走幺半群 W 为对象。方法包括：(1) 分析加性一步读数（边权在阿贝尔群中求和）并证明其通过有向转移计数分解；(2) 构造两条转移计数相同但约化环字不同的闭合游走，证明交换聚合不能决定约化环字；(3) 研究约化环字给出的真非交换识别商，并确定阿贝尔化平凡但二阶换位子信息非平凡的最短闭合游走；(4) 构造两状态读数与阶对、任意 k 状态读数与阶乘对的分离，以及无界栈恢复约化环字；(5) 分析顶点集整值函数上的势读数与占据绑定，证明占据型约束矩形性的等价条件；(6) 引入声明式四分之一转四元数时钟，构造第二个真阶敏感同余并讨论其不可比性，最后推广到所有超立方体 Qn（n≥3）。

Result: 主要结果：(1) 每个加性一步读数都通过有向转移计数分解；(2) 存在转移计数相同、约化环字不同的两条闭合游走，说明交换聚合不能确定约化环字；(3) 约化环字给出真非交换识别商；(4) 最短的具有平凡阿贝尔化但非平凡二阶换位子信息的闭合游走在立方体边度量下长度为 14；(5) 有限与无界记忆分离：两状态读数可分离一个阶对，但对每个 k≥1 存在显式阶乘对，任何 k 状态读数都无法分离，而无界栈可在每条游走上恢复约化环字；(6) 顶点整值函数的势读数在闭合游走上为零，但占据绑定不是矩形的；更一般地，某类历史上的每个基于占据的约束是矩形的，当且仅当占据向量在该类上为常数；(7) 声明式四分之一转四元数时钟给出第二个真阶敏感同余，与约化字商不可比；(8) 交换恢复的障碍与最小值 14 在每个超立方体 Qn（n≥3）上仍然成立。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，在 Q3（及所有 Qn, n≥3）上，阿贝尔聚合（边权和、转移计数）无法恢复约化环字所携带的非交换自由群信息；约化环字提供真非交换识别商，并存在长度 14 的最短“阿贝尔化平凡但换位子信息非平凡”游走。有限状态读数在表达能力上严格弱于无界栈，势读数在闭合游走上消失而占据约束的矩形性等价于占据向量在相应类上为常数。四元数时钟给出另一不可比的阶敏感同余。整体上，这些结果刻画了交换不变量与非交换识别之间的精确边界。

Abstract: We study the monoid $\mathcal W$ of based closed walks on the $1$-skeleton of the three-dimensional cube $Q_3$. The fundamental group of this graph is the free group $F_5$. We prove that every additive one-step reading, given by a sum of edge weights in an abelian group, factors through the directed transition counts, and we exhibit two closed walks with equal transition counts and different reduced loop words. Hence commutative aggregation does not determine the reduced loop word.
  The reduced loop word gives a proper noncommutative recognition quotient. We also determine the shortest closed walk with trivial abelianization but nontrivial degree-two commutator information. Its minimum length in the cube edge metric is $14$.
  We also obtain a separation between finite and unbounded memory. A two-state reading separates an order pair, while for every $k\geq1$, there is an explicit factorial pair which no $k$-state reading separates. An unbounded stack recovers the reduced loop word on every walk.
  For integer-valued functions on the vertex set, potential readings vanish on closed walks, while occupation binding is not rectangular. More generally, every occupation-based constraint is rectangular on a class of histories if and only if the occupation vector is constant on that class.
  Finally, a declared quarter-turn quaternion clock gives a second proper order-sensitive congruence, incomparable with the reduced-word quotient. The obstruction to commutative recovery, and the minimum $14$, remain valid on every hypercube $Q_n$, $n\geq3$.

</details>


### [59] [Intractable enumeration problems are like Russian nesting dolls: structural properties of monomer-dimer coverings on two-dimensional quadratic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19231)
*Yong Kong*

Main category: math.CO

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维格上二聚体（dimer）覆盖计数属于 #P-complete 类，通常被认为难以精确求解。作者希望揭示该问题精确解的内在结构，并给出可直接计算的显式表达，从而在特定参数 s 下提供可行算法。

Method: 针对二维方形格上 s 个二聚体的覆盖数，推导其精确解的显式公式，公式包含 s-1 重嵌套求和；通过该嵌套结构组织计算，得到复杂度为 O(2^s) 的算法，并识别出决定解的一条呈双指数增长的序列。

Result: 得到计数问题的显式精确解，代价为 O(2^s) 的指数级时间复杂度；解的结构由一条双指数增长的序列确定。

Conclusion: 尽管二聚体覆盖计数整体属 #P-complete，但对固定 s 的情形，可通过含 s-1 重嵌套求和的显式公式在 O(2^s) 时间内精确求解，为理解该类计数问题的精确结构提供了新视角。

Abstract: Counting the number of coverings of $s$ dimers on two-dimensional quadratic lattices is considered as intractable and belongs to \#P-complete class. We reveal the structure of the exact solution to the problem and provide an explicit formula for it, which includes $s-1$ nesting sums. This results in an exponential time complexity of $O(2^s)$. The solution is explicitly determined by a sequence that exhibits double-exponential growth.

</details>


### [60] [A Complete Proof of the Strong Conjecture about $F$-Irregular Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19245)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Chartrand等(1987)提出经典存在性猜想，问是否存在无穷多个顶点F-度互不相同的图；Dovzhenok、Filuta和Chuhai(2024)进一步提出强猜想：对任意至少三阶的连通图F均成立。本文旨在证明该强猜想。

Method: 利用F-度的定义（图中包含给定顶点的、与F同构的子图个数），构造性地证明：对任意连通图F（阶数≥3），可构造出无穷多个所有顶点F-度两两不同的图。文中为原2024年2月手稿的授权英文译本。

Result: 成功证明了关于F-不规则图的强猜想，即对每个阶数≥3的连通图F，存在无穷多个F-不规则图。

Conclusion: 该结果从根本上推广了Chartrand等(1987)的经典存在性猜想，确立了F-不规则图在一般连通模式图下的大范围存在性。

Abstract: A graph $G$ is called $F$-irregular if all its vertices have distinct $F$-degrees, defined as the number of subgraphs of $G$ isomorphic to a given graph $F$ and containing the respective vertex. We prove the Strong Conjecture about $F$-irregular graphs (Dovzhenok, Filuta, and Chuhai, 2024), which states that for every connected graph $F$ of order at least three, there exist infinitely many $F$-irregular graphs. Fundamentally generalizing the classical existence conjecture by Chartrand et al. (1987), this work presents an authorized English translation of our original February 2024 manuscript, which was publicly presented in full at two scientific conferences the same year.

</details>


### [61] [Schrijver-Delsarte rigidity in association schemes and undecidability of quantum graph homomorphism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20678)

TL;DR: 本文证明了由经典度量结合方案导出的图族（Kneser图、q-Kneser图，以及Johnson、Grassmann、Hamming图的补图）参数化的量子同态问题是RE-完全的。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We prove RE-completeness of the quantum homomorphism problem parameterised by families of graphs derived from the classic metric association schemes. These include Kneser graphs, $q$-Kneser graphs, and the complements of Johnson, Grassmann, and Hamming graphs. Our proof develops a spectral method for establishing non-contextuality of quantum polymorphisms. It combines an equality analysis of Roberson's bound on the projective packing number in terms of Schrijver's theta with a structural argument inspired by Erdős-Ko-Rado theory.

Abstract: We prove that there is an absolute constant $c>0$ such that for every finite $(α,M)$-Ahlfors--David regular set $A\subseteq[N]$ with $0\leqα<1$ and $M\geq2$, we have the sumset estimate \[|A+A|\geq |A|^{1+c(1-α)/\log M}.\] We also prove a corresponding statement for Ahlfors--David regular sets in $[0,1]$. The proof combines a multiscale entropy decomposition with inverse results from additive combinatorics, showing that Ahlfors--David regularity forces a definite entropy gain at each scale. Abstract: Posets of integer partitions, ordered by refinement, were criticized for unruly structural behavior by both Birkhoff and Ziegler. However, recent work of Propp, McConville, and Sagan demonstrated that such posets of hyperbinary partitions are distributive, with posets of join irreducible elements isomorphic to the well-studied class of fence posets. We provide a generalization of this result for hyper $(b,t)$-ary partitions, where all parts are powers of some positive integer $b$, and multiplicities are restricted to $t\ge b$. Then, we show that posets of hyper $(b,t)$-ary partitions are indeed also distributive lattices.

</details>


### [64] [The phylogenetic rank of a graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19372)
*Franklin Ashworth,Oliver Clarke,Jeffrey Giansiracusa,Jackson Jones,Julio Quijas-Aceves,Yue Ren*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究图的Pachter-Sturmfels系统发育秩（等距嵌入所需度量树的最少数目），提出贪心与精确算法，构建含6、7个顶点所有图的秩数据库，并给出若干反例及理论刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统发育秩是Pachter与Sturmfels提出的图论—度量几何交叉概念，用于刻画图作为度量树乘积（上确界范数）子图的嵌入复杂度。此前缺乏高效计算手段，且关于其性质（遗传性、上界、广义4点猜想）的若干自然猜想尚待验证，作者希望系统计算并澄清这些结构性质。

Method: 定义图为边长1的图，乘积空间赋予上确界范数，系统发育秩为等距嵌入所需最小度量树数。作者设计了贪心算法与精确算法来计算该秩；用算法穷举计算所有6、7个顶点的图的系统发育秩，建立数据库；并给出秩为1图的完整分类及特定无限族的构造证明。

Result: 通过计算数据库发现：系统发育秩不是遗传的（不随诱导子图单调），不被⌈n/2⌉界住，且Pachter-Sturmfels的广义4点猜想不成立。同时证明该秩在1-sum及某些2-顶点-sum下具有次可加性，并有平凡上界n−1；给出秩为1图的完整分类，构造若干秩为⌈n/2⌉的无限图族。

Conclusion: 系统发育秩的结构比先前猜想更复杂，非遗传且可超过⌈n/2⌉，广义4点猜想被证伪；但它在若干图运算下表现良好（次可加性、上界n−1），并存在丰富的极值族达到⌈n/2⌉，为后续研究提供了数据库与算法工具。

Abstract: The Pachter-Sturmfels phylogenetic rank of a graph G is the minimal number of metric trees needed to embed G isometrically. Here, all edges of G are of length one and the product of metric trees is endowed with the supremum norm. We develop both a greedy and an exact algorithm for computing phylogenetic ranks. Using our algorithms, we construct a database of phylogenetic ranks which includes all graphs on 6 and 7 vertices. In particular, we exhibit examples disproving that the phylogenetic rank is hereditary, bounded by $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$, and a generalised 4-point conjecture by Pachter and Sturmfels. In addition, we show that the phylogenetic rank is subadditive under 1-sums and certain 2-vertex-sums, and that it is trivially upper bounded by n-1, where n denotes the number of vertices. We also provide a complete classification of graphs with phylogenetic rank 1 and construct several infinite families with phylogenetic rank $\lceil \frac{n}{2} \rceil$.

</details>

TL;DR: 证明了不含某些特殊4-圈的最大度6平面图是7-全可着色的，推进了全着色猜想在平面图Δ=6这一唯一开放情形下的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用全着色的结构性证明方法，在最大度6的平面图中通过排除或限制某些特殊4-圈（而非完全禁用所有4-圈）来开展可约性分析，利用放电法等技巧构造并验证7-全着色的存在性。

Result: 证明任何最大度为6且不包含某些特殊4-圈的平面图G都是7-全可着色的。该结论推广了"不含4-圈"这一已知结果，放宽了结构性前提条件。

Conclusion: 在平面图Δ(G)=6这一TCC唯一未决情形下取得了进展：将"不含4-圈"的可着色性条件弱化为"不含某些特殊4-圈"，从而扩大了已证实7-全可着色的平面图范围，朝向完全解决该情形下的猜想迈进一步。

Abstract: The Total Coloring Conjecture (TCC) is a challenging unsolved problem posed by Behzad and Vizing independently, which states that every simple graph $G$ admits a ($Δ(G)$ +2)-total-coloring, where $Δ(G)$ denotes the maximum degree of $G$. This conjecture has been confirmed for graphs with $Δ(G)\leq 5$. However, for planar graphs, the only open case is $Δ(G)=6$. It was known that planar graphs with maximum degree 6 and without 4-cycles are 7-totally-colorable. In this paper, we improve this result by showing that any planar graph $G$ of maximum degree 6, which does not contain some special 4-cycles, is 7-totally-colorable.

</details>


### [66] [The smallest square tileable by pairwise incomparable integer rectangles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19536)
*George M. Georgiou*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了用两两不可比较的整数矩形铺砌正方形时，最小的可能边长为27：对任意 n≤26 和 k≥2，n×n 正方形都不存在这样的 k 块铺砌。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Croft、Falconer 和 Guy 在《Unsolved Problems in Geometry》问题 C5 中给出 27×27 正方形由八块两两不可比较的整数矩形铺砌的例子，并指出 27 是否为最小边长未知。本文旨在解决这一开放问题。

Method: 证明结合两个结构性归约，将问题化简为少量候选铺砌方案；随后对幸存的 167 538 个候选瓦片集合进行穷举搜索，搜索由两个独立编写的程序完成，并附带完整软件、构建说明和输出日志。

Result: 对每个整数 n≤26 和每个 k≥2，n×n 正方形均不存在由 k 块两两不可比较的整数矩形构成的铺砌；因此 27 是最小边长。

Conclusion: 所得结果确认了问题 C5 中提出的猜想，即 27 是用两两不可比较整数矩形铺砌正方形的最小边长，且该结论经由详尽计算验证。

Abstract: Croft, Falconer and Guy ({Unsolved Problems in Geometry}, Problem~C5) exhibit a tiling of the $27\times27$ square by eight pairwise incomparable integer rectangles and remark that it is not known whether $27$ is the smallest side length of a square that can be tiled by pairwise incomparable integer rectangles, no restriction being placed on the number of tiles. We show that it is: for every integer $n\le26$ and every $k\ge2$, the $n\times n$ square admits no tiling by $k$ pairwise incomparable integer rectangles. The proof combines two structural reductions with an exhaustive search over the $167\,538$ surviving candidate tile sets, carried out by two independently written programs. The complete software, build instructions and output logs are included as ancillary files.

</details>

Conclusion: 方向性限制与加权前缀树分析能有效改进递归 3-分支构造的剥离序列计数上界，为进一步精确估计 g(n) 提供了工具。

Abstract: A \emph{peeling sequence} of a finite planar point set is an ordering of point removals, in which
  each point is a vertex of the convex hull of the points not yet removed. Write $g(S)$ for the number of such sequences, and $g(n)$ for the minimum of $g(S)$ over $n$-point sets in general position. We present a method which can be used to prove better upper bounds on the previously analysed recursive 3-branch constructions $S_n$. In fact, we prove $g(n)\leq g(S_n)=O(6.57^n)$, using directional restrictions and a weighted prefix-tree argument.

</details>


### [68] [Ordered matchings versus triangles via pseudorandom triangle-free graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19632)
*Wen Chen,Qizhong Lin,Chunlin You*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究有序图的多色拉姆齐数 $\rt(K_3,M)$，证明随机有序匹配 $M$ 渐近几乎必然满足 $\rt(K_3,M)=\Omega(n^{4/3}/(\log n)^{1/3})$，改进了此前最优下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序图拉姆齐数是拉姆齐理论的推广，$\rt(K_3,M)$ 的下界刻画有助于理解有序结构中三角形与匹配的强制关系。已有工作给出一般存在性下界 $\Omega(n/\log n)$ 及针对随机匹配的 $\Omega((n/\log n)^{5/4})$，本文旨在进一步逼近真值、提升随机匹配情形下的下界。

Method: 构造性组合证明。结合三条工具：伪随机的无三角形图作为着色底层；对保序嵌入进行粗粒度编码；以及由 Brègman 不等式导出的排列避免估计。三者共同用于在随机构造下控制单色有序三角形的出现。

Result: 对区间色数为 2 的均匀随机有序匹配 $M$（$n$ 个顶点），渐近几乎必然有 $\rt(K_3,M)=\Omega\!\left(n^{4/3}/(\log n)^{1/3}\right)$。该结果强化了 Balko–Poljak 对随机匹配的 $\Omega((n/\log n)^{5/4})$，并将 Conlon–Fox–Lee–Sudakov 的一般存在性下界提升了 $\log n$ 因子。

Conclusion: 在随机有序匹配情形下显著推进了 $\rt(K_3,M)$ 的下界，展示伪随机图、保序嵌入编码与排列避免估计相结合的方法在高维/有序拉姆齐问题中的有效性，为后续确定该量真实渐近阶提供了新工具。

Abstract: For ordered graphs $H_1,\ldots,H_t$, let $\rt(H_1,\ldots,H_t)$ denote the least integer $N$ such that every $t$-coloring of the edges of the naturally ordered complete graph on $[N]$ contains an ordered copy of $H_i$ in color $i$ for some $i\in[t]$. We prove that a uniformly random ordered matching $M$ on $n$ vertices with interval chromatic number two asymptotically almost surely satisfies \[
  \rt(K_3,M)
  =Ω\left(\frac{n^{4/3}}{(\log n)^{1/3}}\right). \] This strengthens the lower bound $Ω((n/\log n)^{5/4})$ of Balko and Poljak for such random matchings and improves the general existential lower bound of Conlon, Fox, Lee and Sudakov by a factor of $\log n$. The proof combines pseudorandom triangle-free graphs, a coarse encoding of order-preserving embeddings, and a permutation avoidance estimate derived from Brègman's inequality.

</details>


### [69] [Unimodality for IDP Lattice Simplices of Prime Normalized Volume](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19637)
*Feihu Liu,Zihao Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明：具有整数分解性质且标准化体积为素数的格单纯形，其 $h^*$-多项式是单峰的，并给出若干确保单峰性的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 证明了每个具有整数分解性质且标准化体积为素数的格单纯形都拥有单峰的 $h^*$-多项式；此外还得到了若干确保此类单纯形 $h^*$-多项式单峰的充分条件。

Conclusion: 在 Ferroni 反例之后，本文在格单纯形这一受限框架内恢复了单峰性结果，表明整数分解性质与素数标准化体积相结合可保证 $h^*$-多项式的单峰性。

Abstract: Recently, Ferroni constructed a family of counterexamples to the well-known conjecture in Ehrhart theory stating that the $h^*$-polynomial of a lattice polytope with the integer decomposition property is unimodal. This raises the question of whether the $h^*$-polynomial of a lattice simplex with the integer decomposition property remains unimodal. In this note, we prove that every lattice simplex with the integer decomposition property and prime normalized volume has a unimodal $h^*$-polynomial. Furthermore, we establish several sufficient conditions for the unimodality of the $h^*$-polynomial of such simplices.

</details>


### [70] [Maximum and Minimum Spectral Radii in an Exceptional Family for Edge-Disjoint Spanning Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19655)
*Xuanyu Cao,Chunxiang Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文在 Chang–Li–Zhang 提出的例外图族 $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,\delta)$ 中，精确确定了最大与最小邻接谱半径，并据此给出 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的尖锐邻接谱阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对连通图 $G$，$\tau(G)$ 为两两边不相交生成树的最大数目，$\rho(G)$ 为邻接谱半径。已有工作引入例外图族 $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,\delta)$，并指出 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的尖锐邻接谱阈值由该族中 $\rho(G)$ 的最大值决定。然而该族内部谱半径的极值图结构尚未明确，且原猜想中的匹配构型是否为极值尚未解决，因此需要刻画该族内谱半径的最大、最小元及等号情形。

Method: 固定 $n\ge1$、$k\ge2$、$k\le\delta\le2k-1$，令 $h=\delta-k$。采用精确的核心—放置（core–placement）参数化，结合统一的 Schur 补预解展开。关键系数分析表明：首个依赖于候选图的系数关于 $\sum_{x\in V(M)}d_M(x)^2$ 是仿射的，其中 $M$ 为有界核心的缺边图；首个对“放置”敏感的系数是平方负载泛函。通过等配商矩阵（equitable quotient matrix）得到两个极值谱半径。

Result: 对每个容许对 $(k,\delta)$ 及充分大的 $n$，确定了 $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,\delta)$ 中邻接谱半径的最大值与最小值。当 $h\ge2$ 时，唯一极大图（同构意义下）满足 $M\cong K_{1,h}\cup(h+1)K_1$，且例外边嵌套在大团一侧；由此表明 Chang–Li–Zhang 猜想 2 中提出的匹配构型并非极值。对每个 $h\ge1$，唯一极小图满足 $M\cong hK_2\cup2K_1$，其 $2h$ 条例外边在大团一侧端点互异。此外还解决了 $h=0,1$ 的情形以及全部等号情形。极大图同时给出尖锐的全局邻接谱阈值。

Conclusion: 该族内邻接谱半径的极大元由 $M\cong K_{1,h}\cup(h+1)K_1$ 给出，而非原猜想中的匹配构型；极小元由 $M\cong hK_2\cup2K_1$ 给出。所得极大图确定了 $\tau(G)\ge k$ 的尖锐邻接谱阈值，并为该例外族提供了完整的极值刻画与等号分类。

Abstract: For a connected graph $G$, let $τ(G)$ denote the maximum number of pairwise edge-disjoint spanning trees, and let $ρ(G)$ be its adjacency spectral radius. For integers $n\ge1$, $k\ge2$, and $k\leδ\le2k-1$, let $\mathcal{G}_{n,δ}$ be the class of connected $n$-vertex graphs with minimum degree $δ$, and let $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,δ)\subseteq\mathcal{G}_{n,δ}$ be the exceptional family introduced by Chang, Li, and Zhang. For sufficiently large $n$, the sharp adjacency-spectral threshold for $τ(G)\ge k$ is determined by maximizing $ρ(G)$ over this family.
  Set $h=δ-k$. For each fixed admissible pair $(k,δ)$ and all sufficiently large $n$, we determine the maximum and minimum adjacency spectral radii in $\mathcal{L}_{\mathcal{H}}^{2}(n,k,δ)$. In the core--placement representation, let $M$ be the missing-edge graph of the bounded core. For $h\ge2$, the unique maximizer, up to isomorphism, satisfies $M\cong K_{1,h}\cup(h+1)K_1$, with the exceptional edges nested on the large-clique side. Hence the matching configuration proposed in Conjecture~2 of Chang--Li--Zhang is not extremal. For every $h\ge1$, the unique minimizer satisfies $M\cong hK_2\cup2K_1$, with the $2h$ exceptional edges having distinct endpoints on the large-clique side. We also settle the cases $h=0,1$ and all equality cases.
  The proof combines an exact core--placement parametrization with a uniform Schur-complement resolvent expansion. The first candidate-dependent coefficient is affine in $\sum_{x\in V(M)}d_M(x)^2$, while the first placement-sensitive coefficient is a squared-load functional. Equitable quotient matrices yield the two extremal radii, and the maximizing graph gives the sharp global adjacency-spectral threshold.

</details>

*Xiang Fan*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文完整分类了有限域 $\mathbb F_{q^e}$ 上的置换二项式 $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$，给出充要条件，证明了 Masuda–Rubio–Santiago 猜想，并确定了该族不同置换函数的精确数目，进一步分类了 $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$ 在 $\gcd(d,\ell_e(q))=1$ 时的情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置换二项式（尤其是 $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ 型）是有限域置换多项式研究中的核心族，但此前缺乏对所有 $q$ 与 $e$ 的完整判定；Masuda、Rubio 和 Santiago 提出猜想：该型置换二项式都可由 $(X^{q^h}+aX)\circ X^r$ 得到，但该猜想未被证明。本文旨在给出完整分类并验证该猜想。

Method: 设 $\ell_j(q)=(q^j-1)/(q-1)$。用 Hermite 幂和判别法，将选定的幂和系数通过 Lucas 定理转化为数位条件；再用 Farey 引导的局部论证逐步约束 base-$q$ 数位，并由循环旋转得到逆同余。特征 2 情形下，通过向辅助环作 mod 4 提升来保留模 2 丢失的端点信息。

Result: 对任意素数幂 $q$ 与 $e\ge2$，$X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ 为置换当且仅当 $\gcd(r,q-1)=1$、$(-a)^{\ell_e(q)}\ne1$，且存在 $1\le h<e$、$\gcd(h,e)=1$ 使 $r\ell_h(q)\equiv1\pmod{\ell_e(q)}$。由此证明了上述猜想，并给出该族所表示的不同置换函数的精确个数；进一步完整分类了 $\gcd(d,\ell_e(q))=1$ 时的 $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了 $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ 型置换二项式的完整判定，验证了 Masuda–Rubio–Santiago 猜想，并推广到 $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$ 的互素指数情形；所用 Hermite 幂和结合 Lucas 定理与 Farey 局部分析的数位方法，以及特征 2 的 mod 4 提升技巧，为相关分类问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: We classify, for every prime power $q$ and every $e\geqslant2$, the permutation binomials $X^r(X^{q-1}+a)$ over $\mathbb F_{q^e}$. Writing $\ell_j(q)=(q^j-1)/(q-1)$, such a binomial is a permutation if and only if $\gcd(r,q-1)=1$, $(-a)^{\ell_e(q)}\ne1$, and $r\ell_h(q)\equiv1\pmod{\ell_e(q)}$ for some $1\leqslant h<e$ coprime to $e$. This proves a conjecture of Masuda, Rubio, and Santiago: every permutation binomial of this form arises from $(X^{q^h}+aX)\circ X^r$ for a suitable $h$. We also determine the exact number of distinct permutation functions represented by this family. As a further consequence, we completely classify the broader family $X^r(X^{d(q-1)}+a)$ in the coprime-index case $\gcd(d,\ell_e(q))=1$. The new ingredient in the main classification is the necessity argument: selected Hermite power sums are organized so that Lucas' theorem turns their coefficients into digit conditions; a Farey-guided local argument then forces successive base-$q$ digits, and cyclic rotations yield the inverse congruence. In characteristic $2$, a mod-$4$ lift to an auxiliary ring retains endpoint information lost modulo $2$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性符号矩阵的秩计算（交换Edmonds问题）与非交换Edmonds问题是代数与组合优化中的重要基础问题，已有工作多从代数角度刻画nc-rank。本文旨在通过多面体与线性优化的视角统一理解并刻画这两类问题。

Method: 将矩阵A及blow-up的秩计算视为在整多面体P(A)与Q(A)上的线性优化，其中P(A)为子行列式指数向量的凸包，Q(A)由blow-up序列的凸包定义；通过推广已知的nc-rank结果，构造递增的整多面体层级。

Result: 建立了整多面体层级 P(A) ⊆ P^{≤2}(A) ⊆ P^{≤3}(A) ⊆ ⋯ = Q(A)，并证明Q(A)相对P(A)的整性间隙至少为1/2；进一步地，当每个A_k为秩-2反对称矩阵时，层级在第二层终止，整性间隙改善为2/3。

Conclusion: 交换与非交换Edmonds问题可被纳入统一的多面体优化框架，其差异体现为整多面体层级与整性间隙；特定结构（秩-2反对称）可使层级有限终止并收紧间隙界。

Abstract: In this paper, we study polyhedral aspects on commutative and noncommutative Edmonds' problems for computing the rank of linear symbolic matrix $A = \sum_{k=1}^m A_k x_k$. We regard them as linear optimization over integral polytopes ${\cal P}(A)$ and ${\cal Q}(A)$, respectively, which are obtained by the convex hulls of exponent vectors of subdeterminants of~$A$ and its blow-ups $A^{\{d\}} = \sum_{k=1}^m A_k \otimes X_k$ $(d=1,2,\ldots)$. By extending previously known results on nc-rank, we establish a hierarchy of integral polytopes ${\cal P}(A) \subseteq {\cal P}^{\leq 2}(A) \subseteq {\cal P}^{\leq 3}(A) \subseteq \cdots = {\cal Q}(A)$ and show that the integrality gap of ${\cal Q}(A)$ relative to ${\cal P}(A)$ is at least $1/2$. Further, we show that if each $A_k$ is rank-2 skew-symmetric, then the above hierarchy terminates at the second level and the integrality gap is improved to $2/3$.

</details>

*Kam Cheong Au,Kazuhiro Onodera*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文将超平面配置的组合学引入紧Lie群表示zeta函数的研究，为根系统Witten zeta函数给出ζ_Φ(0)与ζ_Φ'(0)的公式，并推广至锥形zeta函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统解析技术难以直接获得Witten zeta函数等表示zeta函数在特殊点（如0处及其导数）的显式公式，需要新的组合视角。

Method: 把超平面配置的组合学方法引入紧Lie群表示zeta函数研究；对根系统Φ的Witten zeta函数，利用各抛物子系统的指数表达ζ_Φ(0)与ζ_Φ'(0)；更一般地，通过相关超平面配置相交偏序集的Möbius函数处理一类锥形zeta函数。

Conclusion: 超平面配置组合学为表示zeta函数的特殊值计算提供了有效且更普适的框架，这些公式不易由文献中的常规解析技术得到。

Abstract: We introduce a new method that brings the combinatorics of hyperplane arrangements into the study of representation zeta functions of compact Lie groups. For the Witten zeta function $ζ_Φ(s)$ associated with a root system $Φ$, our method yields elegant formulas for $ζ_Φ(0)$ and $ζ_Φ'(0)$ in terms of the exponents of various parabolic subsystems of $Φ$. Such formulas do not appear to be readily accessible through the conventional analytic techniques in the literature. More generally, the method applies to a broad family of conical zeta functions, expressing these two special values through the Möbius function of the intersection poset of the associated hyperplane arrangement.

</details>

*Yinfeng Zhu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了在相同阶数的连通二部图中，路径最大化均匀图同态到整数的期望极差（数值固定为0），从而证实了Benjamini–Häggström–Mossel (BHM) 猜想的期望形式，并由此推出Loebl–Nešetřil–Reed (LNR) 猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BHM 猜想与 LNR 猜想是关于图同态/整值 1-Lipschitz 函数极差期望的极值问题，长期未决。本文旨在建立 BHM 的期望形式，并揭示其与 LNR 猜想的蕴含关系。

Method: 对每个二部类上的同态进行限制与重标度（restrict and rescale），然后收缩高度函数取常数的边；通过对这些"零边"的秩给出定量估计，补偿简单随机游走期望极差中的奇偶项，从而实现对顶点数的归纳。随后证明 BHM 不等式蕴含 LNR 不等式。

Result: 路径在所有同阶连通二部图中最大化均匀选取的整数图同态的期望极差，即证实 BHM 期望形式；并证明 BHM 不等式可推出均匀选取整值 1-Lipschitz 函数在任意连通图上的 LNR 不等式，将其作为推论得到 LNR 猜想。

Conclusion: BHM 期望形式成立，且 LNR 猜想可由 BHM 推出。证明通过与大模型交互获得并由作者验证，主要结果已在 Lean 4 中形式化并检验。

Abstract: We prove that a path maximizes the expected range of a uniformly chosen graph homomorphism into the integers, with one vertex pinned at zero, among all connected bipartite graphs of the same order. This establishes the expectation form of the Benjamini--Häggström--Mossel conjecture. The proof restricts and rescales a homomorphism on each bipartition class, then contracts the edges on which the resulting height function is constant. A quantitative estimate for the rank of these zero edges compensates for a parity term in the expected range of a simple random walk, allowing an induction on the number of vertices. We then prove that the BHM inequality implies the Loebl--Ne\v set\v ril--Reed inequality for uniformly chosen integer 1-Lipschitz functions on arbitrary connected graphs, and hence obtain the LNR conjecture as a corollary of BHM. The proof was obtained through interaction with OpenAI GPT-6 Astra and verified by the author. The main results have also been formalized and checked in Lean~4.

</details>

Result: 主要定理：每个连通2-距离传递循环有向图必同构于以下之一：无向循环 C_n；完全二部图 K_{n/2,n/2}；完全多部图 K_{m[b]}（m≥3,b≥2）；当 n/2 为奇数时的 K_{n/2,n/2} − (n/2)K_2；素数阶 Paley 图；有向循环 \overrightarrow{C}_n；满足条件 \ref{p-power-normal-2dt-cond} 的定向图 G(p^m,r)；r≥3,b≥2,rb=n 的定向图 C_r(b,1)；以及由 G(p^m,r)[\overline{K}_d] 给出的字典积定向图，其中 G(p^m,r) 满足该条件。

Conclusion: 本文完成了连通2-距离传递循环有向图的完全分类，涵盖无向、完全多部、Paley 图、有向循环、p 幂阶定向图及其字典积等所有情形，为循环有向图的对称性研究提供了完整的结构刻画。

Abstract: Circulant digraphs form a prominent class of Cayley digraphs defined on finite cyclic groups. Building on the existing classification of $2$-arc-transitive circulant graphs, this paper presents a complete classification of $2$-distance-transitive circulant digraphs. Our main theorem establishes that every connected $2$-distance-transitive circulant digraph is isomorphic to one of the following: the undirected cycle $C_n$, the complete bipartite graph $\K_{\frac{n}{2},\frac{n}{2}}$, the complete multipartite graph $\K_{m[b]}$ with $m\geq 3,b\geq 2$, the graph $\K_{\frac{n}{2},\frac{n}{2}}-\frac{n}{2}\K_2$ for odd $\frac{n}{2}$, prime-order Paley graphs, the directed cycle $\overrightarrow{C}_n$, the oriented graph \(G(p^m,r)\) satisfying Condition~\ref{p-power-normal-2dt-cond}, the oriented graph \( C_r(b,1)\) with $r\geq 3,b\geq 2$ and $rb=n$, the lexicographic product oriented graph \( G(p^m,r)[\overline{\K}_d]\) where \(G(p^m,r)\) obeys Condition~\ref{p-power-normal-2dt-cond}.

</details>


### [76] [Spreads of degrees in graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19762)
*Yair Caro,Riste Škrekovski,Christina Zarb*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文给出了任意图 $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ 的下界，并精确确定了极大外平面图的 $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Caro、Lauri 和 Zarb 已确定 $\mathrm{MOP}(n,k)$（$k\neq 2$ 时近似到常数），但 $k=2$ 的情形尚未解决，仅知道 $4n/9\le \mathrm{MOP}(n,2)\le (5n+19)/11$。本文旨在填补这一空白并推广一般图的下界。

Method: 首先用图的阶 $n$、边数 $m$ 和最小度 $\delta$ 建立任意图 $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ 的下界；刻画该下界取等时的图并给出显式取等图；证明当 $n\ge n_0(\delta,k,d)$ 时该界精确。随后将此界调整应用到极大外平面图，推导 $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)$ 的新下界。

Result: 新下界涵盖 Caro–Lauri–Zarb 的界，且在 $k=0$ 时包含 Caro–West 的 $\mathrm{rep}(G)\ge \lceil n/(2d-2\delta+1)\rceil$。对极大外平面图证明 $n\ge 14$ 时 $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)\ge \lceil(4n+10)/9\rceil$，当 $n\equiv 2\pmod{18}$ 时取等，并且对所有 $n$ 有 $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)=4n/9+O(1)$。

Conclusion: 论文解决了 $k=2$ 的遗留问题，把 $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)$ 从原有区间 $\left[4n/9,\,(5n+19)/11\right]$ 缩小并确定其渐近常数，同时提供了更一般、在足够大阶数下精确的图参数下界。

Abstract: For a graph $G$ and a set $B\subseteq V(G)$, the spread $\mathrm{sp}(B)$ of $B$ is the difference between the largest and the smallest degree in $G$ of a vertex of $B$, and for an integer $k\geq0$ the parameter $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ is the largest cardinality of a set $B$ with $\mathrm{sp}(B)\leq k$. Caro, Lauri and Zarb derived a lower bound for $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ and, among several families of graphs, considered \[ \mathrm{MOP}(n,k)=\min \{\mathrm{sp}(G,k):G\text{ is a maximal outerplanar graph of order }n\} \] and determined $\mathrm{MOP}(n,k)$ up to an additive constant for every $k\not =2,$ leaving the case $k=2$ open, with the bounds $4n/9\leq \mathrm{MOP}(n,2)\leq (5n+19)/11$. We first prove a lower bound on $\mathrm{sp}(G,k)$ for an arbitrary graph $G$ in terms of its order $n$, its number of edges $m$ and its minimum degree $δ$. This lower bound contains the bounds of Caro, Lauri and Zarb and, for $k=0$, the bound $\mathrm{rep}(G)\geq \left\lceil n/(2d-2δ+1)\right\rceil $ of Caro and West, where $d=2m/n$. We determine when this lower bound is attained, exhibit explicit graphs attaining it, and show that it is exact for all graphs once $n\geq n_{0}(δ,k,d)$. We then apply the bound to maximal outerplanar graphs: adjusting the count to this class we prove \[ \mathrm{MOP}(n,2)\geq \left\lceil \frac{4n+10}{9}\right\rceil \qquad \text{for every }n\geq 14, \] with equality for $n\equiv 2\ (\mathrm{mod}\ 18)$, and $\mathrm{MOP}(n,2)=4n/9+O(1)$ for every $n$.

</details>


### [77] [The resonance graphs of nanotubes and toroidal polyhexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19766)
*Lingmei Liang,Heping Zhang*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作对冠状系统与基本纳米管给出了两完美匹配位于共振图同一连通分量的割流判据，但该判据的充分性对一般纳米管与环面多联六边形不成立，需寻求更一般且有效的判定条件。

Method: 在原有割流条件基础上强化要求：对纳米管（环面多联六边形）要求两完美匹配沿x轴（经线与纬线）跨越各割的流相同且具有相同的梯（ladders）。环面情形引入环面上简单闭曲线的同伦类方法，并证明当两完美匹配具有交替六边形时，可用简单流简化判据。

Result: 建立了适用于一般纳米管与环面多联六边形的共振图连通性判据：两完美匹配处于同一连通分量当且仅当它们沿相应方向跨越割的流相同且梯相同；在环面多联六边形含交替六边形情形下判据可简化为仅需比较简单流。

Conclusion: 强化后的割流与梯条件弥补了原判据对一般纳米管与环面多联六边形充分性失效的问题，结合环面同伦类方法给出了统一且可简化的共振图连通性刻画。

Abstract: Coronoid systems, nanotubes and toroidal polyhexes (or fullerenes) can all be regarded as carbon networks composed of carbon atoms linked in hexagonal shapes. The resonance graphs of coronoid systems and nanotubes are not necessarily connected. For coronoid systems and elementary nanotubes, by using flow across cuts the present authors gave criteria for two perfect matchings lying in the same connected component of the resonance graph (Discrete Appl. Math. 395 (2026) 443-455). However, the sufficiency of such criterion does not hold for general nanotubes and toroidal polyhexes. In this paper we strengthen this requirement to obtain valid criteria for two perfect matchings of a nanotube (resp. toroidal polyhex) to lie in the same connected component of its resonance graph: they have the same flows across cuts along the $x$-axis (resp. longitude and latitude) and the same ladders. For toroidal polyhexes, our method uses homotopic classes of simple loops on the torus, and the above criterion can be simplified by using only simple flows, for the case in which two perfect matchings have alternating hexagons.

</details>


### [78] [Nearly Spanning Regular Subgraphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19777)
*Varun Sivashankar*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文正面回答了Alon与Mubayi关于正则图中存在几乎覆盖全部顶点的k-正则子图的问题，对一般k、r给出ε=O_k(r^{-1/2})，并在k=2、奇数r情形给出最优的ε=1/(r²-3)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alon和Mubayi提出猜想：对任意k≥1和ε>0，存在r₀，使得顶点数n、度数r≥r₀的r-正则图含有覆盖至少(1-ε)n个顶点的k-正则子图。该猜想此前仅在k∈{1,2}以及k、r均为偶数时获证，一般情形尚属空白，本文旨在解决这一问题。

Result: 对一般k和r，ε可取O_k(r^{-1/2})；特别地，当k=2且r≥3为奇数时，ε=1/(r²-3)即可，且该界是最优的（best possible）。

Conclusion: 本文肯定地回答了Alon–Mubayi猜想的一般情形，将已知结果从k∈{1,2}及k、r同为偶数的特殊情况推广到一般k、r，并在k=2、奇数r时给出最优逼近比例。

Abstract: Alon and Mubayi asked whether, for every integer $k\ge1$ and every $\varepsilon>0$, there exists $r_0=r_0(k,\varepsilon)$ such that every $r$-regular graph on $n$ vertices with $r\ge r_0$ contains a $k$-regular subgraph covering at least $(1-\varepsilon)n$ vertices. Previously, the conjecture was known for $k\in\{1,2\}$ and for $k$ and $r$ both even. We answer this question affirmatively for general $k$ and $r$ with $\varepsilon=O_k(r^{-1/2})$. For $k=2$ and every odd $r\ge3$, we show that $\varepsilon=1/(r^2-3)$ suffices which is best possible.

</details>


### [79] [The small Davenport constant of $H_{27}\times C_3^r$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19810)
*Andreas Volkmann*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了群 $H_{27}\times C_3^r$ 的Davenport常数 $\mathsf{d}(H_{27}\times C_3^r)=2r+6$，其中 $H_{27}=\mathrm{UT}_3(\mathbb{F}_3)$ 是阶为27、指数为3的非交换群。证明结合了基本交换群群代数中的仿射系数恒等式与 $\mathbb{F}_3^2$ 非正交图分解为八个边不相交的零和三角形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定有限群（尤其是非交换群）的Davenport常数是零和理论中的核心问题。对于 $H_{27}\times C_3^r$ 这类群，已有方法往往依赖于较小非交换群的小Davenport常数值或计算枚举，缺乏统一且不依赖枚举的证明，本文旨在给出一个对所有 $r$ 一致成立的证明。

Method: 证明方法结合了两项关键技术：（1）基本交换群群代数中的仿射系数恒等式；（2）将 $\mathbb{F}_3^2$ 的非正交图分解为八个边不相交的零和三角形。该论证对 $r$ 一致，不使用较小非交换群的小Davenport常数值，也不需要计算枚举。

Result: 证明了 $\mathsf{d}(H_{27}\times C_3^r)=2r+6$ 对每个整数 $r\geq0$ 成立。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了 $H_{27}\times C_3^r$ 的Davenport常数的精确值，并提供了一种统一、无需计算枚举的证明途径，避免了依赖较小非交换群的小Davenport常数值。

Abstract: Let $H_{27}=\mathrm{UT}_3(\mathbb{F}_3)$ be the nonabelian group of order $27$ and exponent $3$. We prove that $\mathsf{d}(H_{27}\times C_3^r)=2r+6$ for every integer $r\geq0$. The proof combines an affine coefficient identity in the group algebra of an elementary abelian group with a decomposition of the nonorthogonality graph of $\mathbb{F}_3^2$ into eight edge-disjoint zero-sum triangles. It is uniform in $r$, does not use the value of the small Davenport constant for a smaller nonabelian group, and requires no computational enumeration.

</details>


### [80] [Fractal Hyper-Trees: Combinatorial Enumeration, Symmetry Properties, and Ultrametric Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19895)
*Jean-Jacques Salone*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文提出并形式化了一类新型超图——分形超树（Fractal Hyper-Trees, FHT），具有深层递归包含层次与受控关系纹理。作者建立其公理化基础，研究组合枚举并与OEIS序列A398931建立显式联系，分析自同构群刻画的对称性、全局层次超度量拓扑及渐近视角（顶点替换算子、与p-adic整数环的启发式类比）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图论框架难以刻画同时具备深层递归包含结构和受控关系纹理的对象，需要超越传统图/超图模型，建立新的结构化数学对象及其理论体系。

Method: 1) 提出FHT概念并给出公理化定义；2) 开展组合枚举研究，与OEIS序列A398931建立显式对应；3) 通过自同构群分析结构对称性；4) 考察其诱导的全局层次超度量拓扑；5) 从渐近角度引入顶点替换算子，并与p-adic整数环进行启发式类比。

Result: 建立了FHT的公理体系；发现其枚举与OEIS A398931的显式联系；刻画了自同构群与超度量拓扑结构；给出顶点替换算子和与p-adic整数环的启发式平行。

Conclusion: FHT为具有递归包含层次和受控关系纹理的离散结构提供了统一的形式化框架，连接组合枚举、对称性、超度量拓扑与渐近分析，拓展了经典图论/超图理论的范围。

Abstract: This paper introduces and formalizes Fractal Hyper-Trees (FHT), a novel class of hypergraphs characterized by deep recursive inclusion hierarchies and controlled relational textures. Moving beyond classical graph-theoretic frameworks, we establish an axiomatic foundation for FHTs and investigate their combinatorial enumeration, revealing explicit connections to OEIS sequence A398931. Furthermore, we analyze the structural symmetry properties via automorphism groups, examine the resulting global hierarchical ultrametric topologies, and discuss asymptotic perspectives, including vertex substitution operators and heuristic parallels with $p$-adic integer rings.

</details>


### [81] [The face lattice of any simplicial polytope is Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19938)
*Robert Lauff*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了任意单纯多胞形（simplicial polytope）的面格是哈密顿图，从而验证了"任意多胞形面格为哈密顿图"这一猜想的主要部分（SODA26）。方法上利用双向线壳构造两种不相交立方体分解，进而应用Gregor定理得到哈密顿圈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期有猜想（SODA26）提出任意多胞形的面格都是哈密顿图，但该猜想整体尚未解决。本文旨在解决其中最重要的情形——单纯多胞形，从而为该猜想提供关键进展。

Method: 对单纯多胞形进行双向线壳（line shelling），将其面格分解为两类互不相交的立方体分解；然后应用Gregor关于立方体分解的定理构造出哈密顿圈。

Result: 证明了任意单纯多胞形的面格是哈密顿图，即验证了该猜想在单纯多胞形情形下的主要部分。

Conclusion: 单纯多胞形面格的哈密顿性得证，为"任意多胞形面格哈密顿"这一更一般猜想提供了强有力的支持，并展示了双向线壳与立方体分解结合这一组合方法在面格问题上的有效性。

Abstract: We show that the face lattice of any simplicial polytope is Hamiltonian. This proves a major part of a recent conjecture by \citeauthor{Listingfaces} which states that the face lattice of any polytope is Hamiltonian (SODA26). We use a line shelling in both directions to obtain two different decompositions of the face lattice into disjoint cubes, which allows the application of a theorem by Gregor to obtain the Hamilton cycle.

</details>


### [82] [Improved Bounds on the Szeged-Wiener Gap and the BKLPS Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20025)
*Lily Zhang,Evan Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bonamy-Knor-Lužar-Pinlou-Škrekovski (2017)提出的关于Szeged-Wiener gap的猜想：若$G$是$n \ge 10$阶有限简单2-连通图且不同构于$K_n$、$K_n^2$或$K_n^{n-2}$，则$\eta(G) \ge 2n$，并构造了达到等号的图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bonamy等人于2017年定义了图$K_n^t$并提出一个更强的猜想：满足特定条件的$n \ge 10$阶2-连通图其Szeged-Wiener gap满足$\eta(G) \ge 2n$。作者希望改进其界并完整证明该猜想，从而推进对Szeged-Wiener gap下界的理解。

Method: 作者改进了前人对Szeged-Wiener gap的界估计，收紧了下界，从而证明该猜想成立；随后构造出每个$n \ge 10$时达到等号的图，并提出刻画等号成立充要条件的公开问题。

Result: 证明了猜想：对$n \ge 10$阶有限简单2-连通且非$K_n$、$K_n^2$、$K_n^{n-2}$的图$G$，有$\eta(G) \ge 2n$；并对每个$n \ge 10$构造出达到等号的图。

Conclusion: 该猜想得证，Szeged-Wiener gap的下界被确认为$2n$，且等号可达；作者留下问题：确定等号成立的充要条件。

Abstract: Bonamy-Knor-Lužar-Pinlou-Škrekovski (2017) define $K_n^t$ to be the complete graph of $n-1$ vertices but with an extra vertex that's adjacent to $t$ vertices of the complete graph part. They propose a stronger conjecture which asserts that if $G$ is a finite simple $2$-connected graph of order $n \ge 10$ not isomorphic to $K_n$, $K_n^2$, nor $K_n^{n-2}$, then the Szeged-Wiener gap of $G$ is $η(G) \ge 2n$. We improve upon their work to tighten the bounds on the Szeged-Wiener gap, allowing us to prove this conjecture in the affirmative. Afterwards, we construct graphs attaining equality for each $n \ge 10$ and pose a problem for interested readers to determine a necessary and sufficient condition for equality.

</details>


### [83] [Switching graphs and designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20030)
*Aida Abiad,Dean Crnković,Louka Peters,Andrea Švob*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文将已知的构造2-设计的切换方法推广到可除设计，给出应用示例，并证明了图与设计的切换方法之间的若干等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中已有工作试图关联和统一码与设计、Hadamard矩阵与图的切换理论，本文旨在将图的切换方法拓展至设计，并建立图与设计切换方法之间的联系。

Method: 以图与设计作为组合结构，将已知的2-设计切换方法推广到可除设计，并证明图与设计的切换方法之间的若干等价关系。

Result: 成功将构造2-设计的切换方法推广到可除设计并给出应用示例；证明了图与设计切换方法之间的若干等价性，并由此得到一种新的构造2-设计的切换方法。

Conclusion: 切换方法在图与设计之间具有深刻联系，该推广和等价性为构造设计提供了新的工具。

Abstract: Switching methods can be seen as certain local transformations that do not alter their basic parameters of a combinatorial structure. Efforts have been devoted in the literature to relate and unify the switching theories for codes and designs, and also for Hadamard matrices and graphs. The combinatorial structures we consider in this paper are graphs and designs. We show an extension of known switching method for constructing 2-designs to divisible designs, and then provide some examples of its application. Moreover, we prove several equivalences between switching methods for graphs and designs, and as a byproduct, we obtain a new switching method to obtain 2-designs.

</details>


### [84] [Is the Signed Zarankiewicz Number the Same as the Recursive-line Zarankiewicz Number?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20071)
*Zhiwei Chen,Yannan Chen,Liqun Qi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了 \(z_{RL}(15,6)=60\)，从而消除了引入符号 Zarankiewicz 数 \(z_{SL}\) 的动机，并给出了 \(6\le m\le 16\) 时 \(z_{RL}(m,6)\) 的精确值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Löfberg 和 Qi 为双单纯双二次型引入第二阶、递归线与符号 Zarankiewicz 数 \(z_2, z_{RL}, z_{SL}\)，并证明 \(z_2\ge z_{SL}\ge z_{RL}\)。引入 \(z_{SL}\) 的动机在于例外情形 \(m=15,n=6\)：当时已知 \(z_2(15,6)=z_{SL}(15,6)=60\)，但 \(z_{RL}(15,6)\) 未知。本文旨在确定该值，从而检验 \(z_{SL}\) 的引入是否有必要。

Method: 通过组合与代数方法确定递归线 Zarankiewicz 数 \(z_{RL}(15,6)\) 的精确值，并系统计算 \(6\le m\le 16\) 范围内的 \(z_{RL}(m,6)\)。

Result: 证明 \(z_{RL}(15,6)=60\)；同时给出 \(6\le m\le 16\) 时 \(z_{RL}(m,6)\) 的全部精确值。

Conclusion: \(z_{RL}(15,6)=60\) 消除了引入 \(z_{SL}\) 的原始动机；但 \(z_{SL}(m,n)\) 是否恒等于 \(z_{RL}(m,n)\) 仍是公开问题。受 Lebedev 在 \(m=n=1893\) 处分离 \(z_A\) 与 \(z_L\) 的构造启发，作者期望 \(z_{SL}\) 与 \(z_{RL}\) 的分离问题同样能得到解决。

Abstract: Löfberg and Qi introduced the second order Zarankiewicz number \(z_2\), the recursive-line Zarankiewicz number \(z_{RL}\), and the signed Zarankiewicz number \(z_{SL}\) for doubly simple biquadratic forms. It was shown that \[ z_2(m,n)\ge z_{SL}(m,n)\ge z_{RL}(m,n) \] for all \(m\) and \(n\). However, there was no evidence that there exist particular \(m\) and \(n\) such that \(z_{SL}(m,n)>z_{RL}(m,n)\). The motivation for introducing \(z_{SL}\) was as follows: during the study of the exceptional case \(m=15\), \(n=6\), Löfberg and Qi showed that \[ z_2(15,6)=z_{SL}(15,6)=60, \] but the exact value of \(z_{RL}(15,6)\) was unknown then.
  In this paper we show that \[ z_{RL}(15,6)=60. \] This eliminates the motivation for introducing \(z_{SL}\). Whether \(z_{SL}(m,n)=z_{RL}(m,n)\) in general remains an open problem. Recently, Lebedev presented an explicit construction separating the augmented Zarankiewicz number \(z_A\) from the limited augmented Zarankiewicz number \(z_L\) at \(m=n=1893\). We hope that the separation problem for \(z_{SL}\) and \(z_{RL}\) can also be solved.
  We also present the exact values of \(z_{RL}(m,6)\) for \(6\le m\le 16\).

</details>


### [85] [So's Conjecture for Integral Circulant Graphs of Order p^aq](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20072)
*Jianwei Jiang,Chunhua Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明：对于阶为 \(p^a q\)（\(p<q\) 为素数，\(a\ge 1\)）的整循环图，其普通邻接谱唯一确定其除子集，从而在特定阶数类上验证了 So 的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: So 猜想：固定正整数 n，阶为 n 的整循环图的普通邻接谱决定其除子集。该猜想一般情形尚未解决，本文旨在对一类特定阶数 \(p^a q\) 给出证明。

Method: 引入谱计数测度以处理来自不同最大公约数类的重合特征值；对连通图，利用精确恒等式恢复除子集中包含 1 和 q（若存在）的部分，以及阶为 \(p^{a-1}q\) 图的计数测度；再通过强归纳法与连通分量分解逐步恢复完整除子集。

Result: 证明了当阶为 \(p^a q\)（\(p<q\) 为素数，\(a\ge 1\)）时，整循环图的邻接谱确定其除子集，包括 \(p=2\) 的情形以及非连通图。

Conclusion: 该结果在阶为两素数幂乘积形式的整循环图上验证了 So 的猜想，所采用的谱计数测度与归纳分解方法为处理重合特征值和一般阶数情形提供了可行框架。

Abstract: So conjectured that, for a fixed positive integer n, the ordinary adjacency spectrum of an integral circulant graph of order n determines its divisor set. We prove this for graphs of order p to the power a times q, where p and q are primes with p less than q and a is at least one. To handle coincident eigenvalues arising from distinct greatest-common-divisor classes, we use a spectral counting measure. For connected graphs, an exact identity recovers the part of the divisor set consisting of one and q, when present, together with the counting measure for a graph of order p to the power a minus one times q. Strong induction and decomposition into connected components then recover the full divisor set, including the case p equals two and disconnected graphs.

</details>


### [86] [The Outer Multiset Dimension of Toroidal Grids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20073)
*Bo Peng*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文确定了笛卡尔积图 $C_s \square C_t$（$s,t\geq 3$）的外多重集维数 $\operatorname{odim}(C_s \square C_t)$，其值介于 3 与 8 之间，回答了 Klavžar、Kuziak 和 Yero 提出的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定笛卡尔积图 $C_s \square C_t$ 的外多重集维数，解决 Klavžar、Kuziak 和 Yero 提出的公开问题。

Method: 方法结合了半转（half-turn）论证以在两因子长度均至少为 4 时建立普适的四标记点下界，针对无限族给出显式的三标记点与四标记点构造，并在剩余条带上进行精确有限枚举。无限四标记点构造背后的碰撞分类通过线性整数算术中的精确量词消去进行认证。

Result: 对所有 $s,t\geq 3$，确定了 $\operatorname{odim}(C_s \square C_t)$ 的精确值，取值范围为 3 到 8。

Conclusion: 完整刻画了环面 $C_s \square C_t$ 的外多重集维数，解决了该公开问题；论文附带源代码及所有有限上界证书。

Abstract: Let $S$ be a set of vertices in a connected graph $G$. A vertex outside $S$ is represented by the multiset of its distances to the vertices of $S$. The outer multiset dimension $\operatorname{odim}(G)$ is the minimum cardinality of an $S$ for which these representations distinguish all vertices outside $S$. We determine $\operatorname{odim}(C_s \square C_t)$ for all $s,t\geq 3$, answering a problem of Klavžar, Kuziak, and Yero. The values range from $3$ to $8$. The proof combines a half-turn argument giving a universal four-landmark lower bound when both factors have length at least four, explicit three- and four-landmark constructions for the infinite families, and exact finite enumeration on the remaining strip. The collision classification behind the infinite four-landmark construction is certified by exact quantifier elimination in linear integer arithmetic; source code and all finite upper certificates accompany the paper.

</details>


### [87] [Linear Tur'an Numbers of Four-Edge Uniform Paths via Incidence Rank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20173)
*Mahesh Ramani*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明若 $P_4^r$ 表示四边路径的 $r$-一致展开，则任意 $n$ 顶点、线性的、无 $P_4^r$ 的 $r$-一致超图至多有 $(r+1)n/r$ 条边，对每个 $r\ge 2$ 解决了 Adak–Verma 猜想，且等号仅在顶点不交的 Steiner 系 $S(2,r,r^2)$ 之并时成立。核心工具是尖锐的关联秩不等式：若 $N(H)$ 为线性 $r$-一致超图的边–顶点关联矩阵且其线图为余图，则 $(r+1)\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb{R}} N(H)\ge r|E(H)|$，等号当且仅当每个含边连通分量为 $S(2,r,r^2)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在解决 Adak 与 Verma 提出的关于线性 $r$-一致超图中无 $P_4^r$（四边路径的 $r$-一致展开）时的极值边数猜想，即确定此类超图的 Turán 型上界并刻画等号情形。其意义在于将通常针对图的线性 Turán 问题推广到超图，同时揭示极值结构与 Steiner 系之间的内在联系。

Method: 主要方法是建立一个尖锐的关联秩不等式：当线性 $r$-一致超图 $H$ 的线图为 cograph 时，边–顶点关联矩阵 $N(H)$ 满足 $(r+1)\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb{R}} N(H)\ge r|E(H)|$。证明沿 cograph 的 union-join 分解进行：在 join 结点处，各 co-component 的行差空间两两正交，可能的秩亏由 balanced co-component 决定；再用 Perron–Frobenius 理论识别最小 balanced 片段为 $r$ 个互不相交 $r$-元集的平行类，并通过正交性论证将其数量上界限制为 $r+1$。最后结合一般秩上界 $\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb{R}} N(H)\le |V(H)|$ 推出线性 Turán 界，并从等号情形重构 Steiner 系。
