Result: 对每个 $r\ge 2$，每个 $n$ 顶点的线性 $r$-一致无 $P_4^r$ 超图至多有 $(r+1)n/r$ 条边；等号恰在边集为若干顶点不交的 Steiner 系 $S(2,r,r^2)$ 之并时取得。作为关键引理，所建立的关联秩不等式 $(r+1)\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb{R}} N(H)\ge r|E(H)|$ 对线图为 cograph 的线性 $r$-一致超图成立，且等号当且仅当每个含边连通分量均为 $S(2,r,r^2)$。

Conclusion: 该工作完全解决了 Adak–Verma 猜想，给出了线性无 $P_4^r$ 超图的精确 Turán 边数上界，并精确刻画了唯一极值构型（顶点不交的 $S(2,r,r^2)$ 之并）。其方法创新在于通过 cograph 的 union-join 分解、行差空间正交性与 Perron–Frobenius 理论建立尖锐关联秩不等式，从而把超图极值问题转化为矩阵秩与组合结构的分析，为后续线性超图 Turán 问题提供了可推广的技术框架。

Abstract: Let $P_4^r$ denote the $r$-uniform expansion of the graph path with four edges. We prove that every $n$-vertex linear $r$-uniform $P_4^r$-free hypergraph has at most $(r+1)n/r$ edges, resolving a conjecture of Adak and Verma for every $r \geq 2$. Equality holds precisely for vertex-disjoint unions of Steiner systems $S(2,r,r^2)$. The main ingredient is a sharp incidence-rank inequality. If $N(H)$ is the edge-vertex incidence matrix of a linear $r$-uniform hypergraph whose line graph is a cograph, then $(r+1)\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb{R}} N(H) \geq r|E(H)|$. Equality holds exactly when every edge-containing component is an $S(2,r,r^2)$. The proof follows the union-join decomposition of cographs. At a join node, the row-difference spaces of the co-components are mutually orthogonal, and the possible rank defect is determined by balanced co-components. Perron-Frobenius theory identifies the smallest balanced pieces as parallel classes of $r$ disjoint $r$-sets, while an orthogonality argument bounds their number by $r+1$. The equality case then reconstructs the Steiner system. The linear Turán bound follows from $\operatorname{rank}_{\mathbb{R}} N(H) \leq |V(H)|$.

</details>


### [88] [Two-distance and list-two-distance coloring of cacti: the subcubic case and the C5 obstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20204)
*Vaibhav Suvagiya*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文精确确定了仙人掌图的2-距离色数与选择数，证明二者总相等，并给出所有度情形下的具体值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅覆盖Δ≥6（因仙人掌为外平面图且K_{2,3}-minor-free），Δ∈{4,5}及次立方情形尚未确定，且List Square Coloring Conjecture一般情形为假，需要寻找其成立的正例。

Method: 对仙人掌图G研究其平方图G^2的染色，结合块结构与唯一5-圈障碍分析，构造一个统一的消去序（elimination order）处理所有Δ≥4的情形，并进行次立方情形的完全分类。

Result: 对仙人掌G，χ(G^2)=ch(G^2)恒成立；共同值为Δ+1（Δ≥4）、4（Δ=3且无C_5块）、5（Δ=3且有C_5块），Δ≤2时为经典值。次立方情形在普通与列表设定下均获完全分类，5-圈为唯一障碍。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了小度区间的空白，统一解决Δ∈{4,5}，给出List Square Coloring Conjecture的一个真实正例，并揭示数字5在三种形式下实为同一障碍。

Abstract: The square $G^2$ of a graph joins two vertices at distance at most two; a proper coloring of $G^2$ is a 2-distance coloring of $G$. For a cactus $G$ (every edge on at most one cycle) we determine both the 2-distance chromatic number $χ(G^2)$ and the choice number $\mathrm{ch}(G^2)$ exactly: they are always equal, and the common value is $Δ+1$ if $Δ\ge4$, is 4 if $Δ=3$ and $G$ has no block equal to $C_5$, is 5 if $Δ=3$ and $G$ has a $C_5$ block, and is the classical value if $Δ\le2$. For $Δ\ge6$ the value $Δ+1$ is already known, since cacti are outerplanar and hence $K_{2,3}$-minor-free (Hetherington-Woodall; Agnarsson-Halldorsson). Our contribution is the small-degree regime. For subcubic cacti we obtain a complete classification in both the ordinary and list settings, with the 5-cycle as the unique obstruction; the list statement has no prior analogue and is a genuine positive instance of the List Square Coloring Conjecture, which is false in general. A single elimination order then handles all $Δ\ge4$ uniformly and, in particular, settles the two cases $Δ\in\{4,5\}$ that the superclass bounds leave at $Δ+2$. The number 5 turns out to be one obstruction wearing three disguises: $C_5^2=K_5$, the Frobenius number of $\{3,4\}$, and a degenerate $K_4$ list-coloring instance.

</details>


### [89] [Complex coordinates forced by finite orthogonality hypergraphs in dimension three](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20205)
*Mirko Navara,Karl Svozil*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文构造了两个有限正交超图，它们在复三维空间中存在忠实正交表示，但在实三维空间中不存在，表明三维可坐标化性依赖于标量域是复数还是实数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正交图（orthogonality diagram）的可坐标化性是否依赖于标量域（复数与实数）的选取，即同一正交图在不同标量域下可能具有不同的可实现性。

Method: 构造两个有限正交超图：第一个含20个顶点、12个context；第二个在其基础上增加1个顶点和2个context；并利用相位调整实化（phase-adjusted realification）给出显式表示。

Result: 两个超图均在复三维空间中拥有由射线构成的忠实正交表示，但在实三维空间中不存在此类表示；相位调整实化方法给出了二者在实六维空间中的显式忠实正交表示。

Conclusion: 正交图的三维可坐标化性本质上取决于标量域（复数或实数），即使正交图本身不变，其可表示性也会因标量域不同而改变。

Abstract: We exhibit two finite orthogonality hypergraphs that admit faithful orthogonal representations by rays in complex three-dimensional space but admit none in real three-dimensional space. Thus, although each orthogonality diagram is unchanged, its coordinatizability in dimension three depends on whether the scalar field is complex or real. The first hypergraph has 20 vertices and 12 contexts. The second hypergraph adds one vertex and two contexts. Phase-adjusted realification also gives explicit faithful orthogonal representations of both hypergraphs in real six-dimensional space.

</details>


### [90] [Sharp spectral lower bounds for the booksize of a graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20225)
*Lele Liu,Bo Ning*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明当λ(G)≥λ(T_{n,2})且含三角形时，booksize满足 bk(G)≥λ(G)-n/3，核心是更强的定量估计 bk(G)≥λ(G)-2e(G)/(3λ(G))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究图谱理论中最大特征值λ(G)与图的书本大小bk(G)（共享同一条边的最多三角形数）之间的定量关系，并以此解决Erdős猜想、Edwards结果以及Zhai-Lin、Li-Liu-Zhang的公开问题。

Method: 证明更精确的不等式 bk(G)≥λ(G)-2e(G)/(3λ(G))（在λ(G)^2>e(G)条件下），并将其应用于推导主定理及各推论。

Result: 1）主定理：若λ(G)≥λ(T_{n,2})且G含三角形，则bk(G)≥λ(G)-n/3；2）推论：e(G)>n^2/4时，bk(G)>2e(G)/n - n/3，强于Erdős猜想并由Edwards证明；3）正面解决Zhai-Lin (JGT,2023)的公开问题；4）正面解决Li-Liu-Zhang (JCTB,2026)的公开问题（Zhang等独立证明）。

Conclusion: 论文通过一个更强的定量估计统一并强化了图谱极值中若干已知结果，同时解决了多个公开问题。

Abstract: Let $G$ be an $n$-vertex graph with $e(G)$ edges, and let $λ(G)$ denote the largest eigenvalue of its adjacency matrix. The booksize $\mathrm{bk} (G)$ of $G$ is defined as the largest number of triangles sharing a common edge. The main purpose of this note is to prove that if $λ(G)\geqλ(T_{n,2})$ and $G$ contains a triangle, then $\mathrm{bk} (G)\geq λ(G) - n/3$. To obtain this goal, we prove the sharper quantitative estimate $\mathrm{bk} (G)\geqλ(G)-\frac{2e(G)}{3λ(G)}$ whenever $λ(G)^2 > e(G)$. As the first consequence, we derive that an $n$-vertex graph $G$ with $e(G) > n^2/4$ satisfies that $\mathrm{bk} (G) > \frac{2e(G)}{n} - \frac{n}{3}$, which is stronger than an old conjecture of Erdős, and was proved by Edwards. The second corollary is a positive solution to an open problem by Zhai and Lin (JGT, 2023). The third application is a positive solution to an open problem of Li, Liu, and Zhang (JCTB, 2026), which was independently proved by Zhang et al.

</details>


### [91] [Self-complementary completions on six vertices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20231)
*Xinan Dai,Wenhao Deng,Yingdong Shi,Tailin Wu,Yuchen Yang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了 cthreshold(6)=7，并完整刻画了八弧障碍层：5个同构类（识别逆向图后为3个），均为弧极小，且每个都能与自身同构副本打包，说明一般打包严格弱于同阶自互补完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定自互补完成阈值 cthreshold(n) 的具体取值，并理解障碍结构及其与一般打包性质的关系。

Method: 组合构造与直接论证：利用自互补置换给出上界，并对八弧障碍层进行同构类分类与弧极小性验证。

Result: cthreshold(6)=7；上界由 K3 ⊎ (x→y→z) 见证；八弧障碍层含5个同构类（逆向识别后3个），全部弧极小，且每个都与自身同构副本打包。

Conclusion: 普通打包严格弱于同阶自互补完成，该差异在首个失败层即出现。

Abstract: Let \(\cthreshold(n)\) be the largest integer \(q\) such that every loopless digraph on \(n\) vertices with at most \(q\) arcs is isomorphic to a spanning subdigraph of a self-complementary digraph of order \(n\). We prove that \(\cthreshold(6)=7\). The upper bound is witnessed by \[ \bK{3}\dunion (x\longrightarrow y\longrightarrow z), \] and follows from a direct argument with a self-complementing permutation. We also determine the complete eight-arc obstruction layer: it consists of five isomorphism classes, or three after converse digraphs are identified. All five are arc-minimal. Each nevertheless packs with an isomorphic copy of itself, so ordinary packing is strictly weaker than same-order self-complementary completion already at this first failure layer.

</details>


### [92] [The homomorphism threshold of odd cycle $C_{2k-1}$ is below $\frac{1}{2k-1}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20237)
*Jian Wang,Shipeng Wang,Zixiang Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究奇圈 C_{2k-1} 的同态阈值 δ_hom(C_{2k-1})，改进了 Ebsen–Schacht 给出的上界 1/(2k-1)，并给出了基于 Nešetřil–Zhu 稀疏同态定理的新下界，从而证明各奇圈（长度≥5）的色数、同态、多项式移除与线性移除阈值两两不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同态阈值的确定一般极难，奇圈 C_{2k-1}（k≥3）是最重要的公开情形之一。此前 Ebsen–Schacht 证明上界为 1/(2k-1)，且该值在禁止所有短奇圈、blowup 阈值等情境下恰好取到，Letzter–Snyder 甚至明确猜测 δ_hom(C_5)=1/5。作者意在检验并推翻这一“最优上界”的直觉。

Method: 下界：基于 Nešetřil 与 Zhu 的稀疏同态定理构造新的图论构造，改进了 Sankar 的定量下界。上界：提出新的结构论证，控制短奇路径上的公共邻域，从而给出严格优于 1/(2k-1) 的上界。

Result: 对每个整数 k≥3，证明 1/(2((k-1)^{4k-5}(2k-1)+((k-1)^{4k-5}-1)/(k-2))) ≤ δ_hom(C_{2k-1}) ≤ 4(k-1)/(4(k-1)(2k-1)+1) &lt; 1/(2k-1)。新下界优于 Sankar 的定量结果；新上界严格小于 1/(2k-1)，否定了 δ_hom(C_5)=1/5 的猜测方向。

Conclusion: δ_hom(C_{2k-1}) 严格小于 1/(2k-1)。由此推出：长度至少为 5 的每个奇圈，其色数阈值、同态阈值、多项式移除阈值和线性移除阈值两两互异，解决了 Fox 与 Wigderson 的两个猜想。

Abstract: The homomorphism threshold $δ_{\text{hom}}(H)$ of a graph $H$ asks how large the minimum degree of an $H$-free graph has to be in order to force a homomorphism to a bounded $H$-free graph. Determining this threshold is in general very difficult, and the odd cycles $C_{2k-1}$ are among the most important open cases for $k\ge 3$. Ebsen and Schacht proved the general upper bound $δ_{\text{hom}}(C_{2k-1})\le\frac{1}{2k-1}$, while a breakthrough of Sankar, using topological methods and a graph-theoretic analogue of homotopy equivalence, gave the first positive lower bound. The value $\frac{1}{2k-1}$ appeared particularly compelling: Ebsen and Schacht obtained the same exact threshold when all odd cycles of length at most $2k-1$ are forbidden, Huang, Liu, Rong and Xu later proved that it is the exact blowup threshold of $C_{2k-1}$, and Letzter and Snyder also explicitly asked whether $δ_{\text{hom}}(C_{5})=\frac{1}{5}$. Surprisingly, we show that the upper bound can be improved. More precisely, for every integer $k\ge3$, we prove $$ \frac{1}{2\left((k-1)^{4k-5}(2k-1)+\frac{(k-1)^{4k-5}-1}{k-2}\right)} \le δ_{\text{hom}}(C_{2k-1}) \le \frac{4(k-1)}{4(k-1)(2k-1)+1} <
\frac{1}{2k-1}. $$ The new lower bound comes from a new graph-theoretic construction based on a sparse homomorphism theorem of Nešetřil and Zhu, and it improves Sankar's quantitative bound. The improved upper bound follows from a new structural argument that controls common neighborhoods along short odd paths. Our results have various consequences, in particular, every odd cycle of length at least five has pairwise distinct chromatic, homomorphism, polynomial removal, and linear removal thresholds, resolving two conjectures of Fox and Wigderson.

</details>


### [93] [On the difference between clique partition and clique covering numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20305)
*Bo Ning*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明 \(d_n=\Theta(n^{4/3})\)，否定了 Erdős、Faudree 和 Ordman 关于 \(d_n=O(n)\) 的猜想，并给出了 deficit 的正确阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果仅给出 \(d_n=O(n^{3/2})\) 的上界，而 Erdős、Faudree 和 Ordman 在 1983 年提问 \(d_n\) 是否满足 \(d_n=O(n)\)。本文旨在确定 \(d_n\) 的真实渐近阶并回答该问题。

Method: 通过构造图并分析边团划分数与团覆盖数之差 \(\operatorname{cp}(G)-\operatorname{cc}(G)\) 的极值 \(\sigma_n\)，进而研究 deficit \(d_n=\lfloor n^2/4\rfloor-\sigma_n\) 的上下界。

Result: 证明了 \(d_n=\Theta(n^{4/3})\)，即 deficit 的正确阶为 \(n^{4/3}\)。

Conclusion: 该结果否定了 Erdős、Faudree 和 Ordman 的问题，表明 \(d_n\neq O(n)\)，并为该参数给出了紧的渐近阶。

Abstract: For a graph \(G\), let \(\operatorname{cp}(G)\) and \(\operatorname{cc}(G)\) be the minimum numbers of cliques in an edge partition and a clique cover of \(G\), respectively. Set $σ_n
  = \max_{\lvert V(G)\rvert=n}
  \bigl(\operatorname{cp}(G)-\operatorname{cc}(G)\bigr), d_n=\left\lfloor\frac{n^2}{4}\right\rfloor-σ_n.$ In 1983, Erdős, Faudree, and Ordman asked whether \(d_n=O(n)\). Caccetta, Erdős, Ordman, and Pullman previously constructed graphs showing \(d_n=O(n^{3/2})\). We prove that $d_n=Θ(n^{4/3}),$ thereby determining the correct order of the deficit and answering their question in the negative.

</details>


### [94] [Two-sided linear hashing and quadratic density bounds for smooth lattice coverings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20351)
*Ben Lund*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限域子集的随机线性投影中纤维大小的集中性，刻画保证所有纤维相对偏差所需的平均纤维规模，并将其应用于光滑格覆盖问题，将O(n^3)界改进为O(n^2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对有限域子集的随机线性投影，需要确定平均纤维规模需多大才能保证所有纤维满足预设相对偏差与失败概率；此类哈希/熵损失问题与二元障碍结果相关，且可用于改进格点覆盖中格点计数的界。

Method: 结合商-平均计数引理、Dhar与Dvir的局部平衡/非平衡论证，以及Dhar、Dvir、Kumar与Mon的Furstenberg估计，对S⊆F_q^n投影到F_q^b进行分析，给出三种参数区域的平均纤维规模充分条件并推导熵损失；再将结果用于Ordentlich、Regev与Weiss的归约来改进光滑格覆盖界。

Result: 在固定偏差与失败概率下，充分平均纤维规模为：任意q时O(q2^b)，q为b的合适常数倍时O(q^2)，固定q时O_q(b)。固定域上熵损失为h-b=log_q h+O(1)，与Alon等人(1999)二元障碍同阶，并给出每个固定域上的定量随机源精化。应用上，把ORW关于光滑格覆盖的O(n^3)界改进为O(n^2)：对每个固定凸体K，体积≥Cn^2且n足够大时，Haar-Siegel随机格在K的每个平移中的格点数以预设高概率落在vol(K)的预设相对误差内；常数与维数阈值仅依赖于误差和失败概率。

Conclusion: 该工作给出有限域随机线性投影下纤维大小的紧致纤维规模与熵损失刻画，匹配已知二元障碍阶数；并以此改进光滑格覆盖的格点数估计至O(n^2)。同时指出Kopparty等人高秩Kakeya集补集表明，在同一归约下任意子集的哈希保证无法达到更小阶数。

Abstract: We study random linear projections of a finite-field subset for which every fiber has cardinality close to its mean. We bound the mean fiber size needed to ensure that all fibers satisfy a prescribed relative discrepancy, with a prescribed failure probability. For $S\subseteq\mathbb F_q^n$ projected to $\mathbb F_q^b$, one theorem gives three regimes: at fixed discrepancy and failure probability, sufficient mean fiber sizes are $O(q2^b)$ for arbitrary $q$, $O(q^2)$ when $q$ is at least a suitable constant multiple of $b$, and $O_q(b)$ for fixed $q$. The resulting entropy loss over fixed fields is $h-b=\log_q h+O(1)$, where $h=\log_q|S|$ is the input entropy. This matches the order of the binary obstruction of Alon, Dietzfelbinger, Miltersen, Petrank, and Tardos (1999); we give a quantitative random-source refinement over every fixed field. Our proof combines a quotient-and-average counting lemma with the local balanced/unbalanced argument of Dhar and Dvir (arXiv:2204.01665) and Furstenberg estimates of Dhar and Dvir and Kumar and Mon (arXiv:2609.17020).
  We apply these bounds in the reduction of Ordentlich, Regev, and Weiss (arXiv:2311.04644) to improve their $O(n^3)$ bound for smooth lattice coverings to $O(n^2)$. For each fixed convex body $K\subseteq\mathbb R^n$, a Haar-Siegel random lattice of covolume one has the number of lattice points in every translate of $K$ within a prescribed relative error of $\operatorname{vol}(K)$, with prescribed high probability, once $\operatorname{vol}(K)\ge Cn^2$ and $n$ is sufficiently large. The constant and dimension cutoff depend only on the error and failure probability. Complements of higher-rank Kakeya sets of Kopparty, Lev, Saraf, and Sudan (arXiv:1003.3736) show that no hashing guarantee for arbitrary subsets can yield a smaller order in the same reduction.

</details>


### [95] [Brown-Gerver-Ramsey Theorems in Small Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20366)
*Stijn Cambie,Erik Kalviainen,J. Shallit*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究标准单位步长的$\mathbb{N}^k$中避免$t$点共线的无穷游走，证明了其在$(k,t)\in\{(6,3),(4,4),(3,7)\}$时存在，并把$k=3$的已知界从$189$改进到$7$，同时给出弱Abel方自由/立自由/6次幂自由无穷词的存在性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原问题关注在$\mathbb{N}^k$中用标准基向量步构造避免$t$点共线的无穷游走；既有结果中$k=3$情形界很大（$189$），亟需更紧构造，并与弱Abel幂自由词的组合词问题建立联系。

Method: 通过显式构造在$\mathbb{N}^k$中满足标准单位基向量步的无穷游走，使其不含$t$个共线点，分别覆盖$(k,t)=(6,3),(4,4),(3,7)$；利用游走与词的联系导出弱Abel方自由、立自由及6次幂自由的无穷词。

Result: 证明了$(k,t)\in\{(6,3),(4,4),(3,7)\}$时此类无穷游走存在；特别地，$k=3$时$t$的界由Lidbetter的$189$降至$7$；并推出在小有限字母表上存在弱Abel方自由（以及弱Abel立自由、弱Abel 6次幂自由）的无穷词。

Conclusion: 该工作显著改进了共线避免游走在三维情形的参数界，并统一处理了一类弱Abel幂自由无穷词的存在性，为高维游走与幂自由词之间的对应提供了更强构造性证据。

Abstract: We consider infinite walks in $\mathbb{N}^k$ with standard unit basis vector steps that avoid $t$ collinear points, and show that these walks exist for $(k,t) \in \{(6,3), (4,4), (3,7)\}$. In particular, our construction for $k = 3$ improves the previous bound $189$, obtained by Lidbetter, to $7$. Our results also imply the existence of infinite words over small finite alphabets that are weakly abelian squarefree (resp., weakly abelian cubefree, weakly abelian 6th-power-free).

</details>


### [96] [New upper bounds for the chromatic numbers of Euclidean spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20436)
*Leonid Ivanov,Nadezhda Glushkova*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究禁止距离段 $[1,\ell]$ 下欧氏空间 $\R^n$ 的着色色数 $\chi(\R^n,[1,\ell])$，通过显式有理格、Eisenstein 格叠层与解析方法，改进了 $\R^4,\R^5,\R^7,\R^9,\R^{10}$ 的色数上界，并给出 $\R^{25},\R^{26}$ 的新界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Nelson--Hadwiger 问题给出 $\chi(\R^n)$ 即禁止距离段 $[1,1]$ 的情形，是组合几何中长期未决的难题；已有上界如 $\R^4$ 的 49、$\R^5$ 的 140 等长期停滞，且 Arman、Bondarenko、Prymak 与 Radchenko 猜测这些值在所有格着色中最优。本文旨在打破这些上界并检验该最优性猜测。

Method: 方法分三类：(1) 构造显式有理格——$\R^4$ 用 Eisenstein 格、$\R^5$ 用一般位置格，并用一个验证协议将每个界归结为有限个显式有理数的精确算术不等式；(2) 对 Eisenstein 着色 $E_6^*/343$ 与 $E_8/2401$ 做叠层，用于 $\R^7,\R^9$；(3) 解析证明：对任意 Eisenstein 格 $\Lambda$，$(3+\omega)\Lambda$ 同色胞元间距等于 $\sqrt{7/3}\,\lambda_1(\Lambda)$，并给出正交积宽度满足 $\sum_i 1/d_i^2\le1$ 的乘积法则。

Result: 得到新上界 $\chi(\R^4)\le43$、$\chi(\R^5)\le132$、$\chi(\R^7)\le1029$、$\chi(\R^9)\le7203$、$\chi(\R^{10})\le45619$，改进了原有的 49、140、1372、17253、$3^{10}$，从而反驳了 49 与 140 在所有 $\R^4,\R^5$ 格着色中最优的猜测。由解析方法得 $45619=2401\cdot19$，是 $\R^{10}$ 首个低于 $3^n$ 的界，并给出 $\chi(\R^{25})\le4\cdot7^{12}$、$\chi(\R^{26})\le19\cdot7^{12}$。此外证明 $E_8$ 无指数低于 2401 的子格能定义合法着色。

Conclusion: 本文通过显式格构造、叠层技术与解析宽度分析，显著改进了多个维度的色数上界，推翻了关于 $\R^4,\R^5$ 最优格着色的既有猜测，并首次使 $\R^{10}$ 的界低于 $3^n$。所有代码、精确证书与数据均公开，为后续 Nelson--Hadwiger 类问题的精确算术验证提供了可复用框架。

Abstract: A coloring of $\R^n$ is \emph{proper for the forbidden distance segment} $[1,\ell]$ if no two points of the same color are at a distance from $[1,\ell]$; the minimum number of colors is $χ(\R^n,[1,\ell])$, and $\ell=1$ gives the classical chromatic number $χ(\R^n)$ of the Nelson--Hadwiger problem. We prove the new upper bounds $χ(\R^4)\le43$, $χ(\R^5)\le132$, $χ(\R^7)\le1029$, $χ(\R^9)\le7203$, $χ(\R^{10})\le45619$, improving the previously known $49$, $140$, $1372$, $17253$ and $3^{10}$; in particular, this refutes the conjecture of Arman, Bondarenko, Prymak and Radchenko that $49$ and $140$ are optimal among all lattice colorings of $\R^4$ and $\R^5$. The first four bounds come from explicit rational lattices --- an Eisenstein lattice in $\R^4$, a lattice in general position in $\R^5$, and laminations of the Eisenstein colorings $E_6^*/343$ and $E_8/2401$ in $\R^7$ and $\R^9$ --- and each is reduced, by one verification protocol, to a finite list of inequalities between explicitly written rational numbers checked in exact arithmetic. The fifth bound is analytic: we prove that for every Eisenstein lattice $Λ$ the distance between same-colored cells of $(3+ω)Λ$ equals $\sqrt{7/3}\,λ_1(Λ)$, which gives the exact widths of all known colorings with $7^{n/2}$ colors, and a product rule $\sum_i1/d_i^2\le1$ for the widths of orthogonal products; together they yield $45619=2401\cdot19$, the first bound in $\R^{10}$ below $3^n$, as well as $χ(\R^{25})\le4\cdot7^{12}$ and $χ(\R^{26})\le19\cdot7^{12}$. We also show that no sublattice of $E_8$ of index below $2401$ defines a proper coloring. All code, exact certificates and data are open.

</details>


### [97] [The extensible no-four-on-a-circle problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20447)
*Anubhab Ghosal,Ritesh Goenka*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文证明了存在一个不含四点共圆或共线的整数点集 S ⊂ Z²，满足 |S ∩ [n]²| = Ω(n)，从而（在常数意义下）解决了关于可扩展无四点共圆构造密度的公开问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 [n]² 中，任何不含四点共圆的集合大小至多为 O(n)，而此前未知能否达到线性规模的构造。作者与 Keevash 提出的问题正是关于这类可扩展无四点共圆构造的密度能否达到 Ω(n)。

Method: 构造方法基于对整数格点的加权随机采样，随后进行仔细的删除操作，以消除所有共圆或共线的四点组。

Result: 构造出的集合 S 满足 |S ∩ [n]²| = Ω(n)，即达到线性密度，与上界 O(n) 仅差常数因子。

Conclusion: 该结果在常数意义下解决了作者与 Keevash 提出的关于无四点共圆构造稠密性的问题，证明了线性密度构造的存在性。

Abstract: We show that there exists a set $S \subset \mathbb{Z}^2$ containing no four points on a circle or a line such that $|S \cap [n]^2| = Ω(n)$ as $n \rightarrow \infty$. Since any no-four-on-a-circle set in $[n]^2$ has size $O(n)$, this resolves (up to a constant) a question raised by the current authors and Keevash concerning the density of extensible no-four-on-a-circle constructions. Our construction is based on weighted random sampling from the integer lattice followed by careful deletion.

</details>


### [98] [Nonexistence of a Leech Tree of Order 18: A Computer-Assisted Proof](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20492)
*Maseeh Ghodsi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过计算机辅助证明，证明不存在 18 阶 Leech 树。证明分三层：Lean 4 形式化验证结构事实、常规数学论证证明精确覆盖条件与递归搜索完备性、穷举计算排除八种局部构型；因搜索程序与证书检查器未在 Lean 中形式化，结论属于计算机辅助证明而非端到端 Lean 证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Leech 树要求其全部 n(n-1)/2 个加权两两距离恰好为 1,2,…,n(n-1)/2。此前该类树的存在性在若干阶数上未知，18 阶是否为不存在的最小未知情形之一，因此需要给出 18 阶不存在性的严格证据。论文旨在以可验证、可复现的方式排除 18 阶 Leech 树，并明确区分已形式化部分与未形式化部分，界定结论的可靠性边界。

Method: 采用三层混合证明结构。第一层：在 Lean 4 中形式化并内核检查结构事实，将所有可能实例归约到八种局部构型，并证明若干必要条件。第二层：以常规数学论证证明“组件对—整块”精确覆盖条件，并证明递归搜索的完备性。第三层：穷举计算封闭全部八种构型，记录精确覆盖、源与输入哈希、终端回执及已检查的精确零结果。

Result: 证明不存在 18 阶 Leech 树。归约后仅剩八种局部构型需处理，穷举计算将其全部排除；计算过程附带精确覆盖记录、输入与源码哈希、终端回执和精确零结果校验。结构层已通过 Lean 内核检查，但搜索程序、其执行过程及证书检查器尚未在 Lean 中形式化。

Conclusion: 18 阶 Leech 树不存在。该结论构成计算机辅助证明，而非端到端 Lean 证明；其可信度建立在 Lean 已验证的结构层、常规数学论证的覆盖与完备性，以及可复现的穷举计算之上。作者明确界定了形式化边界，提示后续工作可将搜索与证书检查形式化以升级为端到端验证。

Abstract: A Leech tree of order $n$ is a tree with positive integral edge weights whose $n(n-1)/2$ pairwise weighted distances are precisely $1,2,\ldots,n(n-1)/2$. This paper gives a computer-assisted proof that no Leech tree of order $18$ exists. The argument has three layers. First, a development in Lean 4 verifies the structural facts used in the paper. These facts reduce every putative example to one of eight local configurations and justify several necessary conditions. Second, conventional mathematical arguments prove a component-pair whole-block exact-cover condition and the completeness of a recursive search. Third, exhaustive computations close all eight configurations. The computation records exact coverage, source and input hashes, terminal receipts, and checked exact-zero results. The structural layer is kernel-checked, but the search program, its execution, and the certificate checker have not been formalized in Lean. The result is therefore a computer-assisted proof, not an end-to-end Lean proof.

</details>


### [99] [Four Special Directions in AG(2,13): The 52-Point Obstruction and the Sharp Minimum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20495)
*Qihang Wang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明 F_13^2 的 52 点子集不可能恰有四个特殊方向，从而结合已有下界与 65 点构造，确定恰有四个特殊方向的子集最小大小为 65。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定仿射平面 F_13^2 中恰有四个特殊方向的子集的最小基数。已知 Ghidelli 给出下界，Kiss 与 Somlai 给出 65 点构造；本文旨在排除中间情形，尤其是排除 52 点这一关键障碍，从而封闭下界与构造之间的间隙。

Method: 利用通用关联恒等式，将四个例外线计数函数化为 F_13 上的多项式恒等式；借助相关三次及以上二元型的线性无关性，推出这些函数次数至多为二次；再对常数、线性、二次剖分进行分类，最终归结为一个无解的二次特征同余式。由此一次性覆盖所有 52 点子集与所有四方向集合的量词。

Result: 证明不存在具有恰好四个特殊方向的 52 点 F_13^2 子集。结合 Ghidelli 的下界与 Kiss–Somlai 的 65 点构造，确定最小大小为 65。

Conclusion: F_13^2 中恰有四个特殊方向的子集最小基数为 65。方法上，关联恒等式与二元型线性无关性的组合将无限量词问题约化为有限精确的二次值表分类。

Abstract: We prove that no $52$-point subset of the affine plane $\mathbb F_{13}^{2}$ has exactly four special directions, where a direction is special when its thirteen parallel affine lines do not all meet the set in the same number of points. A universal incidence identity reduces the four exceptional line-count functions to a polynomial identity over $\mathbb F_{13}$. Linear independence of the associated binary forms of degree at least three then forces those functions to have degree at most two. Classification of the resulting constant, linear, and quadratic profiles leaves a quadratic-character congruence with no solution. The incidence and polynomial argument supplies the quantifier over all $52$-point subsets and all four-direction sets; the remaining classification of quadratic value tables is finite and exact. Together with Ghidelli's lower bound and the $65$-point construction of Kiss and Somlai, this determines the minimum size of a subset of $\mathbb F_{13}^{2}$ with exactly four special directions: it is $65$.
  The AI-assisted workflow used OpenAI GPT-5.6 Sol, Anthropic Claude Fable 5, Grok 4.6, and OpenAI GPT-6 Astra, together with Codex-controlled Danus.

</details>


### [100] [On a class of combinatorial group invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20516)
*Arthur Fernandes,Claudio Qureshi,Lucas Reis,Sávio Ribas*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究与有限群相关的组合结构（幂映射的函数图与幂图）所导出的群不变量，并刻画这些不变量在同构类集合上诱导的等价关系（划分），从最细到最粗进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 幂映射函数图与幂图等组合结构能导出有限群的不变量，并由此在同构类上产生划分。作者希望系统厘清这些划分之间的关系，判断它们是否彼此重合，从而揭示不同组合不变量对群的区分能力。

Method: 结合组合学与数论的工具与思想，仅使用群论的基本概念；构造一个一般性框架，用以比较由组合结构导出的等价关系（划分），并对幂映射函数图、幂图等不变量诱导的划分按精细程度排序。

Result: 在所有有限群层面，这些划分中除三个以外全部互相重合；在幂零群子类中，除两个以外全部重合；作者进一步引入一大类幂零群，并证明在该类内这些划分中除一个以外均一致。

Conclusion: 由不同组合结构导出的群不变量所诱导的划分高度重合，表明这些不变量在区分有限群（尤其是幂零群）时具有显著的等价性；所构造的一般框架可用于类似情形，并据此提出若干开放问题。

Abstract: In this paper, we explore group invariants arising from combinatorial structures associated with finite groups, including the functional graphs of power maps and the well-studied power graphs. These invariants induce equivalence relations (and hence partitions) on the set of isomorphism classes of finite groups, which we classify from the finest to the coarsest. Surprisingly, all but three of these partitions turn out to coincide; for the subclass of nilpotent groups, all but two coincide. Furthermore, we introduce a broad class of nilpotent groups and show that, within this class, all but one of these partitions agree. Our proofs draw on tools and ideas from Combinatorics and Number Theory, while requiring only basic notions from Group Theory. In particular, we construct a general framework that may prove useful in contexts similar to those considered in this paper. Finally, we propose some open questions that emerge from our results.

</details>


### [101] [Fractional expectation thresholds and the "second" Kahn-Kalai conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20546)
*Tuan Tran*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了图H副本上的均匀测度是 Cq_H log(2e(H))-spread 的，给出分数期望阈值上界，并在树及最大度不超过平均度指数的图上消除对数损失，从而对这些图验证了"第二"Kahn-Kalai猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kahn-Kalai猜想刻画了图H副本出现(increasing property)的阈值与期望阈值之间的关系，"第二"Kahn-Kalai猜想聚焦于分数期望阈值(scaled threshold/fractional expectation threshold)与期望阈值的关系。本文旨在给出期望阈值与分数期望阈值之间的定量联系，并在更广的图类上验证该猜想。

Method: 利用图H的"图形期望阈值"q_H（基于期望计数为一定义），研究H副本上的均匀测度(uniform measure)的spread性质，通过证明该测度是 Cq_H log(2e(H))-spread 来推导分数期望阈值估计；对树和最大度不超过平均度指数的图，进一步改进技术以移除对数因子，使下界与 q_H 同阶。

Result: 1) 均匀测度是 Cq_H log(2e(H))-spread；2) 分数期望阈值不超过 C q_H log(2e(H))；3) 对树以及最大度至多为其平均度指数级的图，可移除对数损失，即分数期望阈值为 O(q_H)。

Conclusion: "第二"Kahn-Kalai猜想对所研究图类成立；一般情形下分数期望阈值与期望阈值之间至多相差一个对数因子，而对树和低最大度图，此差距可完全消除。

Abstract: We show that the uniform measure on copies of a graph $H$ is $Cq_H\log(2e(H))$-spread, where $q_H$ is its graphic expectation threshold defined using expected count one. This gives a fractional expectation threshold of at most $C\pe(H)\log(2e(H))$. We remove the logarithmic loss for trees and for graphs whose maximum degree is at most exponential in their average degree. The ``second'' Kahn-Kalai conjecture therefore holds for all such graphs.

</details>


### [102] [On the minimum number of triangles in balanced tripartite graphs with large minimum degree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20590)
*Chunqiu Fang,Rongxing Xu*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文研究三部图中最少三角形数 $f(n,t)$ 的上下界。作者构造出新的图族，证明 $f(n,t)\le(1+\sqrt5)t^3+(1+1/\sqrt5)t^2$，推翻了 Bollobás、Erdős 和 Szemerédi 关于 $f(n,t)\ge4t^3$ 的猜想；同时将下界从 $t^3$ 改进到 $\frac{12}{5}t^3$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1975年 Bollobás、Erdős 和 Szemerédi 提出三部图最少三角形问题，并猜测当 $n\ge5t$ 时 $f(n,t)\ge4t^3$。该猜想长期未解决，作者试图确定 $f(n,t)$ 的真实增长量级并验证或推翻该猜想。

Method: 构造三部图族：在每部 $n$ 个顶点、最小度至少 $n+t$ 的条件下，设计特定连边结构使得三角形总数仅约为 $(1+\sqrt5)t^3$，从而给出上界；并通过组合计数与极值图论方法改进下界估计。

Result: 对任意整数 $t\ge1$ 及 $n\ge3t+2\lceil(1+\sqrt5)t/2\rceil$，有 $f(n,t)\le(1+\sqrt5)t^3+(1+1/\sqrt5)t^2$，其中 $1+\sqrt5\approx3.236<4$，故当 $t\ge2$ 时该上界严格小于 $4t^3$，推翻原猜想。此外证明对任意整数 $t\ge2$ 及 $n\ge18t^6$，有 $f(n,t)\ge\frac{12}{5}t^3$，改进了原有的 $t^3$ 下界。

Conclusion: Bollobás、Erdős 和 Szemerédi 关于 $f(n,t)\ge4t^3$ 的猜想不成立；$f(n,t)$ 的真实值介于 $\frac{12}{5}t^3$ 与 $(1+\sqrt5)t^3$ 之间，上下界之间的差距仍待进一步缩小。

Abstract: Let $f(n,t)$ be the minimum number of triangles in a tripartite graph with $n$ vertices in each part and minimum degree at least $n+t$. In 1975, Bollobás, Erdős and Szemerédi proved that $f(n,1)=\min\{4,n\}$. They further remarked that it is ``very likely'' that $f(n,t)\ge4t^3$ for $n\ge5t$. They also proved that $f(n,t) \ge t^3$ for all integers $n \ge t \ge 1$. We construct graphs showing that, for all integers $t\ge1$ and $n\ge3t+2\lceil(1+\sqrt5)t/2\rceil$, \[ f(n,t)\le(1+\sqrt5)t^3+\left(1+\frac1{\sqrt5}\right)t^2. \] Here $1+\sqrt5\approx3.236<4$, and the displayed upper bound is strictly less than $4t^3$ for every $t\ge2$, disproving their proposed bound. We also improve their lower bound $t^3$ by showing that $f(n,t)\ge\frac{12}{5}t^3$ for all integers $t\ge2$ and $n\ge18t^6$.

</details>


### [103] [Arboricity and Simplicial Geometric Category of Wedges and Joins of Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20606)
*Nursultan Kuanyshov,Islam Yeginbay*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 研究图的arboricity在wedge与join运算下的行为，导出simplicial geometric category的wedge公式及join的估计，并给出若干图类的显式计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: arboricity与simplicial geometric category之间存在刻画关系，但该不变量在两个基本图运算（wedge与join）下如何变化尚缺乏系统结论，研究此问题可加深对该不变量的理解并拓展其可计算性。

Method: 考察arboricity在wedge与join下的行为：对wedge给出精确公式，对join建立一般的上界与下界；借助连通图simplicial geometric category用arboricity的刻画，将结论迁移至该范畴不变量；通过构造森林分解及相应强可塌缩子复形的覆盖，对若干图类进行显式计算。

Result: 得到wedge下arboricity的精确公式，以及join下的一般上、下界；据此导出simplicial geometric category的wedge公式并给出图join的相应估计；通过显式算例验证了这些结果。

Conclusion: arboricity与simplicial geometric category在wedge运算下具有精确可计算性，在join运算下可由上下界有效控制，森林分解与强可塌缩子复形覆盖为相关计算提供了可行工具。

Abstract: We investigate the behavior of arboricity under two fundamental graph operations, namely wedges and joins, proving an exact formula for wedges and establishing general upper and lower bounds for joins. Using the characterization of the simplicial geometric category of connected graphs in terms of arboricity, we derive a wedge formula for simplicial geometric category and obtain corresponding estimates for graph joins. Finally, we illustrate these results through explicit computations for several classes of graphs by constructing forest decompositions and the associated covers by strongly collapsible subcomplexes.

</details>


### [104] [Infinite log-concavity of the Boros--Moll sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20653)
*Matthew H. Y. Xie,Philip B. Zhang*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明Boros--Moll系数序列对应的多项式$M_n(x)$仅有简单负零点，且与同次Narayana多项式的零点严格交错，从而解决Chen--Yang--Zhang猜想及Boros--Moll无限对数凹性猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boros与Moll提出的无限对数凹性猜想长期未决；Chen、Yang和Zhang进一步提出关于$M_n(x)$零点性质的具体猜想。本文旨在直接证明该猜想并借此解决无限对数凹性问题。

Method: 将$M_n(x)$的反向归一化形式展开为Narayana多项式导数的线性组合，并对归一化导数的权重及部分和进行估计，结合Brändén的保持定理。

Result: 对每个整数$n\ge1$，多项式$M_n(x)=\sum_{i=0}^n(d_i(n)^2-d_{i-1}(n)d_{i+1}(n))x^i$仅有简单负零点，且这些零点与同次Narayana多项式的零点严格交错。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了Chen--Yang--Zhang猜想，并利用Brändén保持定理确立了Boros--Moll无限对数凹性猜想。

Abstract: Let $(d_i(n))_{i=0}^n$ be the Boros--Moll coefficient sequence. We prove that, for every integer $n\ge1$, the polynomial \[
  M_n(x)=\sum_{i=0}^n
  \bigl(d_i(n)^2-d_{i-1}(n)d_{i+1}(n)\bigr)x^i \] has only simple negative zeros, which strictly interlace those of the Narayana polynomial of the same degree. This proves a conjecture of Chen, Yang, and Zhang and, by Brändén's preservation theorem, settles the infinite log-concavity conjecture of Boros and Moll. The proof uses an expansion of the reversed and normalized form of $M_n(x)$ in derivatives of the Narayana polynomial, together with estimates for the weights and partial sums of the normalized derivatives.

</details>


### [105] [Framingtopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20656)
*Sergio Alejandro Fernandez de soto Guerrero,Cesar Ceballos,Matias von Bell*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文引入"framingtope"多胞体复形，作为framing格的几何对应物，证明其具有三种等价描述并构造热带实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: framing格由flow多胞体的framed (DKK)三角剖分的对偶图产生，统一了布尔格、Tamari格、弱序格等经典格以及gentle代数的τ-tilting偏序集。作者希望为该格结构提供几何对应物，从而揭示其组合与几何本质。

Method: 定义framingtope为与framing格对应的多胞体复形，其边图即framing格的Hasse图。证明其三种等价描述：framed三角剖分的内部面、覆盖图的成对相干路径集合、framing格的纯区间。进一步利用可容许高度函数构造热带超曲面配置的有界胞腔复形，实现framingtope的热带实现。

Result: framingtope的边图恰为framing格的Hasse图；三种描述等价成立。热带实现给出plane framed图和multioruga图等大类framed图的显式顶点坐标。在multioruga情形，这些坐标给出多重排列上弱序的热带实现；在普通oruga情形，恢复经典permutahedron。

Conclusion: framingtope为framing格提供了统一的几何框架，将格论、三角剖分组合与热带几何联系起来，并推广了permutahedron等经典构造到更广的framed图类。

Abstract: Framing lattices arise from the dual graphs of framed (or DKK) triangulations of flow polytopes and provide a common framework encompassing classical lattices such as the Boolean, Tamari, and weak-order lattices, as well as $τ$-tilting posets of certain gentle algebras. In this paper, we introduce the \emph{framingtope}, a polytopal complex that provides a geometric counterpart to a framing lattice: its edge graph is the Hasse diagram of the framing lattice. We prove that the framingtope admits three equivalent descriptions, in terms of interior faces of the framed triangulation, sets of pairwise coherent routes covering the graph, and pure intervals of the framing lattice. We further construct a tropical realization of the framingtope as the bounded-cell complex of an arrangement of tropical hypersurfaces associated with an admissible height function. This construction yields explicit vertex coordinates for broad classes of framed graphs, including plane framed graphs and multioruga graphs. In the multioruga case, these coordinates give tropical realizations of weak orders on multipermutations and, in the ordinary oruga case, recover the classical permutahedron.

</details>


### [106] [A counterexample to the quantum Hedetniemi conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20690)
*Julius A. Zeiss*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文否定了Godsil-Roberson-Šámal-Severini关于范畴积图的量子色数等于因子量子色数最小值的猜想，构造出有限图G,H使得χ(G×H)≤1538<1539=min(χq(G),χq(H))。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Godsil、Roberson、Šámal与Severini曾猜想范畴积的量子色数等于两因子量子色数的最小值。该文旨在检验并否定这一猜想，并探讨其在量子色数各变体（空间、近似、交换算子、C*-代数）下是否成立。

Method: 基于Zhu对Hedetniemi猜想的反例，选取其补图的Lovász theta数（而非仅分数色数）较大的基图来构造图G,H。第一因子的下界来自theta界；第二因子则把Zhu的论证推广到非交换投影上，将固定团颜色的步骤替换为算子恒等式，使下界对任意非零含单位C*-代数中的投影着色都成立。若干较小反例由精确整数数据认证，图构造、证书及射影形式下的反例陈述均在Lean 4中形式化。

Result: 构造出显式有限图G,H满足χ(G×H)≤1538<1539=min(χq(G),χq(H))，从而否定了原猜想；该反例同时说明猜想在空间、近似、交换算子与C*-代数量子色数变体下均失败；并给出可由精确整数数据认证的更小反例。

Conclusion: 范畴积的量子色数等于因子量子色数最小值这一猜想不成立，且其失败不限于某一种量子色数定义。相关构造、整数证书与射影形式反例已在Lean 4中形式化验证。

Abstract: Godsil, Roberson, Šámal and Severini conjectured that the quantum chromatic number of the categorical product of two graphs equals the minimum of the quantum chromatic numbers of the factors. We disprove this conjecture: we construct explicit finite graphs $G,H$ with \[ χ(G\times H) \leq 1538 < 1539 = \min(χ_q(G),χ_q(H)).\]The graphs are obtained from Zhu's counterexample to Hedetniemi's conjecture by using a base graph for which the Lovász theta number of the complement, and not only the fractional chromatic number, is large. The lower bound for the first factor is the theta bound. For the second factor we adapt Zhu's argument to projections that do not commute: the step that fixes the colors of a clique is replaced by identities between operators. Both lower bounds hold for colorings by projections in an arbitrary nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra. Hence the conjecture also fails for the spatial, approximate, commuting-operator and $C^*$-algebraic variants of the quantum chromatic number. We also give smaller counterexamples certified by exact integer data. The graph constructions, the certificates and the counterexample statements in the projective formulation are formalized in Lean~4.

</details>


### [107] [Cutting a convex body into fat parts and approximating Euclidean distance by graph distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20702)
*János Pach,Gábor Tardos*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文对 Benjamini 等人提出的关于 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 上是否可构造使图上最短路长度与欧氏距离相差绝对常数的开放问题给出了较弱形式的肯定回答，并借助一个关于胖凸集等面积剖分的几何结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Benjamini、Erdős、Kleiner、Kozma、Schramm 等人提出：能否在整数点集 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 上构造图 $G$，使得任意两顶点的最短路径长度与欧氏距离之差不超过绝对常数？该问题长期开放，本文旨在求解其较弱形式，并同时建立可独立应用的几何剖分工具。

Method: 核心几何工具：存在常数 $c>0$，使得对任意 $i=1,2,\ldots$，每个 $\rho$-fat 平面凸集 $S$ 可被切成 $2^i$ 个等面积凸块，且每块至少为 $c\rho$-fat（fat 性由内切半径与外接半径之比刻画）。基于此构造一个扩张版 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 的无权生成子图 $G$。

Result: 在扩张版 $\mathbb{Z}^2$ 上构造了无权生成子图 $G$：对欧氏距离为 $d$ 的任意顶点对，其 $G$ 中最短路距离介于 $d-O(1)$ 与 $d+o(d^{5/6})$ 之间。相同界也可由顶点集为 $\mathbb{Z}^2$、每条边连接欧氏距离至多 2 的平面图实现。

Conclusion: 对原开放问题的较弱形式给出肯定回答；所依赖的 $\rho$-fat 凸集等面积剖分定理具有独立意义；主要距离偏差上界为 $d+o(d^{5/6})$，且可由平面图实现。

Abstract: Can one construct a graph $G$ on the set of integer points ${\mathbb Z}^2$ in the plane such that the length of the shortest path between any two vertices of $G$ differs from their Euclidean distance by at most an absolute constant? This question of Benjamini, Erd\H os, Kleiner, Kozma, Schramm, and the first-named author has been open for a long time. We give an affirmative answer to a weaker form of this question, based on the following geometric statement, which is of independent interest. There exists a constant $c>0$ such that for every $i=1,2,\ldots,$ every $ρ$-fat plane convex set $S$ can be cut into $2^i$ convex pieces of equal area, each of which is at least $cρ$-fat. (A convex set is $ρ$-fat if the ratio of its inradius to its circumradius is at least $ρ$.)
  We prove that there exists an (unweighted) spanning subgraph $G$ of an enlarged copy of ${\mathbb Z}^2$ such that, for every pair of vertices at Euclidean distance $d$, their shortest-path distance in $G$ lies between $d-O(1)$ and $d+o(d^{5/6})$. The same bound can be achieved by a planar graph with vertex set ${\mathbb Z}^2$, in which every edge joins two vertices at Euclidean distance at most 2.

</details>


### [108] [Longest cycles intersect linearly in highly connected graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20724)
*Jie Ma,Bo Ning,Ziyuan Zhao*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 Smith (1984) 关于 k-连通图中两条最长圈相交顶点数的猜想的首个线性下界：任意两条最长圈至少共享 k/600 个顶点，并提出一种新的结构性方法，可应用于最长圈与最长路的相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Smith (1984) 猜想：对任意 k≥2，k-连通图中任意两条最长圈至少共享 k 个顶点。该猜想长期未决，此前仅有远弱于线性量级的结果，缺乏线性下界。本文旨在给出首个线性下界并突破原有方法局限。

Method: 作者放弃以往基于 Turán 型极值论证的思路，转而发展一种新颖的结构性方法（structural approach），通过分析 k-连通图的结构性质来刻画最长圈的相交行为，并将该框架推广至最长圈与最长路的相关问题。

Result: 证明了任意 k-连通图中任意两条最长圈至少共享 k/600 个顶点，即首个关于该猜想成立的线性下界。此外，该结构性方法还给出了关于最长圈与最长路相关问题的应用结果。

Conclusion: 论文在 Smith 猜想方向上取得重要进展，首次建立线性下界 k/600，并提出可复用的结构方法，为后续改进常数因子及解决相关最长圈/路问题提供了新工具。

Abstract: A longstanding conjecture attributed to Smith (1984) asserts that for every $k\ge2$, any two longest cycles in a $k$-connected graph share at least $k$ vertices. In this paper, we prove the first linear lower bound, showing that any two longest cycles in a $k$-connected graph share at least $k/600$ vertices. Departing from previous Turán-type extremal arguments, we develop a novel structural approach that also yields applications to related problems on longest cycles and paths.

</details>


### [109] [Proof of the Pach-Tardos conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20726)
*Lior Gishboliner,Xiangyu Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 证明了每个无环矩阵模式 P 的极值函数满足 Ex(n,P) ≤ n^{1+O_P(1/log log n)}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矩阵模式极值问题（Zarankiewicz 问题的推广）中，连续禁模式集的渐近阶刻画长期是核心难题。已有结果对无环（森林）模式给出了多项式阶的界，但上界指数中关于 n 的修正项未达最优形式，本文旨在把无环模式的上界改进到 quasi-linear 级别的指数修正。

Method: 使用组合与正则性/归纳类工具，处理矩阵模式禁模式的超图极值问题，通过递归分解无环模式并控制二分图边数，导出指数修正为 O_P(1/log log n)。（原文摘要未给出具体技术细节。）

Result: 对任意 acyclic matrix pattern P，Ex(n,P) ≤ n^{1+O_P(1/log log n)}，即上界为 quasi-linear 阶，指数中仅含约 1/log log n 的修正，改进此前无环模式的界。

Conclusion: 确认了无环（forest）禁矩阵模式的极值数本质上接近线性，仅在对数对数因子意义下偏离线性；这为连续禁模式集合的阶刻画提供了更强证据。

Abstract: We prove that for every acyclic matrix pattern $P$ it holds that $\operatorname{Ex}(n,P) \leq n^{1+O_P(1/\log\log n)}$.

</details>


### [110] [A note on generating polyhedra and quadrangulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20811)
*Luisa Andreis,Riccardo W. Maffucci,Federico Polito*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文给出从唯一初始图（方锥）出发，经两种图变换迭代构造除锥体外的所有多面体；并在对偶层面从方反双锥出发，经唯一变换构造除反双锥外的所有径向图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有构造方法需以整类锥体（或整类反双锥）为起点，起点不唯一，希望找到单一初始图并配合固定变换系统来生成相应图类，从而得到更简洁、统一的迭代生成刻画。

Method: 利用平面3-连通图（多面体）的图论刻画，设计两种图变换，并从方锥这一唯一初始图迭代生成除锥体外的全部多面体；相应地对偶地以唯一图变换从方反双锥生成除反双锥外的全部球面四角剖分（即所有4-圈皆面的径向图）。

Result: 证明了除金字塔（锥体）外的所有多面体均可由方金字塔经两种图变换迭代构造得到；并证明除反双锥外的所有径向图（所有4-圈为面的球面四角剖分）均可由方反双锥经唯一图变换迭代构造得到。

Conclusion: 将原先以整类锥体／反双锥为起点的构造加强为以单一初始图加固定变换的生成方案，给出多面体与径向图（球面四角剖分）的统一迭代构造。

Abstract: A polyhedron is a planar, $3$-connected graph. We iteratively construct all polyhedra (save for pyramids) from a unique starting graph, namely the square pyramid, via two graph transformations. This builds upon a previous construction, that starts from the full class of pyramids, and applies the same transformations.
  In a related result, we iteratively construct all quadrangulations of the sphere where all $4$-cycles are facial, i.e., the class of radial graphs of the polyhedra (save for antibipyramids), from a unique starting graph, namely the square antibipyramid, via a unique graph transformation. This builds upon a previous construction, that starts from the full class of antibipyramids, and applies the same transformation.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [111] [Solving Inverse Dirac-weighted Sturm-Liouville Problems via Cauchy problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19715)
*Min Zhao,Jiangang Qi,and Xiao Chen*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文基于点交互方法，研究带Dirac权的正则Sturm-Liouville反特征值问题，利用扰动问题首特征值显式重构势函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延续前期工作提出的点交互方法，将Dirac权引入正则Sturm-Liouville反特征值问题，解决此类加权情形的势函数重构。

Method: 借助移动点交互模型导出一族扰动问题，仅用其第一特征值确定一类Cauchy问题的解，再由这些解显式重构单点和两点Dirac权情形下的势。

Result: 给出了单点与两点Dirac权正则Sturm-Liouville问题势函数的显式重构公式，并讨论了多点Dirac权情形。

Conclusion: 点交互方法可有效推广至带Dirac权的正则Sturm-Liouville反特征值问题，实现从扰动问题首特征值数据到势函数的显式重构，并具备向多点加权推广的潜力。

Abstract: Building on the point interaction method developed in our previous work, this paper studies inverse eigenvalue problems for regular Sturm-Liouville problems with Dirac weights. More precisely, we explicitly reconstruct the potential of regular Sturm-Liouville problems with the single-point and two-point Dirac weights from the solutions of a class of Cauchy problems which is completely determined by the first eigenvalues of a family of perturbed problems originating from moving point interaction models in quantum mechanics. Finally, the multi-point Dirac weighted case is also discussed.

</details>


### [112] [Finiteness and exponential growth of full graph $p$-spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20291)
*Matthew J. Colbrook*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 证明了有限图上p-拉普拉斯算子全谱的有限性，解决了Amghibech (2003)提出的问题，并给出谱基数与特征向量集连通分支数的拓扑上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图中p-拉普拉斯算子的全谱有限性此前是Amghibech (2003)提出的未解问题；同时缺乏对谱基数、特征向量集拓扑复杂度的定量刻画，以及p=2处谱结构临界转变的理解。

Method: 结合o-minimal与Pfaffian几何、射影L^p对偶、Morse理论、临界群以及张量特征值界。对带符号加权图p-Schrödinger算子引入势项并统一处理所有系数与p；对均匀加权K_n将非平凡特征线识别为RP^{n-2}坐标超平面胞腔分解的重心，计算p=2两侧的局部Morse数据并给出重整化对数极限。

Result: 证明任意有限图、任意实p>1时全谱有限；n顶点m正权边上谱基数对数与特征向量集连通分支总数均为O((n+m)^2)，全谱有理Betti数之和≤exp(C(n+m)^2)。均匀K_n在p≠2时给出至少(3^n-2^{n+1}+3)/2个不同谱值；p=2时最大谱基数为n，p=4时无符号与一般类的最大谱基数指数增长率恰为3。

Conclusion: 彻底解决全谱有限性问题并推广至更一般的特征问题，给出普适拓扑上界；通过K_n例子揭示p=2的临界现象并回答Amghibech极值问题。

Abstract: We prove that the full spectrum of the graph $p$-Laplacian is finite for every finite graph and every real $p>1$. This resolves the problem of finiteness of the full graph spectrum posed by Amghibech (2003). More generally, for signed weighted graph $p$-Schrödinger operators with arbitrary real potentials, we obtain bounds in terms of the numbers of vertices and positive-weight edges, uniform in all coefficients and in $p$. On $n$ vertices with $m$ positive-weight edges, the logarithms of the spectral cardinality and of the total number of connected components of the normalised eigenvector sets are $O((n+m)^2)$; the sum over the full spectrum of their rational Betti numbers is at most $\exp(C(n+m)^2)$ for an absolute constant $C$. These topological bounds extend to homogeneous eigenproblems built from arbitrary finite families of linear forms, including generalised $p$-eigenvalue problems for matrix pairs. For the uniformly weighted $K_n$ and $p\ne2$, we identify the nonconstant eigenlines with the barycentres of the coordinate-hyperplane cell decomposition of $\mathbb RP^{n-2}$ and determine their local Morse data on either side of $p=2$. A renormalised logarithmic limit describes the transition at $p=2$. Positive integer weights then separate these critical values, giving at least $(3^n-2^{n+1}+3)/2$ distinct spectral values for every fixed $p\ne2$ and every $n\geq2$. By contrast, the maximal spectral cardinality at $p=2$ is $n$; at $p=4$, the maximal spectral cardinalities in the unsigned and general classes both have exponential growth rate exactly $3$. For $n\geq3$ the same examples resolve Amghibech's extremal question. The proof combines o-minimal and Pfaffian geometry with projective $L^p$-duality, Morse theory, critical groups, and tensor eigenvalue bounds.

</details>


### [113] [Essential spectral geometry of the Maxwell system in unbounded domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20339)
*Francesco Ferraresso,Marco Marletta*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 分析旋度-旋度算子 M=curl curl 在 R^3 无界区域无散度向量场上的本质谱，给出准锥、准柱及各类角状区域中本质谱的具体刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究电磁/流体相关算子 curl curl 在无界区域中的谱性质，揭示区域几何（准锥、准柱、角状体、穿孔结构）对本质谱的影响。

Method: 在无散度向量场空间上考察 M=curl curl 的本质谱 σ_e(M)；结合区域几何分类（准锥、准柱、角状体）与横截法向调和场，将环形截面角状体归结为一维薛定谔算子 H_V 进行分析。

Result: 准锥区域 σ_e(M)=[0,+∞)；准柱区域 σ_e(M)≠∅；圆截面且边界最终平均凸的角状体 σ_e(M)=∅（与体积无关）；环形截面时 σ_e(M)⊇σ_e(H_V)；对带双指数孔的穿孔指数角状体，随孔收缩速率不同有 σ_e(M)=∅、[γ^2,+∞) 或 [0,+∞) 三种情形。

Conclusion: 无界区域中 curl curl 的本质谱由几何形状与截面结构（含孔洞收缩速率）共同决定，可呈现空集、半轴或带谱隙的区间等多种形态。

Abstract: We analyse the essential spectrum of ${\mathcal M} = {\operatorname{curl}} {\operatorname{curl}}$ acting on divergence-free vector fields in unbounded domains of $\mathbb{R}^3$. We show that $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [0,+\infty)$ in quasi-conical domains and $σ_e({\mathcal M}) \neq \emptyset$ in quasi-cylindrical domains. For horn-shaped domains with circular cross-section and eventually mean-convex boundary, we establish that $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = \emptyset$, independently of their volume. For horns with annular cross-section, the transverse normal harmonic field determines an effective one-dimensional Schrödinger operator $H_V$ with $σ_e({\mathcal M}) \supseteq σ_e(H_V)$. Finally, for a concrete family of perforated exponential horns with a double-exponential hole, we show that depending on the rate of shrinking of the hole at infinity, either $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = \emptyset$, or $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [γ^2, +\infty)$, or $σ_e({\mathcal M}) = [0,+\infty)$.

</details>


<div id='math.NT'></div>

# math.NT [[Back]](#toc)

### [114] [Asymptotic counting of integers with prime factors $p_{r^a s^b}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19434)
*Mehdi Golafshan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 计数所有素因子形如 $p_{r^a s^b}$ 的整数，给出相对误差 $o(1)$ 的显式渐近公式，并解决序数计数问题的一个特例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在推广并深化对具有特殊素因子结构整数的计数问题，特别是与序数 $\omega^{\omega^2}$ 以下序数的素数编码相对应的情形，从而回答 Vernaeve、Vindas 和 Weiermann 提出的计数问题的一个未决情形。

Method: 设固定且乘法独立的整数 $r,s\ge2$，考虑素因子均形如 $p_{r^a s^b}$（$a,b\ge0$）的整数，对不超过 $x$ 的这类整数进行计数，并推导相对误差为 $o(1)$ 且常数显式的渐近公式。

Result: 得到了该计数的渐近公式，相对误差为 $o(1)$，且对乘法常数是显式的；对 $(r,s)=(2,3)$，这些整数正是 $\omega^{\omega^2}$ 以下序数的素数编码，因此公式确定了该情形下的计数结果。

Conclusion: 该渐近公式不仅给出一般 $r,s$ 情形下的显式计数结果，还解决了 Vernaeve–Vindas–Weiermann 计数问题中 $(r,s)=(2,3)$ 的关键特例。

Abstract: Let $p_n$ be the $n$th prime, and let $r,s\ge2$ be fixed multiplicatively independent integers. We count the integers up to $x$ whose prime factors all have the form $p_{r^a s^b}$ with integers $a,b\ge0$. Our asymptotic formula for this count has relative error $o(1)$ and is explicit down to the multiplicative constant. For $(r,s)=(2,3)$ these integers are the prime codes of the ordinals below $ω^{ω^2}$, so the formula settles that case of the counting problem of Vernaeve, Vindas and Weiermann.

</details>


### [115] [Endoscopic description of the local Langlands correspondence for $\mathrm{G}_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19439)
*Yugo Takanashi*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明 p-进 G2 的 Gan-Savin L-包满足内视特征恒等式，并给出相关的扭曲内视特征恒等式与全局重数公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gan 与 Savin 已构造 p-进 G2 的 L-包，但其内视特征恒等式（内视性）尚未验证，这正是 Langlands 内视理论中的核心问题；同时 G2 经由 triality 到 PGSO8 的函子性提升的扭曲内视特征恒等式也亟待建立。

Method: 在 p-进局部域上，对 Gan-Savin 构造的 L-包计算其稳定分布与内视转移，验证内视特征恒等式；并通过对偶群与 triality 结构，研究 G2 到 PGSO8（带 triality）的函子性提升所对应的扭曲内视特征恒等式；进而结合局部结果与迹公式式的全局论证推导全局重数公式。

Result: 证明了 p-进 G2 的 Gan-Savin L-包满足内视特征恒等式；同时证明了从 G2 到带 triality 的 PGSO8 的函子性提升的扭曲内视特征恒等式；作为副产品，得到 G2 来自 PGL3 尖点自守谱的离散自守谱的全局重数公式。

Conclusion: Gan-Savin 的 p-进 G2 L-包具有内视性，其证明自然涵盖 到 PGSO8 的 triality 函子性提升的扭曲内视特征恒等式，并由此导出 G2 与 PGL3 相关的离散自守谱的全局重数公式，从而在内视理论框架下刻画了 G2 的自守表示。

Abstract: We prove that the $L$-packets for $p$-adic $\mathrm{G}_2$ constructed by Gan and Savin satisfy the endoscopic character identities. In the course of the proof, we also prove twisted endoscopic character identities for functorial lifts from $\mathrm{G}_2$ to $\mathrm{PGSO}_8$ with triality. As a byproduct, we also prove a global multiplicity formula for the discrete automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{G}_2$ coming from the cuspidal automorphic spectrum of $\mathrm{PGL}_3$.

</details>


### [116] [A uniform proof approach for congruences modulo 3 for partitions with $k$-colored odd parts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19440)
*Marie Jameson,James A. Sellers*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文为函数 $a_k(n)$（偶数部分单色、奇数部分有 $k$ 种颜色的分拆数）建立了研究模 3 同余的统一框架，并由此证明了一个无穷族（的无穷族）非嵌套模 3 同余式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $a_k(n)$ 推广了经典分拆函数，且满足大量同余式，因而备受关注。但已有关于 $a_k(n)$ 同余式的研究多是在等差数列上以较为零散（ad hoc）的方式得到结果，缺乏统一处理手段。

Method: 作者构建了一个统一的框架来系统研究 $a_k(n)$ 模 3 的同余性质，而非逐个特殊情形处理。

Result: 借助该框架，证明了无限多个无限族的非嵌套模 3 同余式（an infinite family of infinite families of non-nested congruences modulo 3）。

Conclusion: 该工作为 $a_k(n)$ 的模 3 同余研究提供了统一方法，并系统性地产生了大批非嵌套同余式，深化了对该类分拆函数算术性质的理解。

Abstract: In recent work, Hirschhorn and the second author defined $a_k(n)$ to be the number of partitions of $n$ wherein the even parts come in only one color, while the odd parts may be ``colored'' with one of $k$ colors for fixed $k\geq 1$. This function generalizes the classical partition function and has been of significant interest because it satisfies a number of congruences. Although prior work studying $a_k(n)$ has resulted in congruences in arithmetic progressions in a somewhat ad hoc manner, this work gives a uniform framework for studying congruences modulo 3. This allows us to prove an infinite family of infinite families of non-nested congruences modulo 3.

</details>


### [117] [A new family of maximal curves not covered by the Hermitian curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19546)
*Liming Ma,Yipeng Wang*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 构造了新的 F_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线（亏格 (q^2-1)q^n/2），并证明其不被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖；同时给出 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的 Kummer 模型、亏格与极大性统一证明，扩展了非覆盖性结果，并显式给出一个自同构子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有限域上极大曲线的构造及其与 Hermitian 曲线覆盖关系，是有限域代数几何与编码理论中的核心问题；作者希望推广 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线相关工作，构造更一般的极大曲线并判定其是否被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖。

Method: 对任意素数幂 q>2 与偶数 n≥4，显式给出定义方程构造 F_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线；给出 n 为奇数时 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的 Kummer 模型；通过计算有理除子（rational places）数量，对奇偶 n 统一证明极大性并计算亏格；利用覆盖/Galois 覆盖相关论证证明非覆盖性；并显式构造一个阶为 (q^n+1)q(q^2-1) 的自同构子群。

Result: 构造出亏格为 (q^2-1)q^n/2 的 F_{q^{2n}}-极大曲线，且该曲线不被 F_{q^{2n}} 上的 Hermitian 曲线覆盖；给出奇 n≥3 时 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的 Kummer 模型；对奇偶 n 均计算了亏格并统一证明极大性；对 q>2 与奇 n≥5，证明 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线不被 Hermitian 曲线覆盖，推广了 Galois 覆盖的已知结果；并给出阶为 (q^n+1)q(q^2-1) 的显式自同构子群。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了新的极大曲线族及其覆盖性质判定，将 Beelen–Montanucci 曲线的相关结果从 Galois 覆盖情形扩展到更一般情形，并通过显式自同构子群揭示其结构，对有限域极大曲线分类与编码应用具有意义。

Abstract: For every prime power $q>2$ and every even integer $n\ge4$, we construct an $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$-maximal curve of genus $(q^2-1)q^n/2$ that is not covered by the Hermitian curve over $\mathbb{F}_{q^{2n}}$. The defining equation also gives a Kummer model for the Beelen--Montanucci curves when $n\ge3$ is odd. We compute the genus for both odd and even $n$ and give a uniform proof of maximality by counting rational places. For $q>2$ and odd $n\ge5$, we also prove that the Beelen--Montanucci curves are not covered by the Hermitian curve, extending the known result for Galois coverings. For every prime power $q$ and $n\ge4$, we also give an explicit automorphism subgroup of order $(q^n+1)q(q^2-1)$.

</details>


### [118] [Simultaneous nonvanishing of quadratic twists via Rankin-Cohen brackets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19649)
*Ramin Takloo-Bighash*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明了迹化的对角Rankin--Cohen括积在权2ℓ水平1尖形式空间中的线性无关性，并由此推出满足中心L值非零条件的Hecke特征形式数量下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究权K水平1的Hecke特征形式的中心L值非零问题（非零中心L值/非消失现象），并将该问题与Rankin--Cohen括积的构造及Petersson公式联系起来，以给出非消失特征形式数量的下界。

Method: 取奇的根本判别式D（允许D=1），固定r≥1。考虑权k、水平|D|、nebentypus χ_D的Eisenstein级数G_{k,D}，构造前r个迹化的对角Rankin--Cohen括积 Tr_1^{|D|}[G_{ℓ-2e,D},G_{ℓ-2e,D}]_{2e}（1≤e≤r），并证明其在S_{2ℓ}(SL_2(Z))中线性无关；再结合Kayath--Lane--Neifeld--Ni--Xue的Petersson公式。

Result: 对充分大的ℓ且满足(-1)^ℓ D>0，前r个迹化对角Rankin--Cohen括积在S_{2ℓ}(SL_2(Z))中线性无关。由此得到：至少r个归一化Hecke特征形式f∈S_{2ℓ}(SL_2(Z))满足L(f⊗χ_D,ℓ)≠0。当D=1时，对每个固定r与充分大且K≡0 (mod 4)的K，至少存在r个权K的水平1特征形式具有非零中心值。

Conclusion: 建立了一类迹化对角Rankin--Cohen括积的线性无关性，并由Petersson公式导出中心L值非零的Hecke特征形式数量的下界，特别地在水平1情形给出了权K≡0 mod 4时非零中心值特征形式的至少r个存在性。

Abstract: Let $D$ be an odd fundamental discriminant, with $D=1$ permitted, and let $r\geq 1$ be fixed. We prove that, for every sufficiently large integer $\ell$ satisfying $(-1)^\ell D>0$, the first $r$ traced diagonal Rankin--Cohen brackets \[
  \mathrm{Tr}_1^{|D|}[G_{\ell-2e,D},G_{\ell-2e,D}]_{2e},
  \qquad 1\leq e\leq r, \] are linearly independent in $S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$. Here $G_{k,D}$ is the Eisenstein series of weight $k$, level $|D|$, and nebentypus $χ_D$. The Petersson formula of Kayath, Lane, Neifeld, Ni, and Xue then implies that at least $r$ normalized Hecke eigenforms $f\in S_{2\ell}(SL_2(\mathbb Z))$ satisfy $L(f\otimesχ_D,\ell)\neq 0$. For $D=1$, this gives, for every fixed $r$ and every sufficiently large $K\equiv 0\pmod 4$, at least $r$ level-one eigenforms of weight $K$ with nonzero central value.

</details>


### [119] [Finite subgroups of outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19696)
*Yu Nishio*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文从单远阿贝尔几何视角研究混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的有限子群，引入"拟几何子群"概念，并证明存在非拟几何的有限子群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由混合特征局部域自同构群到其绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的自然单射给出的像是典型的拟几何子群；作者希望澄清外自同构群中哪些有限子群能由此几何方式产生。

Method: 采用单远阿贝尔几何（mono-anabelian geometry）的框架，将混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的有限子群定义为拟几何子群（若其在外自同构群中的原像同构于某混合特征局部域的绝对伽罗瓦群），并构造反例。

Result: 证明了混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群的外自同构群存在非拟几何的有限子群。

Conclusion: 混合特征局部域绝对伽罗瓦群外自同构群的拟几何子群集合一般不能等同于其全部有限子群的集合，即拟几何性并非仅由群论结构刻画。

Abstract: In the present paper, we study finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields from the point of view of mono-anabelian geometry. We shall refer to a finite subgroup of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field as quasi-geometric if its inverse image in the automorphism group of the corresponding absolute Galois group is isomorphic to the absolute Galois group of some mixed-characteristic local field. It is well-known that the image of the natural injective homomorphism from the automorphism group of a mixed-characteristic local field to the outer automorphism group of the associated absolute Galois group yields a typical example of a quasi-geometric subgroup. The main result of the present paper asserts the existence of non-quasi-geometric finite subgroups of the outer automorphism groups of the absolute Galois groups of mixed-characteristic local fields. This result shows that the set of quasi-geometric subgroups of the outer automorphism group of the absolute Galois group of a mixed-characteristic local field cannot be characterized, in general, as the set of all finite subgroups of the outer automorphism group.

</details>


### [120] [Norms of Schneider--Teitelbaum Polynomials in $p$-adic Fourier Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20002)
*Yu Katagiri*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文显式确定了Schneider–Teitelbaum p进Fourier理论中多项式 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部 $F$ 解析函数Banach空间上的范数，并给出了Bannai–Kobayashi基的显式归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schneider–Teitelbaum的p进Fourier理论中的多项式 $P_n$ 是构造局部解析函数Banach空间标准正交基的核心对象。然而，这些函数在给定范数下的具体大小此前并不明确，尤其缺乏显式的范数公式，这限制了对Bannai–Kobayashi所构造基的规范化理解。

Method: 设 $F$ 为 $\mathbb{Q}_p$ 的非分歧二次扩域，$\Omega$ 为Lubin–Tate周期。利用Ardakov–Berger关于值 $P_n(\Omega)$ 的最新赋值公式，计算 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在固定阶局部 $F$ 解析函数Banach空间中的范数。

Result: 显式给出了 $P_n(x\Omega)$ 在各固定阶局部 $F$ 解析函数Banach空间上的范数公式。

Conclusion: 基于所得范数计算，得到了Bannai–Kobayashi所构造基的显式归一化，使该基的每个元素范数均为1。

Abstract: Let $F$ be the unramified quadratic extension of $\mathbb{Q}_p$, and let $P_n(T)$ denote the polynomials arising in the $p$-adic Fourier theory of Schneider and Teitelbaum. We determine explicitly the norms of the functions $P_n(xΩ)$ on the Banach spaces of locally $F$-analytic functions of fixed order, where $Ω$ is a Lubin--Tate period. Our computation is based on a recent valuation formula of Ardakov and Berger for the values $P_n(Ω)$. As an application, we obtain an explicit normalization of the basis constructed by Bannai and Kobayashi, whose elements all have norm one.

</details>


### [121] [Sampling and Moments of Reciprocal Quadratic Mellin Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20201)
*K. Srinivasa Raghava*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 研究倒数二次有理函数相关的对数Mellin积分，给出生成函数的代数性、极小四次方程、微分方程、递推、系数渐近、全正性与矩问题结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画带代数系数的倒数二次有理核的对数Mellin积分及其正整数采样生成函数，揭示其代数性与解析结构，并建立与Hausdorff矩、全正性及鞍点渐近的联系。

Method: 利用Mellin变换、整函数形变、鞍点分析（鞍点区域 c>1，-2√c<b<0）、极小微分方程与多项式递推、双项对角渐近，以及对数矩与采样半径的统一极大值控制。

Result: 当核具代数系数时，采样生成函数除以π后对任意 0<|a|<1（a∈Q）为代数函数；确定主半权例的极小四次方程、完整有限分支轨迹、极小微分方程、递推与系数渐近；在鞍点区域给出正双曲公式，导出确定Hausdorff矩序列、严格全正性与单调商极限；一般系数渐近表明单一极大值同时控制采样半径与高阶对数矩；固定临界轨迹上双项对角渐近的归一化乘积趋于π，除以修正 1+γ/M 后达 O(M^{-2}) 近似。

Conclusion: 对数Mellin积分在代数核下具有代数生成函数，其渐近、全正性与矩性质可由单一鞍点极大值统一描述；固定临界轨迹上的修正公式提供了高精度π近似。

Abstract: We study logarithmic Mellin integrals attached to a reciprocal quadratic rational function. When the rational kernel has algebraic coefficients, positive-integer samples of an entire deformation have a generating function which, after division by $π$, is algebraic for every $a\in\mathbb Q$ with $0<\vert{}a\vert{}<1$. For the principal half-weight example we determine the minimal quartic, complete finite branch locus, minimal differential equation, polynomial recurrence, and coefficient asymptotics. Throughout the saddle region $c>1$, $-2\sqrt c<b<0$, positive hyperbolic formulas for real $0<a<1$ give determinate Hausdorff moment sequences, strict total positivity, and monotone quotient limits. For real $\vert{}a\vert{}<1$, a general coefficient asymptotic in the same region shows that one maximum controls both the sampling radius and the high logarithmic moments. On the fixed-critical locus, two-term diagonal asymptotics give a normalized product tending to $π$; division by the displayed correction $1+γ/M$ gives an $O(M^{-2})$ approximation.

</details>


### [122] [On a question of Ruzsa about densities of sumsets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20206)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文否定了Ruzsa提出的一个关于集合的和集与渐近密度之间不等式关系的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Ruzsa曾提出是否存在常数使得和集A+A的下渐近密度可由其渐近密度与上渐近密度的某种插值不等式控制，本文旨在检验该问题的成立性。

Method: 在自然数集上构造反例：对于任意0<ν<1和c>0，构造具有渐近密度的集合A，使得上述不等式不成立。

Result: 证明了不存在常数0<ν<1与c>0使得对一切具有渐近密度的集合A，d_*(A+A) ≥ c (d^*(A+A))^{1-ν} d(A)^ν恒成立。

Conclusion: Ruzsa关于该密度插值不等式的猜想被否定，此类控制关系在和集的下渐近密度意义下不成立。

Abstract: Let $\mathsf{d}$, $\mathsf{d}^{\ast}$, and $\mathsf{d}_{\ast}$ denote, respectively, the asymptotic density, the upper asymptotic density, and the lower asymptotic density on $\mathbb N$. We show that there are no constants $0<ν<1$ and $c>0$ such that $$ \mathsf{d}_{\ast}(A+A)\ge c\bigl(\mathsf{d}^{\ast}(A+A)\bigr)^{1-ν}\mathsf{d}(A)^ν $$ for every set $A\subseteq \mathbb{N}$ which admits asymptotic density. This answers in the negative a question of I.Z. Ruzsa.

</details>


### [123] [A characterization of Sophie Germain primes - II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20208)
*Paolo Leonetti*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 偶数 $n$ 下，集合 $\{1^{\sigma(1)},\dots,n^{\sigma(n)}\}$ 能通过某个置换构成模 $n$ 的完全剩余系的充要条件是 $n/2$ 为素数且 $p-1$ 无平方因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献对奇数情形给出了类似刻画（Int. J. Number Theory 14 (2018), 653–660），本文旨在补全偶数情形，完善关于幂次置换构成完全剩余系问题的完整分类。

Method: 设 $n\ge4$ 为偶数，考察是否存在置换 $\sigma$ 使得 $\{1^{\sigma(1)},2^{\sigma(2)},\ldots,n^{\sigma(n)}\}$ 构成模 $n$ 的完全剩余系。通过数论分析（涉及素数分解、平方因子性质与置换构造）证明其充要条件为 $p=n/2$ 为素数且 $p-1$ 无平方因子。

Result: 证明了偶数 $n$ 情形的充要条件：存在这样的置换 $\sigma$ 当且仅当 $p=n/2$ 是素数且 $p-1$ 无平方因子。

Conclusion: 该结果与已有奇数情形结论互补，给出了幂次置换构成模 $n$ 完全剩余系问题的完整刻画。

Abstract: Let $n\ge 4$ be an even integer. We show that $\{1^{σ(1)},2^{σ(2)},\ldots,n^{σ(n)}\}$ is a complete residue system modulo $n$ for some permutation $σ$ of $\{1,2,\ldots,n\}$ if and only if $p:=n/2$ is prime and $p-1$ is squarefree. This complements the results in [Int. J. Number Theory \textbf{14} (2018), 653--660], where an analogue characterization has been proved for odd integers.

</details>


### [124] [The Three Gates: A Rooted-Operator Approach to Weil Positivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20367)
*Marco Desogus*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过三阶段（Gate I/II/III）局域化根算子论证，在实奇数对数信道中证明Weil正性，从而导出Riemann假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在实奇数对数信道中建立Weil正性，需要保留极点的rank-one项并给出严格的符号与能量控制，以推进到Riemann假设的证明。

Method: Gate I给出严格cellular/shell符号定理与区间算术界；Gate II综合Mellin单位胞分解、除数部分等距平方、仿射平移、全形Cauchy–Carleman输运、多源相干shorting、增广标量根与真实度量下的Schur几何，将继承父响应放入共同的post-old-core/common-cut原始形式；Gate III使用压缩、闭包与限定奇数Weil判据。核心等式为 P^{ex}_{k,m} a_m = ω^{ex}_{k,m}，并结合外舍入有限计算与解析尾项。

Result: 得到 g^{M+}_k = 1/2 + log k − (439/(250 log 2)) Σ_m V_{k,m} − (5/2) W_k > 0 对 k ≥ 7 成立；共同cut定理分离父基与横向预算；fold恒等式留下非负余项。

Conclusion: 以严格认证端点 Y=7 为起点，Schur归纳给出整数端点正性，零延拓给出任意有限支撑半径非负性，闭包结合限定奇数Weil判据得到Riemann假设。

Abstract: We present a localized rooted-operator argument for Weil positivity in the real odd logarithmic channel, retaining the polar rank-one term throughout. Gate I establishes a strict cellular/shell sign theorem with rigorous interval-arithmetic bounds. Gate II combines the Mellin unit-cell decomposition, divisor partial-isometry squares, affine translations, full-form Cauchy--Carleman transport, coherent multi-source shorting, the augmented scalar root, and Schur geometry in the true metric. Gate III uses compression, closure, and the restricted odd Weil criterion. A central point in Gate II is to place the inherited parent response in the same post-old-core/common-cut primal form in which the arithmetic mismatch and folded scalar debit are charged. If $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$ is the surviving inherited pivot and $a_m$ the inherited coordinate of the Schur minimizer, then $P^{\rm ex}_{k,m}a_m=ω^{\rm ex}_{k,m}$, so the inherited source-coupling contribution is the negative metric energy of that response. The aligned forcing is retained explicitly, and an outward-rounded finite computation together with the analytic tail gives $g^{M+}_k=\frac12+\log k-\frac{439}{250\log 2}\sum_m V_{k,m}-\frac52 W_k>0$ for $k\ge7$. The simultaneous common-cut theorem keeps the parent-ground and transverse budgets separate before the common infimum, while the fold identity leaves a non-negative remainder. Starting from the rigorously certified endpoint $Y=7$, Schur induction yields positivity at the integer endpoints, zero-extension gives non-negativity at every finite support radius, and closure together with the restricted odd Weil criterion yields the Riemann hypothesis. Short fantasy interludes provide only an expository map and may be omitted without changing any definition, lemma, theorem, or proof.

</details>


### [125] [A problem of Yang and Chen on weighted representation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20385)
*Shuang-Shuang Li,Ya-Ting Xu,Xiao-Hui Yan*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明了 $f_k(t)$ 的渐近阶为 $2^t/t^{k/2}$，从而否定了 Yang 和 Chen 关于 $f_k(t)$ 与 $f_l(t)$ 最终相等的猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yang 和 Chen 提出了一个加法数论中的问题：对于任意整数 $k,l>1$，满足 $R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(\mathbb N\setminus A,n)$（对所有 $n\ge t$）的集合 $A$ 的数量 $f_k(t)$ 与 $f_l(t)$ 是否最终相等。该问题涉及表示函数与集合划分的对称性，具有组合与数论意义。

Method: 论文通过研究 $R_{1,k}(A,n)$ 的表示性质，对 $f_k(t)$ 进行计数。核心是建立 $f_k(t)$ 的渐近估计，利用组合计数与生成函数或递推关系，分析满足对称条件的集合 $A$ 在 $n\ge t$ 时的约束，从而得到 $f_k(t)$ 的精确渐近阶。

Result: 论文证明了 $f_k(t)\asymp_k \frac{2^t}{t^{k/2}}$，即 $f_k(t)$ 的渐近增长由 $2^t$ 与 $t^{-k/2}$ 共同决定。

Conclusion: 由于 $f_k(t)$ 的渐近阶依赖于 $k$（体现在 $t^{-k/2}$ 因子），当 $k\neq l$ 时 $f_k(t)$ 与 $f_l(t)$ 不会最终相等，因此该结果给出了 Yang 和 Chen 问题的否定回答。

Abstract: Let $\mathbb N$ denote the set of nonnegative integers. For an integer $k>1$ and a set $A\subseteq\mathbb N$, let $R_{1,k}(A,n)$ denote the number of solutions of $n=a_1+ka_2$ with $a_1,a_2\in A$. For integers $k>1$ and $t\ge1$, Yang and Chen defined $f_k(t)$ to be the number of sets $A\subseteq\mathbb N$ for which \[ R_{1,k}(A,n)=R_{1,k}(\mathbb N\setminus A,n) \] for all integers $n\ge t$, and asked whether $f_k(t)$ and $f_l(t)$ are eventually equal for any integers $k,l>1$. We prove that \[ f_k(t)\asymp_k \frac{2^t}{t^{k/2}}, \] which gives a negative answer to their problem.

</details>


### [126] [Low moments of automorphic random multiplicative function sums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20460)
*Sun-Kai Leung*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文确定了有界度数随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩的阶，推广了Harper的经典结果，适用于酉或辛型的不可约紧群表示及高秩Sato-Tate模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harper关于随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩的经典工作局限于特定情形，本文旨在将其推广到更广泛的有界度数Euler乘积族，覆盖更高秩的Sato-Tate模型。

Method: 在与有界度数Euler乘积相联系的随机乘性函数上，对其酉局部参数施加"素数平方抵消条件"，据此确定部分和低阶矩的阶。

Result: 在素数平方抵消条件下确定了低阶矩的阶，结果适用于酉型或辛型的不可约紧群表示，包括高秩Sato-Tate模型SU(d)与奇对称幂模型Sym^m SU(2)。

Conclusion: 该工作扩展了Harper的结果，建立了适用于更广泛群表示族的随机乘性函数部分和低阶矩理论。

Abstract: We determine the order of the low moments of partial sums of random multiplicative functions associated with Euler products of bounded degree whose unitary local parameters satisfy a prime-square cancellation condition, thereby generalizing Harper's seminal work. The result applies to irreducible compact-group representations of unitary or symplectic type, including the higher-rank Sato--Tate model $SU(d)$ and the odd symmetric-power Sato--Tate model $\operatorname{Sym}^{m}SU(2)$.

</details>


### [127] [Finding New Limit Points of Mahler Measure by Methods of Missing Data Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20468)
*Jean-Marc Sac-Épée,Souad El Otmani,Armand Maul,Georges Rhin*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文通过引入缺失数据恢复方法，进一步扩展了单变量多项式Mahler测度的极限点列表。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单变量多项式Mahler测度的极限点列表此前由Boyd和Mossinghoff建立，并通过遗传算法等方法得到扩展；作者希望进一步扩充已知极限点集合。

Method: 采用一种缺失数据恢复（missing data restoration）的方法来扩展已知极限点列表。

Result: 成功扩展了已知的Mahler测度极限点列表。

Conclusion: 缺失数据恢复方法可有效用于发现新的Mahler测度极限点，为后续研究提供更丰富的极限点集合。

Abstract: It is well known that the set of Mahler measures of single variable polynomial has limit points of which a list established by D. Boyd and M. Mossinghoff has been extended through approaches based on genetic algorithms. In this paper, we wish to further extend the list of known limit points by adapting a method of missing data restoration.

</details>


### [128] [Computational results on sums of a prime with squares or cubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20505)
*Kenny Applegate,Kyle Pratt*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文证明：所有满足 14<n<10^26 的整数均可表示为一个奇素数加两个正平方数之和；并证明 308 到 10^16 之间的整数均可表示为一个奇素数加两个正立方数之和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于"整数表示为奇素数加两个正平方数"的猜想此前缺乏大范围验证；作者希望在该问题上给出迄今最大范围的数值验证，并顺带研究素数加一个平方数的表示，以作为进一步"自举"的工具。

Method: 先研究"素数 + 一个平方数"的表示，再通过"自举"（bootstrap）推广到"素数 + 两个正平方数"；对"素数 + 两个正立方数"的处理方式类似。方法上需要确定若干椭圆曲线上的整点，以排除或覆盖可能的例外情形。

Result: 证明了所有 14<n<10^26 的整数都是奇素数加两个正平方数之和；并证明了所有 308 到 10^16 之间的整数都是奇素数加两个正立方数之和。

Conclusion: 该工作将相关猜想的验证范围大幅推进，方法核心在于椭圆曲线整点计算与从"一个平方数"到"两个平方数"的自举论证。

Abstract: One conjectures that all integers greater than 14 are the sum of an odd prime and two positive squares. We prove that all integers $n$ with $14<n<10^{26}$ can be represented in this manner. Along the way, we investigate sums of a prime and one square and then ``bootstrap'' to get our results on sums of a prime and two squares. We also show that all integers between $308$ and $10^{16}$ can be represented as the sum of an odd prime and two positive cubes. Our methods require the determination of the integral points on various elliptic curves.

</details>


### [129] [Rogers--Ramanujan identities from the geometry of $X^a=Y^b$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20567)
*Yifeng Huang,Kenny Lau,Ken Ono*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 证明 Huang–Jiang–Oblomkov (HJO) 猜想，将 Rogers–Ramanujan 与 Andrews–Gordon 恒等式几何推广至 torus-knot 奇点 $X^a=Y^b$（$1<a<b$，互素）。建立三重重等式：正规化点计数、HJO $q$-级数 $Z_{a,b}$ 与显式无穷乘积 $P_{a,b}$ 相等。核心是更强有限恒等式：秩 $N$ 的 HJO 和等于 $(q;q)_N$ 乘以有界（上界 $N$）平衡柱面分割的生成函数；取 $N\to\infty$ 得猜想。证明结合 Bergeron–Garsia–Leven–Xin 与 Mellit 的组合有理 shuffle 定理、斜率算子乘性定理及有界柱面分割的行列式模型，通过共同 $q$-差分方程联系。有限恒等式与猜想已在 Lean 中由 AxiomProver 形式化，条件依赖两个文献输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Huang–Jiang–Oblomkov 猜想旨在为 Rogers–Ramanujan 与 Andrews–Gordon 恒等式提供几何/代数几何的推广，把经典分割恒等式与 torus-knot 奇点的几何计数联系起来。该猜想在点计数、$q$-级数与无穷乘积三种表述间建立统一框架，具有组合、表示论与奇点理论的交叉意义，但此前缺乏完整证明。

Method: 1) 定义 $\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)$ 为 $\mathbb F_q$ 上满足 $A^a=B^b$ 的交换幂零矩阵对；2) 对 $\prod_{m\ge1}(1-q^{-m})\sum_n |\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}|/|\mathrm{GL}_n|$、HJO $q$-级数 $Z_{a,b}(q^{-1})$ 与无穷乘积 $P_{a,b}(q^{-1})$ 建立三重重等式；3) 证明更强有限恒等式：秩 $N$ 的 HJO 和 $=(q;q)_N\times$ 有界平衡柱面分割生成函数；4) 综合使用 Bergeron–Garsia–Leven–Xin 与 Mellit 的组合有理 shuffle 定理、斜率算子的乘性定理、有界柱面分割的行列式模型，并借助共同 $q$-差分方程将其联结；5) 令 $N\to\infty$ 推出 HJO 猜想；6) 在 Lean 中经 AxiomProver 对有限恒等式与猜想进行形式化（条件性依赖两个文献输入）。

Result: 对任意互素 $1<a<b$，证明了 HJO 猜想：正规化点计数、HJO $q$-级数 $Z_{a,b}$ 与显式无穷乘积 $P_{a,b}$ 三者相等。进一步得到更强的有限恒等式：秩 $N$ 的 HJO 和等于 $(q;q)_N$ 乘以有界平衡柱面分割的生成函数，取极限即得原猜想。相应结果已在 Lean 中形式化（条件于两个文献输入）。

Conclusion: 该工作完整证明了 HJO 猜想，建立起 torus-knot 奇点的点计数、HJO $q$-级数与显式无穷乘积之间的三重重等式，可视作 Rogers–Ramanujan 与 Andrews–Gordon 恒等式的几何推广。其更强有限恒等式及柱面分割联系为后续研究提供统一框架，并通过 Lean 形式化提升了结果的可靠性。

Abstract: We prove the conjecture of Huang, Jiang, and Oblomkov (HJO) giving a geometric extension of the Rogers--Ramanujan and Andrews--Gordon identities for every torus-knot singularity $X^a=Y^b$ with coprime $1<a<b.$ For a prime power $q$, let $\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)$ denote the set of pairs of commuting nilpotent $n\times n$ matrices $(A,B)$ over $\mathbb F_q$ satisfying $A^a=B^b$. We establish the threefold equality between their normalized counts, the HJO $q$-series $Z_{a,b}$, and the explicit infinite product $P_{a,b}$: \[ \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|} \prod_{m\geq1}(1-q^{-m}) \Biggl(\sum_{n=0}^{\infty} \frac{\lvert\mathcal{NC}_n^{a,b}(\mathbb F_q)\rvert} {\lvert\operatorname{GL}_n(\mathbb F_q)\rvert}\Biggr) }_{\text{point count}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}Z_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{\(q\)-series}} = \underbrace{\vphantom{\Bigg|}P_{a,b}(q^{-1}) }_{\text{infinite product}}. \] Our main result is a stronger finite identity: the rank $N$ HJO sum equals $(q;q)_N$ times the generating function for balanced cylindric partitions with entries bounded by $N$. Taking $N\to\infty$ yields the HJO conjecture. The proof combines the compositional rational shuffle theorem of Bergeron--Garsia--Leven--Xin and Mellit with a multiplicativity theorem for slope operators and a determinantal model for bounded cylindric partitions, linked by a common $q$-difference equation. The finite identity and the HJO conjecture have been formalized in Lean by AxiomProver, conditional on two stated literature inputs.

</details>


### [130] [An ergodic approach to equations of the form $x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20727)
*Vitaly Bergelson,Hao Pan,Saúl Rodríguez Martín*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 本文引入遍历方法研究形如 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 的加性方程解的存在性，完整刻画了使划分问题与密度问题恒可解的多项式增长Hardy域函数，并推广到双集合、线性组合及素数子集情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受Erdős、Sárközy、Sós (1989) 与 Khalfalah、Szemerédi (2006) 的问题与结果启发，需系统处理 x+y=⌊α(n)⌋ 这类加性方程的解存在性，包括密度问题与有限划分问题。

Method: 采用遍历（ergodic）方法，针对充分正则的函数α（如多项式或多项式增长的logarithmico-exponential函数）及正上密度的集合A或有限划分的单元进行刻画。

Result: 完整刻画了多项式增长的Hardy域函数中使划分问题与密度问题恒可解的那些函数；遍历方法还可处理 A1、A2 均为正密度集合时求 x∈A1, y∈A2 的更一般问题，进而处理 kx+ly=⌊α(n)⌋；部分结果对具有正相对上密度的素数子集亦成立。

Conclusion: 遍历方法为这类加性方程问题提供了统一框架，并给出多项式增长Hardy域函数的完整可解性刻画。

Abstract: Motivated by questions and results of Erdős, Sárközy and Sós (1989), and Khalfalah and Szemerédi (2006), we introduce an ergodic approach to additive problems concerning solutions of equations of the form \[ x+y=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor,\quad x,y\in A,n\in\mathbb{N} \] where $α(t)$ is a sufficiently regular function, such as a polynomial or a logarithmico-exponential function of polynomial growth (e.g. $α(t)=t^{5/2}+t^2\log t$), and $A$ is either a given set of positive upper density, or a cell of a prescribed finite partition of $\mathbb{N}$. We give a complete description of the Hardy field functions of polynomial growth for which these partition and density problems are always solvable.
  The ergodic approach also applies to the more general problem of finding $x\in A_1,y\in A_2$, where $A_1,A_2$ are two given sets of positive density. This in turn allows us to treat equations of the form $kx+ly=\lfloorα(n)\rfloor$, where $k,l\in\mathbb{Z}$.
  Some of our results also hold true when $A$ is a subset of the primes with positive relative upper density.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [131] [Bounded curvature manifolds without Euclidean isometric immersions of bounded mean curvature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19168)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对每个 n≥2，在 R^n 上构造曲率范数与单射半径一致有界的完备黎曼度量 G_n，使得任何到有限维欧氏空间的 C^2 等距浸入都无界平均曲率，从而在更强曲率假设下否定 Yau 问题 52。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Yau 问题 52 询问是否存在曲率与单射半径受控的完备度量，使其无法等距浸入有限维欧氏空间并保持有界平均曲率。此前对该问题的认识依赖于较弱的曲率控制，作者希望在"全曲率范数有界"这一更强假设下考察此问题，并给出否定回答。

Method: 在二维情形，将有界第二基本形式转化为共形因子的拉普拉斯算子的有限雅可比表示及其代价控制；构造互不相交的共形块，使曲率保持有界而有限雅可比表示的代价趋于无穷；再通过取欧氏乘积把结论推广到所有更高维 n≥3。

Result: 构造出的度量 G_n 满足全曲率范数 ≤1、单射半径 ≥1，但不存在具有有界平均曲率的 C^2 等距浸入到任何有限维欧氏空间；二维中有限雅可比表示代价无界，高维由乘积构造继承该性质。

Conclusion: Yau 问题 52 在全曲率范数有界的更强条件下仍是否定的；曲率与单射半径的一致控制不足以保证存在具有有界平均曲率的有限维等距浸入。

Abstract: For every integer $n\ge2$, we construct a smooth complete Riemannian metric $G_n$ on $\mathbb{R}^n$ with full curvature norm at most one and injectivity radius at least one for which no $C^2$ isometric immersion into a finite-dimensional Euclidean space has bounded mean curvature. In dimension two, bounded second fundamental form would give uniformly controlled finite Jacobian representations of the Laplacian of the conformal factor. We construct disjoint conformal blocks for which the curvature remains bounded while the finite Jacobian representation cost tends to infinity. Taking Euclidean products gives all higher dimensions. The result answers Yau's Problem~52 negatively under the stronger assumption of bounded full curvature.

</details>


### [132] [Umbilic slopes and cubic Weingarten surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19188)
*Haoxuan Cheng*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究脐点斜率与三次Weingarten曲面的全局分类：非恒定主曲率芽的唯一割线切线斜率只能取特定值（0、-1、∞、≥3的奇数或其倒数）；满足 κ₂=cκ₁³ 的紧致无边界 C⁴ 浸入曲面各连通分支必为旋转椭球面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望刻画光滑曲面在脐点附近主曲率的行为（脐点斜率问题），并据此对一类三次Weingarten曲面（κ₂=cκ₁³）给出全局分类，从而把局部脐点几何与全局曲面形状联系起来。

Method: 方法上，先在脐点处分类齐次Hessian恒等式，用于处理非恒定脐点芽；对光滑斜率问题用标量唯一延拓处理无穷阶接触；对三次分类则结合精确的四阶增长估计与角向能量估计。核心工具是"唯一割线切线"（secant tangent）分析，通过主曲率的连续标号建立平均曲率与高斯曲率之间的光滑正则关系。

Result: 主要结果有三：(1) 对欧氏三维空间中光滑曲面，具有唯一割线切线的非恒定主曲率芽，其斜率为0、-1、∞、不小于3的奇数或其倒数；(2) 在非恒定脐点芽处，若平均曲率与高斯曲率之间存在光滑正则关系，则经连续标号后可得到这样的切线，且有序曲率像至多有两个极限方向，均落在上述集合中；(3) 对固定实数c，满足 κ₂=cκ₁³（按某种顺序）的 C⁴ 紧致无边界浸入曲面，其每个连通分支都嵌入为旋转椭球面。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了脐点处主曲率芽可能的斜率取值，并证明了三次Weingarten曲面 κ₂=cκ₁³ 的紧致解仅为旋转椭球面，说明局部脐点约束对全局几何形状具有强限制作用。各论证都依赖于脐点处齐次Hessian恒等式的分类。

Abstract: We study umbilic slopes and the global classification of cubic Weingarten surfaces. For a smooth surface in Euclidean three-space, a nonconstant principal curvature germ with a unique secant tangent has slope zero, minus one, infinity, an odd integer at least three, or its reciprocal. At a nonconstant umbilic germ, a smooth regular relation between mean and Gaussian curvature supplies such a tangent after a continuous labeling of the principal curvatures; the ordered curvature image has at most two limiting directions, both in the same set. We also classify compact immersed surfaces without boundary of class $C^4$ satisfying $κ_2=cκ_1^3$ in some order at every point, for a fixed real $c$: each connected component is embedded as an ellipsoid of revolution. The arguments share a classification of homogeneous Hessian identities at an umbilic. Scalar unique continuation treats infinite-order contact in the smooth slope problem, whereas an exact fourth-order growth estimate and angular energy estimates yield the cubic classification.

</details>


### [133] [Mean curvature flows with cylindrical singularities II: stability and genericity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19390)
*Ao Sun,Jinxin Xue*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究非退化柱状奇点，证明其在小扰动下稳定，并可将退化奇点的平均曲率流扰动为产生非退化奇点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作[SX25]借助动力系统思想引入了非退化柱状奇点的概念，但该奇点的稳定性及与退化奇点之间的关系尚不明确。

Method: 在[SX25]所建立的非退化柱状奇点框架下，进一步分析其性质，通过扰动论证研究奇点在小扰动下的行为。

Result: 证明非退化柱状奇点在小扰动下是稳定的；并表明可通过扰动的办法，使带有退化奇点的平均曲率流产生非退化奇点。

Conclusion: 非退化柱状奇点具有良好的稳定性，且退化奇点可经扰动转化为非退化奇点，为平均曲率流奇点的分析提供了新途径。

Abstract: In [SX25], we introduced the notion of nondegenerate cylindrical singularity using the ideas from dynamical systems. In this paper, we further study the properties of these singularities, showing that they are stable under small perturbations. Furthermore, we show that we can perturb some mean curvature flow with degenerate singularities to produce nondegenerate singularities.

</details>


### [134] [Complete Self-Shrinkers in Arbitrary Codimension with Positive Constant Scalar Curvature and \(S\le 1\)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19464)
*Shunzi Guo*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明了余维数任意、数量曲率为正常数且第二基本形式模长平方S≤1的完备自收缩子只能为球面或广义柱面，S≡1，无新的高余维例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自收缩子是平均曲率流的自相似解，其分类是几何分析中的重要问题；高余维情形下分类结果较少，作者希望在R为正常数与S≤1的条件下给出刚性分类，排除新的高余维例子。

Method: 利用一个代数引理给出Bakry–Émery Ricci曲率的统一正下界，结合Wei–Wylie比较定理、Cheng–Zhou关于有限Gaussian体积与多项式体积增长的等价性，以及Cao–Li的gap定理进行证明。

Result: 证明S≡1，且X等距于球面S^n(√n)或标准广义柱面S^k(√k)×R^{n−k}（2≤k≤n−1）。

Conclusion: 在该数量曲率与第二基本形式假设下，高余维自收缩子不产生新例子，分类退化为经典的低余维结果。

Abstract: Let \(X:M^n\to\mathbb R^{n+p}\) be an \(n\)-dimensional complete self-shrinker in arbitrary codimension \(p\ge 1\). Suppose that the scalar curvature \(R\) is a positive constant and that the squared norm \(S\) of the second fundamental form satisfies \(S\le 1\).
