We prove that \(S\equiv 1\) and that \(X\) is isometric to either the round sphere \(S^n(\sqrt n)\) or the standard generalized cylinder \(S^k(\sqrt k)\times\mathbb R^{n-k}\), \(2\le k\le n-1\). In particular, no new higher-codimension examples occur under these assumptions. The proof uses an algebraic lemma which yields a uniform positive lower bound for the Bakry--Émery Ricci curvature, together with the comparison theorem of Wei--Wylie, the equivalence of finite Gaussian volume and polynomial volume growth due to Cheng--Zhou, and the gap theorem of Cao--Li.

</details>


### [135] [Infinitesimmaly homogeneous manifolds with symplectic and almost contact structure groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19568)
*Carlos Alberto Marín Arango,Alejandro Pinilla Barrera*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究具有指定结构群的无穷小齐性仿射流形：对辛群Sp(2n,R)证明对应结构恰为平坦无挠辛仿射结构；对U(n)×{1}（等价于殆接触度量数据）分类无挠特征张量，并通过不变量形变得到一般挠情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画给定结构群下的无穷小齐性仿射结构，并将其与已知几何（辛仿射几何、殆接触度量几何）建立对应与分类。

Method: 在无穷小齐性仿射框架下，针对不同结构群分析特征张量与挠率：(1) 对Sp(2n,R)直接确定无穷小齐性结构；(2) 对U(n)×{1}分类无挠特征张量，借不变量形变引入挠率，并用代数关系约束容许挠率与内挠率参数。

Result: 对Sp(2n,R)，无穷小齐性结构正好是平坦无挠辛仿射结构；对U(n)×{1}，容许挠率与内挠率参数由单一代数关系支配，水平分布为接触分布当且仅当参数某组合非零；并描述了相容联络，将无挠分支与trans-Sasakian几何相联系。

Conclusion: 给出了两类结构群下无穷小齐性仿射结构的完整刻画：辛情形归结为平坦无挠辛仿射结构；殆接触度量情形实现无挠分类与带挠形变，并揭示其与接触性及trans-Sasakian几何的联系。

Abstract: We study infinitesimally homogeneous affine manifolds with prescribed structure groups. First, for the real symplectic group $\operatorname{Sp}(2n,\mathbb R)$, we prove that the corresponding infinitesimally homogeneous structures are precisely flat torsion-free symplectic affine structures. Second, for \(\operatorname{U}(n)\times\{1\}\), whose \(G\)-structures are equivalent to almost contact metric data, we classify the torsion-free characteristic tensors and obtain the general torsional case by invariant deformation. The admissible torsion and inner-torsion parameters are governed by a single algebraic relation, and the horizontal distribution is contact precisely when a certain combination of these parameters does not vanish. We also describe the compatible connections and relate the torsion-free branches to trans-Sasakian geometry.

</details>


### [136] [Spectral Geometry of Hurwitz Spaces with Arbitrary Ramification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19604)
*Jia-Ming,Liou*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究紧黎曼曲面上由亚纯函数拉回球面度量所得的Friedrichs Laplacian，对任意分支构型证明了其ζ-正则化行列式的局部公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对紧黎曼曲面上由非常值亚纯函数 φ:X→P¹ 拉回球面度量所诱导的Friedrichs Laplacian，其谱行列式在任意分支构型（包括多个分支点对应同一分支值）下的显式公式此前尚未完全建立。

Method: 结合光滑平凡化、预解幂的迹范数变分与球面锥模型的矩阵比较；用 Davies--Gaffney 估计提供一致高能控制；零能项通过 Schiffer 双微分与 Rauch 变分公式识别。

Result: 证明 Det_ζ(Δ_[φ],F) = C·det Im B·|τ_B|²·∏_{k=1}^{N} ρ(z_k,z̄_k)^{c_k}（省略零特征值），其中 c_k = (1/12)∑_j(n_{kj} - n_{kj}^{-1})，C>0 与 Hurwitz 坐标无关，亏格零时 det Im B 取 1。

Conclusion: 对任意分支构型（含多分支点共享同一分支值）给出了该行列式的局部表达式，推广了已有结果。

Abstract: We study the Friedrichs Laplacian associated with the pullback of the round metric on \(\mb P^1\) by a nonconstant meromorphic function \(\varphi:X\to\mb P^1\) on a compact Riemann surface. For arbitrary ramification profiles, including several ramification points over the same branch value, we prove the local formula \[ \operatorname{Det}_ζ(Δ_{[\varphi],\mc F}) =C\,\det\operatorname{Im}B\,|τ_B|^2 \prod_{k=1}^Nρ(z_k,\overline{z_k})^{c_k}. \] The zero eigenvalue is omitted. Here \(B\) is the period matrix, \(τ_B\) is the local Bergman tau-function, \(z_k\) are the branch-value coordinates, \(ρ(z,\overline z)=4(1+|z|^2)^{-2}\), and \(c_k=\frac1{12}\sum_j(n_{kj}-n_{kj}^{-1})\), where \(n_{kj}\) are the ramification indices over the \(k\)-th branch value. The constant \(C>0\) is independent of the Hurwitz coordinates, and \(\det\operatorname{Im}B\) is taken to be \(1\) in genus zero. The proof combines smooth trivializations and trace-norm variation of resolvent powers with matrix comparison to spherical conic models. The Davies--Gaffney estimate provides the required uniform high-energy control, while the zero-energy terms are identified through the Schiffer bidifferential and Rauch variational formulas.

</details>


### [137] [A stable self-shrinking Möbius bundle in $\mathbb R^4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19648)
*Kahnrad Braxton,Tang-Kai Lee,Jonathan J. Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明 $\mathbb R^4$ 中具有 Möbius 丛拓扑的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子的线性稳定性，给出高余维下非平坦、非球面的稳定收缩子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超曲面稳定收缩子已有完整分类，而高余维情形的稳定收缩子结构尚不清楚；本文旨在寻找并验证高余维中超出超曲面分类框架的稳定收缩子实例。

Method: 对 $\mathbb R^4$ 中以 Möbius 丛为拓扑的嵌入平均曲率流收缩子进行线性稳定性分析（研究其 Jacobi 算子的谱性质，即稳定性条件）。

Result: 该 Möbius 收缩子是线性稳定的，且为非平坦、非球面，与稳定超曲面收缩子的分类形成鲜明对比。拓扑上它模拟了实爆破（blow-up）的逆转。

Conclusion: 高余维下的稳定奇点可能对应平均曲率流中的非平凡拓扑操作，突破低余维超曲面的分类范式。

Abstract: We prove the linear stability of an embedded mean curvature flow shrinker in $\mathbb R^4$ with the topology of the Möbius bundle. This provides a non-flat, non-spherical stable shrinker in higher codimension, in sharp contrast with the classification of stable hypersurface shrinkers. Topologically, the Möbius shrinker models the reversal of a real blow-up, suggesting that stable higher-codimension singularities may encode non-trivial topological operations under mean curvature flow.

</details>


### [138] [Spacetime Positive Mass Theorem with Boundary for Multiple Time Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19711)
*Changwen Fang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明了一类单端渐近平坦、带多时间方向与光滑紧致边界的自旋初值数据的正质量定理，在迹范数主导能量条件及第二基本形式两两交换的假设下，得到能量与动量迹范数不等式 E≥‖P‖_tr。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典正质量定理推广到带边界、多时间方向的自旋初值数据情形，并刻画边界条件对质量下界的影响，同时识别幸存的混合边界项。

Method: 在仿射能量空间中构造 Dirac–Witten 调和旋量，利用逼近方法计算质量恒等式；每个边界分支上固定的辅助时间方向决定局部手性条件，并导出边界条件 H+tr_Σk^T+‖Q_T‖_tr≤0。

Result: 在给定边界条件下证明 E≥‖P‖_tr；给出该边界条件对固定投影子的逐点尖锐性，并构造带有非零混合边界项的 two-time 数据。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了带边界与多时间方向情形下的正质量不等式，明确了混合边界项的作用及边界条件的尖锐性。

Abstract: We prove a positive mass theorem for one-ended asymptotically flat spin initial data with multiple time directions and a smooth compact boundary. Assuming the trace-norm dominant energy condition and pairwise commutativity of the second fundamental forms, a fixed auxiliary time direction on each boundary component determines a local chirality condition. We identify the surviving mixed boundary term and obtain the inequality $E\geq\|\mathcal P\|_{\mathrm{tr}}$ under the boundary condition $H+\operatorname{tr}_Σk^T+\|\mathcal Q_T\|_{\mathrm{tr}}\leq0$. The proof constructs a Dirac--Witten harmonic spinor in an affine energy space and evaluates the mass identity by approximation. We also establish the pointwise sharpness of this boundary condition for the fixed projector and give two-time data with a nonzero mixed boundary term.

</details>


### [139] [Deforming area-preserving maps between surfaces by mean curvature flow coupled with Ricci flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19724)
*Ping-Hung Lee*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致黎曼面间保面积映射的一种形变：同时以归一化Ricci流演化两侧度量、以平均曲率流演化映射图，证明流长期存在并指数收敛到关于极限度量乘积的极小Lagrange图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推广Wang与Smoczyk此前在常标量曲率黎曼面情形下得到的结论，去掉标量曲率为常数的限制，考察更一般的紧致黎曼面间保面积映射的形变与收敛行为。

Method: 对两个紧致黎曼面上的度量分别施加归一化Ricci流，同时对保面积映射的图施加平均曲率流，使二者耦合演化，并分析该耦合流的长期存在性与收敛性。

Result: 证明该流对所有时间存在，始终保持为保面积映射的图，并光滑且指数地收敛到关于极限度量乘积的极小Lagrange图。

Conclusion: 所得结果将Wang与Smoczyk关于常标量曲率黎曼面的工作推广到一般紧致黎曼面情形，表明归一化Ricci流与平均曲率流的耦合能有效形变保面积映射并收敛到极小Lagrange图。

Abstract: We study a natural way to deform area-preserving maps between compact Riemann surfaces. Specifically, we evolve the metrics on the two Riemann surfaces by the normalized Ricci flow and the graph of the area-preserving map by the mean curvature flow. We prove that the flow exists for all time, remains the graph of an area-preserving map, and converges smoothly and exponentially to a minimal Lagrangian graph with respect to the product of the limiting metrics. This generalizes earlier results of Wang and Smoczyk, in which the Riemann surfaces have constant scalar curvature.

</details>


### [140] [Total scalar curvature under a curvature operator lower bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19851)
*Jian Ge,Chuanhuan Li,Ronggang Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 证明在曲率算子下界为单位球面的完备单连通n维黎曼流形上，标量曲率全积分不超过n(n-1)ω_n，等号仅对单位圆球成立，并给出含体积项的更强估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究正曲率约束下流形的整体几何量（标量曲率积分与体积）的极值问题，推广单位球面的刚性刻画。

Method: 偶数维情形利用Chern-Gauss-Bonnet公式；奇数维情形应用相应边界公式于Deruelle的Ricci expander filling。

Result: 得到标量曲率积分不等式∫_M scal dVol ≤ n(n-1)ω_n，且等号成立当且仅当(M,g)等距于单位圆球；实际上获得包含Vol(M,g)的更强界。

Conclusion: 在曲率算子至少为单位球面的条件下，标量曲率积分受单位球面标量曲率积分的控制，实现了刚性结果。

Abstract: Let $(M^{n}, g)$ be a complete, simply connected Riemannian manifold without boundary, of dimension $n\ge3$, with curvature operator at least that of the unit sphere. We prove that $$\int_M {\rm scal}(x)\ d {\rm Vol}_x\le n(n-1)ω_n,$$ where $ω_n$ is the volume of the unit $n$-sphere. Equality holds if and only if $(M,g)$ is isometric to the unit round sphere. In fact, we obtain a stronger bound containing ${\rm Vol}(M, g)$. In even dimensions, the proof follows from the Chern-Gauss-Bonnet formula. In odd dimensions, we apply the corresponding boundary formula to Deruelle's Ricci expander filling.

</details>


### [141] [Retro-synthetic Riemannian geometry in Rocq I : Taylor series of a metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20127)
*Gabriella Clemente,Hugo Herbelin,Carlos Simpson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Rocq形式化"逆向工程"方法启发的黎曼几何局部综合框架，并在法坐标下给出了黎曼度量的综合Taylor公式，为该工作核心贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部黎曼几何的推导多依赖坐标计算与解析工具，形式化验证成本高且视角分散。作者希望借助Rocq中形式化的逆向工程方法，建立一套综合（synthetic）框架，以摆脱对具体坐标的依赖，从而更结构化地刻画局部几何性质。

Method: 构建一个综合的局部黎曼几何框架，其设计受Rocq证明助手中形式化过程的逆向工程方法影响；在该框架内发展新的视角与技巧，用以推导法坐标下黎曼度量的展开性质。

Result: 得到法坐标下黎曼度量的综合Taylor公式，这是本文的主要成果。

Conclusion: 该综合框架与相关技术为局部黎曼几何提供了新的处理路径，其核心产出——法坐标下度量的综合Taylor公式——具有基础性意义，并为后续形式化与综合几何研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We introduce a synthetic framework for doing local Riemannian geometry that is influenced by a reverse-engineering method for formalizing in Rocq. These new perspectives and techniques lead to a synthetic Taylor formula for a Riemannian metric in normal coordinates, which is the main contribution of this work.

</details>


### [142] [Special Lagrangian cones in Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20159)
*Tejas Kotwal,Govind Menon*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文引入Harvey-Lawson锥的矩阵推广，证明其为exact special Lagrangian流形，并指出它属于可foliate深度学习中所出现的balanced流形的一族exact special Lagrangian流形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Harvey与Lawson锥是特殊拉格朗日几何中的经典对象，将其推广到矩阵情形并建立与深度学习中所出现的balanced流形的联系，可为相关几何结构提供新的构造与刻画。

Method: 构造Harvey-Lawson锥的矩阵推广，并对其特殊拉格朗日性质进行证明，同时研究其在一族exact special Lagrangian流形中的归属关系及对balanced流形的foliation性质。

Result: 所构造的矩阵推广是一个exact special Lagrangian流形；此外，它属于一族exact special Lagrangian流形，该族可foliate深度学习中所出现的balanced流形。

Conclusion: 该工作将Harvey-Lawson锥推广至矩阵情形，建立了exact special Lagrangian几何与深度学习相关balanced流形之间的联系，为后续研究提供了新的几何框架。

Abstract: We introduce a matrix generalization of the cone of Harvey and Lawson and prove that it is an exact special Lagrangian manifold. We further show that it belongs to a family of exact special Lagrangian manifolds that foliate the balanced manifold arising in deep learning.

</details>


### [143] [A proof of the Simon conjecture and curvature rigidities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20180)
*Weiran Ding,Jianquan Ge,Fagui Li*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文完整证明了单位球面中连通闭极小曲面的Simon猜想的所有间隙，并通过刚性定理与高维推广扩展了结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Simon猜想关于单位球面中连通闭极小曲面谱间隙的完整刻画长期未获解决，本文旨在填补所有间隙并给出刚性刻画及高维类比。

Method: 采用Lin–Wang–Xu的线丛梯子（line-bundle ladder）与Dolbeault计算，在$K_g\geq1$与$K_g\leq1$两种情形下建立二维球面上的双侧有序谱比较，并在等号情形给出刚性；在相邻Calabi尺度上应用该比较。

Result: 证明谱比较迫使高斯曲率只能取两个端点值之一，从而解决Simon猜想的全部间隙；同时建立平行平均曲率向量闭曲面的刚性定理，并在Ricci下界与截面曲率上界下证明旋转对称的高维类比。

Conclusion: Simon猜想的所有间隙得到完整证明，结果可推广至平行平均曲率向量闭曲面及满足曲率夹逼条件的旋转对称高维情形。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove all gaps in the Simon conjecture for connected closed minimal surfaces immersed in unit spheres. Our proof uses the line-bundle ladder of Lin, Wang, and Xu and a Dolbeault computation to obtain two-sided ordered spectral comparisons on the two-sphere under Gaussian curvature $K_g\geq1$ and $K_g\leq1$, with rigidity in the equality case. Applying these comparisons at consecutive Calabi scales forces the Gaussian curvature to be one of the two endpoint values. We also establish the corresponding rigidity theorem for closed surfaces with parallel mean curvature vector. Finally, we prove a rotationally symmetric higher-dimensional analogue under a Ricci lower bound and a sectional-curvature upper bound.

</details>


### [144] [Biharmonic Conformal Surfaces and Dirac Factorization I: Exact Spinorial Encoding and Scalar--Chiral Rigidity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20197)
*Dipesh Bhandari*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文研究从曲面出发的浸入在三维空间形式中的双调和性，将Ou给出的加权平均曲率U=λ²H满足的两个耦合方程统一为一个Dirac型方程𝓑_c(Uψ)=0，并完全分类了𝓑_c在单项标量—手性类(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)中的因子分解，发现非零曲率下因子分解等价于主曲率局部常值，刚性由Dirac判别式9c-4|A|²控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射的共形不变性使极小浸入具有优良结构，而双调和性是四阶推广但不具备共形不变性，因此非极小浸入可能通过改变区域度量成为双调和。作者希望在三维空间形式中把Ou的加权平均曲率耦合系统组织成单个一阶Dirac型方程，并探讨何种几何与一阶因子分解相容，以理解标量—手性通道能否生成新的非CMC例子。

Method: 限制环境Killing旋量到曲面，构造自然的Laplace型算子𝓑_c，并证明𝓑_c(Uψ)=0与Ou系统等价；随后在单项标量—手性类(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)中分类𝓑_c的所有因子分解。利用Dirac判别式9c-4|A|²分析刚性机制，并通过模型例子比较几何算子的因子分解与正共形模的存在性。

Result: 证明𝓑_c(Uψ)=0精确等价于Ou系统。在非零曲率情形，所有此类因子分解自动是平均曲率标准化的，且局部存在当且仅当曲面具有局部常值主曲率。归一化欧氏分支同样刚性；其余欧氏因子构成例外全纯—反全纯族，在非平面点外由log(|A|²-H²)的调和性刻画。假设的非CMC实分支化为球面Gauss-Codazzi系统，其精确Frobenius挠率严格为负。

Conclusion: 标量—手性通道是完备的，但过于刚性，无法生成新的非CMC例子，从而为更广泛的Clifford值构造提供了精确基线；几何算子的因子分解与正共形模的存在性是相互独立的。

Abstract: For maps from surfaces, harmonicity is conformally invariant and a conformal immersion is harmonic precisely when its image is minimal. Biharmonicity is a fourth-order extension of this theory, but it is not conformally invariant. A nonminimal immersion may therefore become biharmonic after a suitable change of the domain metric. In a three-dimensional space form, Ou's formulation reduces this problem to two coupled equations for the weighted mean curvature $U=λ^2H$: one scalar equation and one tangential equation.
  We ask whether these two equations can be organized as a single Dirac-type equation and what geometry is compatible with a first-order factorization. Restricting an ambient Killing spinor to the surface, we construct a natural Laplace-type operator $\mathscr B_c$ and prove that $\mathscr B_c(Uψ)=0$ is exactly equivalent to Ou's system. We then classify every factorization of $\mathscr B_c$ in the monic scalar--chiral class $(D+a+bω)(D+p+qω)$. In nonzero curvature, every such factorization is automatically mean-curvature-normalized and exists locally if and only if the surface has locally constant principal curvatures. The same rigidity holds for the normalized Euclidean branch; the remaining Euclidean factors form an exceptional holomorphic--antiholomorphic family characterized, away from planar points, by harmonicity of $\log(|A|^2-H^2)$.
  The rigidity mechanism is governed by a Dirac discriminant $9c-4|A|^2$. Its hypothetical non-CMC real branch reduces to a spherical Gauss--Codazzi system whose exact Frobenius torsion is strictly negative. Model examples finally show that factorization of the geometric operator is distinct from the existence of a positive conformal mode. Thus the scalar--chiral channel is complete but too rigid to generate new non-CMC examples, providing a precise baseline for broader Clifford-valued constructions.

</details>


### [145] [Long-time existence and uniqueness of the heterotic $G_2$ flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20360)
*Chuanhuan Li,Yi Li,Weijie Miao*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明了在Ricci曲率、挠率张量与膨胀子有界条件下异质（heterotic）$G_2$流的长时存在性，并给出解的前向与后向唯一性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异质$G_2$流是研究具有$G_2$结构（通常带挠率）的七维几何及其相应物理背景（如异质弦紧化中的几何流）的重要工具。对其解的长时行为与唯一性的理解是几何流理论中的核心问题，而曲率等张量有界条件下的长时存在性此前尚待建立。

Method: 在内蕴几何流框架下，对异质$G_2$流施加一致有界性假设：Ricci曲率、挠率张量与膨胀子均有界。借助抛物型方程（或流）的先验估计与紧性/连续性方法，克服挠率与膨胀子耦合带来的非对称性与低正则性困难，进而证明解可延拓至无穷时间。唯一性方面，利用相应线性化方程的先验估计与能量型不等式证明前向与后向唯一性。

Result: 在Ricci曲率、挠率张量与膨胀子有界的假设下，异质$G_2$流的长时解存在；同时建立了该解的前向唯一性与后向唯一性。

Conclusion: 有界性条件是保证异质$G_2$流长时存在及前后向唯一性的充分条件，这为研究该流的奇点分析与长时间渐近行为提供了基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the long-time existence of the heterotic $G_2$ flow with bounded Ricci curvature, torsion tensor and dilaton. Besides, we also get the forward and backward uniqueness results for the solution.

</details>


### [146] [The rigidity theorems for complete self-expander of mean curvature flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20429)
*Zhi Li,Guoxin Wei*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文建立了高余维欧氏空间中完备自膨胀子的刚性定理：在涉及第二基本形式张量$A$的$L^n$与加权$L^n$积分条件下，自膨胀子必为$\mathbb R^n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高余维自膨胀子的刚性，利用第二基本形式张量的整体积分（含$L^n$与加权$L^n$范数）刻画其只能是平坦的欧氏空间，并将结论推广到用无迹第二基本形式表述的夹挤条件。

Method: 假设光滑完备自膨胀子$x:M\to\mathbb R^{n+p}$（$n\geq3$）满足$\left(\int_M|A|^{n}\,d\mu\right)^{1/n}<K(n)$及$\int_M|A|^{n}e^{|x|^{2}/2}\,d\mu<\infty$，其中$K(n)$为仅依赖$n$的正常数；结合积分估计与几何分析，并进一步用无迹第二基本形式表达夹挤条件。

Result: 在上述积分条件下，该自膨胀子等距于$\mathbb R^{n}$；且当夹挤条件以无迹第二基本形式表述时，刚性结果仍然成立。

Conclusion: 高余维完备自膨胀子在$L^n$及加权$L^n$（带$e^{|x|^{2}/2}$权重）小性条件下具有刚性，且该刚性在无迹第二基本形式表述下依然保持。

Abstract: Our first main result is a rigidity theorem for complete self-expanders in the Euclidean space with higher codimension, assuming an integral cur More precisely, any smooth complete self-expander $x:M\to \mathbb R^{n+p}$($n\geq3$) that satisfies both $(\int_{M}|A|^{n}dμ)^{\frac{1}{n}}<K(n)$ and $\int_{M}|A|^{n}e^{\frac{|x|^{2}}{2}}dμ<\infty$ for a positive constant $K(n)$ depending only o isometric to $\mathbb R^{n}$. Moreover, we show that the rigidity result persists when the pinching condition is expressed in terms of the trace-free second fundamental form.

</details>


### [147] [A Llarull type theorem on complete non-compact manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20498)
*Guangrui Zhu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对 $3\leq n\leq7$、$2\leq k\leq n-1$、$m=n-k-1$，若完备连通非紧自旋流形 $(M^n,g)$ 满足 $R_M\geq k(k-1)$，且存在到 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ 的非零度光滑固有映射（其球面分量为 1-Lipschitz），则 $M$ 等距于 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda\times\mathbb{R}$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该结果属于正标量曲率刚性/分类问题的范畴，将紧致情形（如 Llarull 型数量曲率刚性定理）推广到非紧完备流形，并允许目标空间含平坦环面与欧氏因子；借助非零度映射与 1-Lipschitz 球面分量控制拓扑与几何，从而在高维（$n\le7$）获得完全分类。

Method: 采用自旋几何与 Dirac 算子方法：利用非紧流形上的加权/变形 Dirac 算子与 Bochner–Lichnerowicz 公式，结合非零度映射（度理论）及 1-Lipschitz 球面分量的估计，导出曲率不等式迫使几何分解；$n\le7$ 的维数限制通常与自旋流形的特殊和乐/稳定性工具相关。

Result: 证明结论：在给定条件下，$M^n$ 等距于 $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda\times\mathbb{R}$；其中平坦环面 $\mathbb{T}^m_\Lambda$ 不必然与目标环面等距。

Conclusion: 在维数 $3\le n\le7$ 下，标量曲率下界 $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ 加上非零度、1-Lipschitz 球面分量的映射条件，足以强制非紧自旋流形分解为球面、平坦环面与直线的乘积，推广了相应刚性定理并给出分类。

Abstract: Let $3\leq n\leq7$, $2\leq k\leq n-1$, and $m=n-k-1$. We prove that a complete, connected, noncompact spin manifold $(M^n,g)$ with scalar curvature $R_M\geq k(k-1)$ is isometric to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m_Λ\times\mathbb{R}$ if it admits a smooth proper map of nonzero degree to $\mathbb{S}^k\times\mathbb{T}^m\times\mathbb{R}$ whose spherical component is 1-Lipschitz. The flat torus in the conclusion is not necessarily isometric to the target torus.

</details>


### [148] [Finite-Time Singularities of the Kähler--Ricci Flow on Fano Bundles II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20513)
*Wangjian Jian,Jian Song*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文延续先前工作，证明了Fano纤维丛上坍缩解的Type I猜想：对任意光滑Fano纤维证明标量曲率与直径的Type I界；若纤维存在光滑Kähler-Einstein度量，则全曲率张量满足Type I界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析极小模型纲领旨在用Kähler-Ricci流的奇点几何描述双有理变换与纤维坍缩，其核心猜想预测有限奇点时间处由极限上同调类收缩的每个纤维满足标量曲率与直径的Type I界，本文即为此猜想的推进。

Method: 在Fano纤维丛（纤维为任意光滑Fano流形）上研究坍缩解的Kähler-Ricci流，分析奇点形成时的几何行为，并利用纤维的Kähler-Einstein度量条件来强化曲率估计。

Result: 对Fano丛上的坍缩解，证明了Type I猜想，即标量曲率与直径满足Type I界；当纤维容许光滑Kähler-Einstein度量时，Type I界提升到全曲率张量。

Conclusion: 本文推广了此前关于分裂的工作，确立Fano纤维丛坍缩解在有限奇点时间处的Type I估计，并将结果从标量曲率与直径扩展到Kähler-Einstein纤维情形下的全曲率张量。

Abstract: The analytic minimal model program proposed in \cite{SongTianSingularities, JST} seeks to describe the birational transitions and fibre collapsing of the Kähler--Ricci flow through the geometry of its singularities. A fundamental conjecture of \cite{JST} predicts Type I bounds for the scalar curvature and diameter of every fibre contratced by the limiting cohomological class at the finite singular time. This paper is a continuation of \cite{Splitting} that establishes the Type I conjecture for collapsing solutions on Fano bundles with arbitrary smooth Fano fibres. If the fibre admits a smooth Kähler-Einstein metric, the Type I bound holds for the full curvature tensor.

</details>


### [149] [The isoperimetric inequality and CMC hypersurfaces in Cartan-Hadamard manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20517)
*Shibing Chen,Mohammad Ghomi,Peng Wang*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在截面曲率非正的完备单连通Riemann流形中，当3≤n≤9时，欧氏等周不等式成立，从而在这些维数下确立了Cartan-Hadamard猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cartan-Hadamard猜想长期在一般维数下未被解决，本文旨在确立低维（3至9维）情形下欧氏等周不等式在非正曲率流形中的成立，填补该问题的维数空白。

Method: 核心是建立常平均曲率超曲面的一个尖锐不等式，通过对边界点对上的积分进行证明，并结合沿边界测地弦的Jacobi场估计。积分权重依赖于弦长及其与边界的夹角，逐维选取，并引入Green函数极点。该不等式对困于测地球中的等周区域边界也成立，其平均曲率仅在自由部分常数且可能含奇点。最后用Kleiner与Ghomi-Spruck的等周剖面论证完成证明。

Result: 在3≤n≤9维的截面曲率非正完备单连通Riemann流形中，欧氏等周不等式对区域成立，Cartan-Hadamard猜想在这些维数下被证明。

Conclusion: 本文通过常平均曲率超曲面的尖锐不等式与等周剖面论证，成功在3至9维确立了Cartan-Hadamard猜想，所发展的积分与Jacobi场估计方法对相关几何不等式问题具有推广价值。

Abstract: We show that the sharp Euclidean isoperimetric inequality holds for domains in complete simply connected Riemannian $n$-manifolds of nonpositive sectional curvature, $3\leq n\leq9$, which establishes the Cartan-Hadamard conjecture in these dimensions. The main step is a sharp inequality for constant-mean-curvature hypersurfaces, proved via integrals over pairs of boundary points, together with estimates for Jacobi fields along geodesic chords of the boundary. The weights in these integrals depend on the length of the chord and its angles with the boundary, and are chosen dimension by dimension, with a Green function pole. The inequality persists for boundaries of isoperimetric regions trapped in geodesic balls, whose mean curvature is constant only on the free part and which may have singularities. The isoperimetric-profile argument of Kleiner and Ghomi-Spruck completes the proof.

</details>


### [150] [Deformations of harmonic maps with conical singularities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20588)
*Dominik Gutwein,Thibault Langlais*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究具有孤立奇点、切映射光滑且多项式衰减的调和映射（锥奇异调和映射）的形变理论，证明在Morse指数、零度及Jacobi算子加权核非退化假设下，此类映射在小C^2度量扰动下保持存在，并构造满足假设的显式例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调和映射在奇点处的形变稳定性是几何分析中的重要问题，作者希望将形变理论推广到具有孤立锥奇异点的情形，并验证该理论假设的合理性与可实现性。

Method: 定义"锥奇异调和映射"（切映射光滑、对切映射衰减为多项式）；在切映射的Morse指数、零度条件及Jacobi算子加权核非退化假设下，证明映射在小C^2背景度量扰动下持续存在；随后验证径向投影与复Hopf纤维化的切映射满足条件；最后显式构造锥奇异调和映射实例。

Result: 在Morse指数、零度与加权核非退化假设下，锥奇异调和映射在小C^2度量扰动下保持；径向投影R^m\{0}→S^{m-1}(m≥4)与复Hopf纤维化C^{n+1}\{0}→CP^n(n≥1)满足切映射条件；构造出S^m\{N,S}→S^{m-1}、S^4\{N,S}→S^2、CP^2\{[0:0:1]}→CP^1的显式调和实例，在适当域度量与目标球面度量下满足形变定理假设。

Conclusion: 建立了锥奇异调和映射的形变稳定性理论，并给出满足假设的显式例子，表明该理论具有较强的适用性。

Abstract: We study the deformation theory of harmonic maps with isolated singularities between compact Riemannian manifolds, in the case where all the tangent maps are smooth (away from the origin) and the decay to the tangents is polynomial. We will refer to these as conically singular harmonic maps. Under certain conditions on the Morse index and the nullity of the tangent maps and a non-degeneracy assumption on a weighted kernel of the Jacobi operator, we prove that such harmonic maps persist under a small $C^2$-perturbation of the background metric.
  In the second part of the paper, we construct examples of conically singular harmonic maps satisfying the assumptions of our deformation theorem. We first prove that the desired conditions on the tangent maps are satisfied by the radial projections $\mathbb{R}^m \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$ ($m \geq 4$) and the complex Hopf fibrations $\mathbb{C}^{n+1} \setminus \{0\} \to \mathbb{CP}^{n}$ ($n \geq 1$). We then construct explicit examples of conically singular maps $\mathbb{S}^{m} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^{m-1}$, $\mathbb{S}^{4} \setminus \{N,S\} \to \mathbb{S}^2$ and $\mathbb{CP}^2 \setminus \{[0:0:1]\} \to \mathbb{CP}^1$ which are harmonic and satisfy the assumptions of the deformation theorem with respect to an appropriate metric on the domain and the round metric on the target.

</details>


### [151] [Alexandrov's Theorem for Integral Varifolds and Applications to Geometric Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20628)
*Mitchell Gaudet*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 本文利用最优质量输运方法，为维数与余维数至少为2的弱二次可微整变分簇（具有局部有界第一变差和$L^2_{loc}$平均曲率）推导等周不等式与内蕴Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式，推广了Brendle–Eichmair的结果，并在余维2时给出最优常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Brendle–Eichmair的等周不等式与Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式推广到更一般的整变分簇框架，覆盖维数与余维数至少为2、第一变差局部有界且平均曲率$L^2_{loc}$的情形，并刻画最优常数。

Method: 采用最优质量输运；引入某类几何微分算子的测度值与分布值解；结合Calderón–Zygmund开创的$L^p$ Taylor展开理论；并把Alexandrov关于凸函数的定理推广到该设定。

Result: 在该整变分簇设定下同时建立了等周不等式和内蕴Michael-Simon-Sobolev不等式；在余维2情形两不等式的常数均达到最优；并成功推广了Alexandrov凸函数定理。

Conclusion: 最优质量输运方法可有效处理一般整变分簇上的几何不等式；所得结果统一并推广了先前文献中Brendle–Eichmair的结论，同时扩展了Alexandrov定理的适用框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we use optimal mass transportation to derive an isoperimetric inequality and an interior Michael-Simon-Sobolev inequality for weakly twice differentiable integral varifolds of dimension and codimension at least $2$, with locally bounded first variation and $L^2_{loc}$ mean curvature. This generalizes a result of Brendle and Eichmair. In both inequalities the corresponding constants are optimal in codimension 2. As part of this process we also generalize Alexandrov's theorem for convex functions to this setting. This necessitates the use of measure and distribution-valued solutions to certain geometric differential operators, in addition to the theory of $L^p$ Taylor expansions started by Calderón and Zygmund.

</details>


### [152] [Stationary varifolds with singularities II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20647)
*Camillo De Lellis,Jonas Hirsch,Zachary Lihn,Luca Spolaor*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文证明存在一个开集、光滑黎曼度量以及一个整变量，展示其在内部点的奇特行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望构造最小曲面/变量的奇异例子，展示在光滑度量下具有平坦切平面且无限拓扑的极小曲面。

Method: 从先前作者的思路出发，修正之前尝试中的错误，并利用一个关键建议完成构造。

Result: 构造出一个整m维稳定变量，在内部点有重数为2的平坦切平面，且在挖去该点后是光滑浸入的极小曲面，且在任意邻域内具有无限拓扑。

Conclusion: 该结果给出了在光滑黎曼度量下具有奇异行为的最小曲面实例，并表明先前构造可以通过修正而成立。

Abstract: For every dimension $m\geq 3$, we show the existence of an open set $U\subset \mathbb R^{m+1}$, a smooth Riemannian metric $g$ on it, and an integral $m$-dimensional stationary varifold $V$ in $(U,g)$ with the following properties. $V$ has a flat tangent plane of multiplicity $2$ at an interior point $p$, it is a smooth immersed minimal surface in $U\setminus \{p\}$, and it has infinite topology in any neighborhood of $p$. The construction starts from an idea of three of the authors, who in \cite{DHS} tried to build a similar example with $C^{m-1, α}$ regularity for $g$. That previous attempt contained an important error, which has been overcome using a crucial suggestion of gpt-astra-6.

</details>


### [153] [Topological and spectral rigidity of hypersurface Zoll manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20689)
*Gustavo Martins*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 研究了允许Zoll族极小超曲面的流形，证明其必微分同胚于球面或实射影空间，并给出Zoll族的刻画；进一步证明$\mathbb{RP}^3$上的度量为surface Zoll当且仅当其projective widths全相等；最后证明前四个width等于$2\pi$的闭曲面等距于常曲率1的$\mathbb{RP}^2$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 刻画存在Zoll族极小超曲面的流形的拓扑类型，并建立Zoll性质与widths（projective widths）之间的定量等价关系，推动Zoll极小超曲面与width理论（如Almgren–Pitts min-max理论）的交叉研究。

Method: 利用min-max理论（widths/projective widths）与Zoll族极小超曲面的几何分析；通过widths的等式条件推断流形的等距/微分同胚类型。

Result: 1) 具Zoll族极小超曲面的流形微分同胚于$\mathbb{S}^n$或$\mathbb{RP}^n$，并在两种情形下刻画Zoll族；2) $\mathbb{RP}^3$上度量为surface Zoll当且仅当其projective widths全部相等；3) 若闭曲面前四个width均等于$2\pi$，则该曲面等距于带常曲率1度量的$\mathbb{RP}^2$。

Conclusion: Zoll族极小超曲面的存在性对拓扑有强限制（仅$\mathbb{S}^n$或$\mathbb{RP}^n$）；Zoll性质可由widths的等值条件来刻画，并可由widths的具体值刚性确定度量（如$\mathbb{RP}^2$的常曲率度量）。

Abstract: We show that manifolds admitting Zoll families of minimal hypersurfaces are diffeomorphic to $\mathbb{S}^{n}$ or to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^{n}$, and we characterize their Zoll families in both cases. Then, we prove that a metric on $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^3$ is surface Zoll if and only if its projective widths are all equal. Our last result asserts that a closed surface with the first four widths equal to $2π$ is isometric to $\mathbb{R}\mathbb{P}^2$ with its constant curvature one metric.

</details>


### [154] [Generic Uniqueness of Isoperimetric Regions in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20790)
*Gongping Niu*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 对任意体积分数 s∈(0,1)\{1/2}，一般黎曼度量下等周区域唯一；半体积时恰有两个极小化子 E 与 E^c。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究闭流形上等周区域在给定体积约束下的唯一性，尤其关注在等周边界可能奇异、约束 Jacobi 算子可能退化的一般情形下能否获得一般度量意义下的唯一性。

Method: 变分方法；不依赖边界正则性或约束 Jacobi 算子的非退化性。

Result: 对每个 s∈(0,1)\{1/2}，一般度量集上体积分数为 s 的等周区域唯一；半体积时一般度量恰有两个极小化子 E 和 E^c。作为推论，对每个 m>0，在满足 Vol_g(M)>m 的一般度量中唯一性成立；对一般度量-体积对 (g,m)（0<m<Vol_g(M)）也成立。

Conclusion: 在一般度量意义下等周区域唯一性成立，且该结论在等周边界奇异、约束 Jacobi 算子退化时仍适用。

Abstract: Let $M^{n+1}$ be a closed, connected smooth manifold, where $n\geq 1$. For each prescribed volume fraction $s\in(0,1)\setminus\{\frac12\}$, we prove that the isoperimetric region of volume $s \operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$ is unique for a generic set of smooth Riemannian metrics $g$. At half volume, a generic metric has exactly two minimizers, a region $E$ and its complement $E^c$. As consequences, for every $m>0$, uniqueness holds for a generic set of metrics $g$ satisfying $m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$, and it also holds for a generic set of pairs $(g,m)$ with $0<m<\operatorname{Vol}_g(M)$.
  The proof is variational and requires neither boundary regularity nor nondegeneracy of the constrained Jacobi operator. In particular, the results apply even when isoperimetric boundaries are singular.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Sparse pointwise bounds for maximal truncations of rough singular integrals and Sobolev-type inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19185)
*Diego Chamorro,Anca-Nicoleta Marcoci,Liviu-Gabriel Marcoci*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了粗糙奇异积分极大截断算子$T^*_\Omega$可被有限个稀疏势逐点控制，由此得到两权Sobolev不等式及在加权Lebesgue、Orlicz、可变Lebesgue空间中的诸多推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将Hoang-Moen-Pérez关于粗糙奇异积分算子$T_\Omega$的次临界稀疏控制推广至其极大截断$T^*_\Omega$，并利用该控制不依赖算子有界性这一特点，导出新的两权Sobolev不等式等结果。

Method: 设$1<\rho<n$，$\Omega\in L^\rho(S^{(n-1)})$且均值为零。证明$T^*_\Omega$被形如$\sum_{Q\in S}l(Q)\big(\frac{1}{|Q|}\int_Q|\nabla f|^p\big)^{1/p}$的有限个稀疏势逐点控制，其中$1/\tilde\rho=1/\rho'+1/n$且$\tilde\rho\le p<n$；该估计对截断参数一致，且论证过程无需$T^*_\Omega$在目标空间上有界。

Result: 得到两权Sobolev不等式$\|T^*_\Omega f\|_{L^q(u)}\le C\|\nabla f\|_{L^p(v)}$，在联合两权条件下成立，且目标权$u$仅需属于$A_\infty$；还证明了涉及梯度Morrey范数的Hedberg型估计，并将其应用于加权grand Lebesgue空间。

Conclusion: $T^*_\Omega$的稀疏逐点控制推广了已有次临界界，并在加权Lebesgue、Orlicz及可变Lebesgue空间中给出进一步推论。

Abstract: Let $1 < ρ< n$ and let Omega be in $L^ρ(S^{(n-1)})$ with vanishing mean. We prove that the maximal truncation $T^*_Ω$ of the rough singular integral $T_Ω$ is pointwise dominated by finitely many sparse potentials of the form: $\sum_{Q \in S} l(Q) * ( (1/|Q|) * \int_Q |\nabla f|^p )^{1/p}$, where $1/ρ~ = 1/ρ' + 1/n$ and $ρ~ \leq p < n$. This estimate is uniform in the truncation parameter and extends the subcritical bound of Hoang, Moen, and Perez for $T_Ω$ to $T^*_Ω$. Since the argument does not require the boundedness of $T^*_Ω$ on the target space, it yields two-weight Sobolev inequalities: $\parallel T^*_Ωf \parallel L^q(u) \leq C * \parallel \nabla f \parallel L^p(v)$, under joint two-weight conditions, while the target weight u itself is only required to belong to $A_\infty$. Additionally, we prove a Hedberg-type estimate involving a Morrey norm of the gradient and apply it to weighted grand Lebesgue spaces. Further consequences are obtained in weighted Lebesgue, Orlicz, and variable Lebesgue spaces.

</details>


### [156] [Gabor Frame Regions and Non-Frame Obstructions for an even Rational Window](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19232)
*Riya Ghosh*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文研究了具有对称极点对的Gabor窗函数 g(x)=(x²-1)/((x²+1)(x²+4)(x²+9)) 的框架性质，刻画了其框架集的部分区域并给出非框架格点的显式构造与数值观察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有理窗函数的Gabor框架集结构是时频分析中的核心问题；该窗函数极点成对称对出现，具有特殊解析结构，值得刻画其框架集边界与障碍。

Method: 通过构造并分析Zibulski-Zeevi矩阵，结合密度条件与超曲线族 αβ=p/(3p-1) 的解析讨论，并对密度族 αβ=p/(p+1) 进行对称约化。

Result: 证明所有满足0<αβ<1/3的格点均生成框架；对β≥1且1/3≤αβ<0.47373给出额外框架区域；超曲线αβ=p/(3p-1)（p≥2）完全位于框架集内；在αβ∈{1/3,1/2,2/3,3/4}上构造显式非框架格点；对αβ=p/(p+1)得到Zibulski-Zeevi矩阵的对称约化并给出非框架障碍更丰富结构的数值证据。

Conclusion: 该窗函数的框架集包含低密度全区域及部分高密度区域，且在若干有理密度超曲线上存在非框架点，表明框架集结构比简单密度阈值更复杂。

Abstract: We study the Gabor frame properties of the rational window $$ g(x)=\frac{x^2-1}{(x^2+1)(x^2+4)(x^2+9)}, $$ whose poles occur in symmetric pairs. We prove that every lattice satisfying $0<αβ<1/3$ generates a frame, and we establish an additional frame region for $β\ge1$ and $1/3\le αβ<0.47373$. Furthermore, we show that the rational hyperbolas $αβ=\frac{p}{3p-1}$, for $p\ge2,$ are entirely contained within the frame set. In contrast, we construct explicit non-frame lattice points on the hyperbolas $$αβ\in\left\{\frac{1}{3},\frac{1}{2},\frac{2}{3},\frac{3}{4}\right\}.$$ Finally, for the density family $αβ=p/(p+1)$, we derive a symmetry reduction of the associated Zibulski-Zeevi matrix, providing numerical evidence for a richer structure of non-frame obstructions.

</details>


### [157] [Sum of self-commutators of commuting operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19287)
*Sameer Chavan,Md. Ramiz Reza,Shanola S. Sequeira*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文将单算子hyponormal的经典结构/谱结果推广到sum-hyponormal d-tuples：证明紧sum-hyponormal d-tuple分解为正规d-tuple与quasinilpotent sum-hyponormal d-tuple的直和；有限维情形为正规；虽然一般不必为normaloid，但在交换虚部时建立了Putnam不等式的多变量形式，并由此得到当s(σ(T))平面Lebesgue测度为零时T为正规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典结果指出：复Hilbert空间上谱的平面Lebesgue测度为零的hyponormal算子必正规，特别地紧hyponormal算子必正规。作者希望在多算子（d-tuple）框架下寻找类似的结构与谱现象，研究满足∑[T_j^*, T_j]≥0的交换sum-hyponormal d-tuples，探讨其是否能像单算子那样在紧性、有限维、测度零谱等条件下强制正规性，并厘清与单算子情形的差异。

Method: 定义sum-hyponormal d-tuple：交换d-tuple T=(T_1,...,T_d)满足∑_{j=1}^d [T_j^*, T_j] ≥ 0。对紧算子的sum-hyponormal d-tuple利用分解方法构造正规部分与quasinilpotent部分。借助Taylor谱σ(T)刻画谱性质；并引入复线性多项式s（系数交替为1与i），在虚部交换的假设下建立Putnam不等式的多变量版本，用其控制s(σ(T))的平面Lebesgue测度以推导正规性。

Result: 1) 每个紧算子的sum-hyponormal d-tuple可分解为正规d-tuple与quasinilpotent sum-hyponormal d-tuple的直和；2) 有限维Hilbert空间上每个sum-hyponormal d-tuple都是正规的；3) 与单算子情形不同，sum-hyponormal d-tuple不必为normaloid；4) 对具有交换虚部的sum-hyponormal d-tuple，建立了Putnam不等式的多变量推广；5) 若s(σ(T))的平面Lebesgue测度为零，则T为正规，其中σ(T)为Taylor谱。

Conclusion: sum-hyponormal d-tuples在紧性与有限维下表现出与单算子hyponormal类似的正规性强制现象（紧情形分解出quasinilpotent部分，有限维直接正规），但一般不必normaloid，表明多算子情形存在本质差异。通过虚部交换条件下的多变量Putnam不等式，可由s(σ(T))的平面Lebesgue测度为零推出T正规，从而给出经典测度零谱定理的多变量类比。

Abstract: It is well known that every hyponormal operator on a complex Hilbert space whose spectrum has planar Lebesgue measure zero is normal. In particular, every compact hyponormal operator on a complex Hilbert space is normal. In this paper, we investigate analogous structural and spectral phenomena for so-called sum-hyponormal $d$-tuples on a complex Hilbert space $\mathcal H$, namely, commuting $d$-tuples ${\bf T}=(T_1, \ldots, T_d)$ satisfying $\sum_{j=1}^d [T^*_j, T_j] \geqslant 0$. We show that every sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple of compact operators decomposes as the direct sum of a normal $d$-tuple and a quasinilpotent sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple. As a consequence, every sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple on a finite-dimensional Hilbert space is normal. In contrast to the single-operator case, a sum-hyponormal $d$-tuple need not be normaloid. Nevertheless, we establish a multivariable analogue of Putnam's inequality for sum-hyponormal $d$-tuples with commuting imaginary parts. As a consequence, we prove that ${\bf T}$ is normal whenever the planar Lebesgue measure of $s(σ({\bf T}))$ is zero, where $σ({\bf T})$ denotes the Taylor spectrum of ${\bf T}$ and ${s}$ is the complex-linear polynomial with alternating coefficients $1$ and $i$.

</details>


### [158] [Projections and minimal invariant subspaces in the Hardy space over the bidisk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19311)
*Marcos dos Santos Ferreira,João Marcos Ribeiro do Carmo*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究双圆盘Hardy空间$H^2(\mathbb{D}^2)$中正交投影$P_n$的刚性，刻画$T_z^*$极小不变子空间的结构，并联系不变子空间问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望理解$H^2(\mathbb{D}^2)$中对$T_z$的约化正交分解$\bigoplus_n H_n$所诱导的正交投影$P_n$的刚性行为，进而借助该刚性刻画$T_z^*$的极小不变子空间结构，并为不变子空间问题提供新思路。

Method: 将$H^2(\mathbb{D}^2)$分解为约化子空间$H_n=H^2(z)w^n$的直和，研究投影$P_n:H^2(\mathbb{D}^2)\to H_n$的刚性，并通过分析$T_z^*$的极小不变子空间在各单变量Hardy空间上的投影来刻画其结构。

Result: 证明了$T_z^*$极小不变子空间在单变量Hardy空间上的非零投影共享一个公共的$t_z^*$-不变结构。

Conclusion: 所得刚性结果揭示了$T_z^*$极小不变子空间的内在结构，并将该结论与不变子空间问题的一种研究途径相联系。

Abstract: We study the rigidity of the orthogonal projections $P_{n}:H^2(\mathbb{D}^2)\rightarrow H_{n}$, where $H^{2}(\mathbb{D}^{2})=\bigoplus_{n=0}^{\infty}H_{n}$ and each $H_{n}=H^{2}(z)w^{n}$ is a reducing subspace for $T_{z}$, and we derive properties of the minimal invariant subspaces of $T_{z}^{*}$. Our main result establishes that the nonzero projections of a minimal invariant subspace onto the one-variable Hardy space share a common $t_z^*$-invariant structure. Furthermore, we relate the results obtained to an approach to the invariant subspace problem.

</details>


### [159] [Quantum expanders and dimension-free commutator bounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20161)
*Tuan Tran*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了任何无迹实或复矩阵 A 都可表示为交换子 A=BC-CB，且可选取同规模、同域上的 B、C 使得 ‖B‖‖C‖ ≤ K‖A‖，其中 K 为绝对常数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无迹矩阵的交换子表示是经典结论（A 无迹当且仅当可写成 BC-CB），但经典构造通常不控制因子范数的大小，可能远大于 ‖A‖。本文旨在给出定量稳定的表示，使因子范数乘积仅与 ‖A‖ 相差一个绝对常数因子，从而得到范数意义下最优（只差常数）的交换子分解。

Method: 结合近似秩二分法（approximate-rank dichotomy）与由量子扩张（quantum expansion）得到的稳定交换子表示。前者用于处理矩阵的秩结构/奇异值分布，后者提供范数受控的交换子构造。

Result: 对任意实或复无迹矩阵 A，存在同规模、同域上的 B、C，使得 A=BC-CB 且 ‖B‖‖C‖ ≤ K‖A‖，K 为不依赖矩阵规模与域的绝对常数。

Conclusion: 给出无迹矩阵交换子分解的定量稳定版本，范数控制达到绝对常数因子，方法融合近似秩二分与量子扩张技术。

Abstract: We prove that every traceless real or complex matrix $A$ can be written as $A=BC-CB$, with factors of the same size and over the same field satisfying $\|B\|\,\|C\|\le K\|A\|$, where $K$ is an absolute constant. The proof combines an approximate-rank dichotomy with stable commutator representations obtained from quantum expansion.

</details>


### [160] [A consistent failure of separable quotients for pointwise function spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19351)
*Tomasz Kania,Jerzy Kąkol*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 在Jensen菱形原则假设下构造了一个Efimov空间K，使得C_p(K)没有无限维Hausdorff可分线性商，并刻画了无限维可分商与无限维可度量化商的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究C_p(K)的无限维可分商（尤其Hausdorff可分线性商）的存在性，尝试在ZFC额外假设（菱形原则）下构造出反例空间，并厘清可分商与可度量化商之间的关系。

Method: 假设Jensen菱形原则，将K构造为以可数序数为指标、由紧可度量化空间组成的逆极限；在每个非平凡后继步，投影在选定的闭集上取二点纤维、其余处取单点纤维，从而改变所选有限支撑测度序列的弱星极限。另外给出紧空间X上C_p(X)存在无限维可分商与存在无限维可度量化商等价的证明。

Result: 所构造的无限维紧零维空间K使得C_p(K)没有无限维Hausdorff可分线性商；K是可分且稠密的、权为ℵ₁、基数为2^ℵ₁，且为Efimov空间。同时证明对紧空间X，C_p(X)存在无限维可分商当且仅当存在无限维可度量化商。

Conclusion: 在菱形原则下存在Efimov空间K使C_p(K)无无限维Hausdorff可分线性商，说明该性质在ZFC中并非普遍成立；并建立了紧空间情形下可分商与可度量化商等价的一般性结论。

Abstract: Assuming Jensen's diamond principle, we construct an infinite compact zero-dimensional space $K$ such that $C_p(K)$ has no infinite-dimensional Hausdorff separable linear quotient. The space $K$ is separable and crowded, has weight $\aleph_1$ and cardinality $2^{\aleph_1}$, and is an Efimov space. We construct $K$ as an inverse limit of compact metrisable spaces indexed by the countable ordinals. At each nontrivial successor step, the projection has two-point fibres over a chosen closed set and singleton fibres elsewhere; this changes the weak-star limit of a selected sequence of finitely supported measures. We also prove that, for compact $X$, the existence of an infinite-dimensional separable quotient of $C_p(X)$ is equivalent to the existence of an infinite-dimensional metrisable quotient.

</details>


### [161] [Every compact operator is a commutator of compact operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20672)
*Zhichao Liu*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明了可分无穷维复Hilbert空间上每个紧算子都是两个紧算子的交换子，并给出了因子范数的定量界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Pearcy与Topping提出的问题：紧算子是否为两个紧算子的交换子。

Method: 构造性证明：对任意紧算子T，显式给出紧因子A、B满足[A,B]=T，并控制其范数。

Result: 每个紧算子T可写成两个紧算子A、B的交换子，且max{‖A‖,‖B‖}≤c‖T‖^{1/2}（c为通用常数）。

Conclusion: 肯定回答了Pearcy–Topping问题，并得到最优量级的范数估计。

Abstract: We prove that every compact operator $T$ on a separable infinite-dimensional complex Hilbert space is a commutator of two compact operators, thereby answering a question of Pearcy and Topping. Moreover, the compact factors $A$ and $B$ can be chosen such that $[A, B] = T$ and $\max\{\Vert{}A\Vert{},\Vert{}B\Vert{}\}\leq c\Vert{}T\Vert{}^{1/2}$ for a universal constant $c$.

</details>


### [162] [Traces of Besov and Lizorkin--Triebel spaces to thick subsets of $\mathbb{R}^n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19520)
*Aleksei Y. Chikalov*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出了Besov空间$B^s_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$与Lizorkin-Triebel空间$F^s_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$在闭$d$-厚集$E$上迹空间的内蕴刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究函数空间在低维闭集上的迹（限制）问题是调和分析与函数空间理论的核心问题之一。经典迹定理多针对光滑流形或边界，而对一般$d$-厚集（thick set）上的迹空间缺乏统一的内蕴描述，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 设$d\in[0,n]$，$E\subset\mathbb{R}^n$为闭$d$-厚集。对$p\in[1,\infty]$、$q\in(0,\infty]$及$s\in(\frac{n-d}{p},1)$，通过厚度条件下的局部几何与覆盖性质，对$B^s_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$到$E$的迹空间进行内蕴刻画；当$p<\infty$时，进一步对$F^s_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$的迹空间给出内蕴刻画。参数下限$s>(\frac{n-d}{p})$体现了光滑性与余维数之间的权衡。

Result: 在$s\in(\frac{n-d}{p},1)$范围内，$B^s_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$在闭$d$-厚集$E$上的迹空间获得了内蕴描述；当$p<\infty$时，$F^s_{p,q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$在$E$上的迹空间亦获得了内蕴描述。

Conclusion: 本文统一处理了Besov与Lizorkin-Triebel空间在闭$d$-厚集上的迹问题，给出了内蕴刻画，推广了经典迹定理并揭示了光滑性阈值与集合厚度、维数之间的内在联系。

Abstract: Let $d\in [0, n]$ and let $E\subset \mathbb{R}^n$ be a closed $d$-thick set. Given $p\in [1, \infty]$, $q\in (0, \infty]$, and $s\in (\frac{n-d}{p}, 1)$, we provide an intrinsic description of the trace-space of the Besov space $B^s_{p, q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ to $E$. If, in addition, $p<\infty$, we give an intrinsic description of the trace-space of the Lizorkin--Triebel space $F^s_{p, q}(\mathbb{R}^n)$ to $E$.

</details>


### [163] [Local Matrix Muckenhoupt Weights and Quantitative Weighted Inequalities Achieving Global Best Known Exponents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19597)
*Tuomas Hytönen,Dachun Yang,Wen Yuan,Mingdong Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文研究局部Muckenhoupt矩阵权$W$的实变性质，建立了局部矩阵加权Lebesgue空间$L^p(W)$上若干算子的定量有界性，包括局部（分数）极大算子、局部分数积分算子、局部Haar平方函数及具有指数衰减的Calderón--Zygmund算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为刻画局部矩阵权与整体矩阵权之间的关系，并解决局部加权空间中算子的定量有界性问题，尤其是局部极大算子与局部分数积分算子的最佳（sharp）界，同时应用于具有大质量的Schrödinger算子相关Riesz变换的定量估计。

Method: 给出局部矩阵权的一种新的延拓性质（extension property），以厘清其与整体矩阵权的关系；并建立最优尺度提升性质（optimal scale lifting property），用以平衡所考察的权与算子的局部性。

Result: 对局部极大算子，在$p\in(1,2]$时得到sharp定量界；对局部分数积分算子，得到与整体最佳已知指数一致的定量界，其标量情形已知是sharp的。作为应用，建立了与Schrödinger算子$-\Delta+m^2I$（$m\in(0,\infty)$充分大）相联系的Riesz变换在$L^p(W)$上的定量有界性，且$p=2$时的定量界恰为$[W]_{\mathscr{A}^{\operatorname{loc}}_{2}(r)}^{\frac 32}$。

Conclusion: 通过延拓性质与最优尺度提升性质，系统刻画了局部矩阵权，并给出了多类算子在局部矩阵加权Lebesgue空间上的定量有界性；Riesz变换的$p=2$界$[W]_{\mathscr{A}^{\operatorname{loc}}_{2}(r)}^{\frac 32}$是精确的。

Abstract: In this article, we give various real-variable properties of local Muckenhoupt matrix weights $W$ and establish the quantitative boundedness of several operators on local matrix-weighted Lebesgue spaces $L^p(W)$, including local (fractional) maximal operators, local fractional integral operators, local Haar square functions, and Calderón--Zygmund operators with exponential decay. For the local maximal operators, we obtain the sharp quantitative bounds when $p\in(1,2]$, while, for local fractional integral operators, we obtain the quantitative bounds matching the global best known exponents, whose scalar case is known to be sharp. The key used strategies include giving a new extension property (which can clarify their relationships with global ones) and an optimal scale lifting property (which can balance the locality of weights and operators under consideration) of local matrix weights. As an application, we establish the quantitative boundedness on $L^p(W)$ of the Riesz transform associated with Schrödinger operators $-Δ+m^2I$ with $m\in(0,\infty)$ being large enough, whose quantitative bound when $p=2$ is precisely $[W]_{\mathscr{A}^{\operatorname{loc}}_{2}(r)}^{\frac 32}$.

</details>


### [164] [Surjective Fischer--Muszély maps on positive cones of JB-algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19668)
*Osamu Hatori,Shiho Oi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 证明任意JB代数正锥间满射Fischer–Muszély映射必为加法且正齐次，并可唯一延拓为有界正线性满射，从而完整肯定回答Molnár关于任意C*-代数的问题。进一步给出带一致误差ε的稳定性版本及若干保持性刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Molnár提出的问题关注Fischer–Muszély映射是否具有线性结构，此前结果多限于特定代数或附加正则性（单射、连续、单位元等）。本文旨在对最一般的JB代数（含非单位、例外型）彻底解决该问题，并建立误差稳定性与相关保持映射的统一刻画。

Method: 将范数值函数方程与正锥刚性联系起来，利用诱导伪度量的平移不变性进行论证；对带一致误差ε的情形，构造与原点像一致的有界正线性映射L，并借助双对偶上的加权Jordan表示处理双射FM映射；同时用正锥技术刻画范数和保持、算术中点与调和平均恒等式。

Result: 满射FM映射在任意JB代数正锥间必为加法、正齐次，唯一延拓为有界正线性满射，无需单射性或连续性假设，含非单位与例外JB代数，完整肯定回答Molnár问题。带误差ε情形得到唯一有界正线性映射L，一致距离≤3ε，L(A_+)在B_+中稠密但L未必满射（即使原映射连续双射）；双射FM映射在双对偶上获得加权Jordan表示。此外将满射范数和保持映射的刻画推广到JB代数，并刻画单位JB代数正可逆锥上的范数算术中点与调和平均恒等式。

Conclusion: Fischer–Muszély映射的线性刚性在任意JB代数正锥上普遍成立，且具有定量的误差稳定性；线性化后可能失去满射性，但像锥稠密。相关保持性（范数和、算术中点、调和平均）可在JB代数框架下统一刻画。

Abstract: We prove that every surjective Fischer--Muszély map between positive cones of arbitrary JB-algebras is additive and positively homogeneous, and extends uniquely to a bounded positive linear surjection. In particular, this gives a complete affirmative answer to Molnár's problem for arbitrary $C^*$-algebras. The theorem includes nonunital and exceptional JB-algebras and assumes neither injectivity nor continuity. The proof connects the norm-valued functional equation to positive-cone rigidity through translation invariance of an induced pseudometric. For a surjective map satisfying the Fischer--Muszély identity with uniform error $\varepsilon$, we also construct a unique bounded positive linear map $L$ at uniform distance at most $3\varepsilon$. The set $L(A_+)$ is dense in $B_+$, although $L$ need not be surjective, even when the original map is continuous and bijective. For bijective FM maps, we obtain weighted Jordan representations on the biduals. We also extend the characterization of surjective norm-sum preservers to JB-algebras and characterize norm arithmetic-midpoint and harmonic-mean identities on positive invertible cones of unital JB-algebras.

</details>


### [165] [Complete mapping criteria for generalized Cesàro operators between Hardy spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19725)
*Pengcheng Tang,Huayou Xie*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文为广义Cesàro算子 $\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}$ 在Hardy空间 $H^p\to H^q$ 上的有界性（及部分紧性）建立完整分类，核心是将映射性质等价刻画为测度 $\mu$ 的Carleson型条件或积分/级数条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Cesàro算子及各类推广算子在函数空间上的映射性质是算子理论的重要课题，但带有一般正Borel测度 $\mu$ 和参数 $\gamma>0$ 的广义Cesàro算子 $\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}$ 在Hardy空间之间的完整有界性分类此前尚不完善，尤其是 $H^\infty$ 端点、$p=q<1$ 等情形缺乏统一刻画。

Method: 以 $[0,1)$ 上的有限正Borel测度 $\mu$ 及系数 $\mu_n=\int_{[0,1)}t^n\,d\mu(t)$ 定义算子；借助Carleson测度理论、Hardy空间实变方法以及辅助函数 $F_{\mu,\alpha}=\mathcal C_{\mu,\alpha}(1)$，将算子有界/紧性转化为测度条件与系数级数条件。

Result: (1) 对 $0<p<q<\infty$、$0<p=q<1$、$0<p\le1$ 且 $q=\infty$，$\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}:H^p\to H^q$ 有界当且仅当 $\mu$ 为 $(\gamma+1/p-1/q)$-Carleson测度，且对 $\gamma$ 无额外限制。(2) 对 $1\le q<p\le\infty$，令 $1/r=1/q-1/p$，有界与紧等价于 $\int_0^1(\mu([t,1))/(1-t)^{\gamma-1/r})^r\,dt/(1-t)<\infty$，也等价于 $F_{\mu,\gamma}\in H^r$ 及 $\sum_{n\ge0}(n+1)^{r\gamma-2}\mu_n^r<\infty$。(3) 对 $1<p<\infty$，$H^p\to H^\infty$ 的有界与紧由平移条件 $F_{\mu,\gamma+1}\in H^{p'}$（$p'=p/(p-1)$）刻画。由此得到全范围 $0<p,q\le\infty$ 的完整有界性分类。

Conclusion: 广义Cesàro算子 $\mathcal C_{\mu,\gamma}$ 在Hardy空间 $H^p$ 到 $H^q$（含 $H^\infty$ 端点）之间的有界性可由测度Carleson条件、积分条件、函数空间成员条件及系数级数条件统一刻画，从而完成全参数范围的分类。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be a finite positive Borel measure on $[0,1)$ and let $γ>0$. We establish sharp mapping criteria for the generalized Cesàro operator \begin{equation*}
  \mathcal C_{μ,γ}f(z)
  =\sum_{n=0}^\infty μ_n
  \left(\sum_{k=0}^n
  \frac{Γ(n-k+γ)}{Γ(γ)(n-k)!}a_k\right)z^n,\qquad z\in \mathbb{D},\end{equation*}
  between Hardy spaces, including the source and target $H^\infty$ endpoints. For $0<p<q<\infty$, for $0<p=q<1$, and for $0<p\le1$ with $q=\infty$, the boundedness of $ \mathcal C_{μ,γ}: H^p \to H^q$ is equivalent to $μ$ being a $(γ+1/p-1/q)$-Carleson measure, without further restrictions on $γ$. For $1\le q<p\le\infty$, set $1/r=1/q-1/p$. In this range, boundedness and compactness are equivalent to \[
  \int_0^1
  \left(\frac{μ([t,1))}{(1-t)^{γ-1/r}}\right)^r
  \frac{dt}{1-t}<\infty. \] This condition is also equivalent to $F_{μ,γ}\in H^r$ and to $\sum_{n\ge0}(n+1)^{rγ-2}μ_n^r<\infty$, where $F_{μ,α}=\mathcal C_{μ,α}(1)$ and $μ_n=\int_{[0,1)}t^n\,dμ(t)$. For $1<p<\infty$, boundedness and compactness from $H^p$ to $H^\infty$ are characterized by the shifted condition $F_{μ,γ+1}\in H^{p'}$, where $p'=p/(p-1)$. We therefore obtain a complete boundedness classification of the generalized Cesàro operators $\mathcal C_{μ,γ}$ between Hardy spaces $H^p$ and $H^q$ for the full range $0<p,q\le\infty$.

</details>


### [166] [Sharp variational, jump and oscillation bounds in a general Gaussian context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19959)
*Valentina Casarino,Paolo Ciatti,Peter Sjögren*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究一般非对称Ornstein-Uhlenbeck半群的跳跃拟半范数L^p有界性与弱(1,1)振荡不等式（阶ϱ=2），基于对半群按时间与空间分解的分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨非对称Ornstein-Uhlenbeck半群在不变测度下的变分/跳跃型不等式，弥补对称情形之外的空白，并厘清有界性失效的根源。

Method: 将半群(\mathcal H_t)按t的小值与大值、以及局部与全局空间区域分解，逐块分析各分量的有界性。

Result: 对1<p<∞证明跳跃拟半范数的L^p界，并建立关于不变测度的弱(1,1)振荡不等式；明确识别出哪些部分保持有界、哪些部分即使在Lebesgue测度下也导致弱/强有界性失效。

Conclusion: 该分解方法能精确定位非对称情形下有界性成立与失效的结构性来源，为阶ϱ=2的分析提供了完整刻画。

Abstract: We consider a general, nonsymmetric Ornstein--Uhlenbeck semigroup $(\mathcal H_t)_{t>0}$. We prove an $L^p$ bound for the jump quasi-seminorms for $1 < p < \infty$ and a weak type (1,1) oscillation inequality, both with respect to the invariant measure. These results are established for the order $\varrho=2$. To do so, we analyze specific components of $(\mathcal H_t)_{t>0}$, by distinguishing between small and large values of $t$, and between local and global spatial zones. This decomposition allows us to explicitly identify which parts of the semigroup remain bounded and which are responsible for the failure of boundedness, both in a weak and in a strong sense, even with respect to Lebesgue measure.

</details>


### [167] [A computer-assisted upper bound of 1.7813 for the real Grothendieck constant](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20074)
*Bo Peng*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该文给出计算机辅助证明：通用实 Grothendieck 常数满足 \(K_G^{\mathbb R}\le 1.7813\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 改进实 Grothendieck 常数 \(K_G^{\mathbb R}\) 的显式上界，超越近期文献中的 \(1.7818666069360661\) 与系统测试值 \(1.7813319810625639\)。

Method: 构造由 11 次奇 Hermite 阈值与 51 次带符号相关多项式组合的方案；通过将标量系数头部展开至 301 次并用加权高斯迹估计控制整个余项，验证充分逆优函数不等式；对有限积分在完整区间划分上给出界，空间外部则解析控制。

Result: 证明 \(K_G^{\mathbb R}\le 1.7813\)，改进了现有两个上界；随文提供可复现全部数值输入的精确参数与源代码。

Conclusion: 该计算机辅助证明进一步收紧了通用实 Grothendieck 常数的显式上界，并提供可复现的数值认证框架。

Abstract: We give a computer-assisted proof that the universal real Grothendieck constant satisfies $K_G^{\mathbb R}\le 1.7813$. The construction combines an explicit odd Hermite threshold of degree $11$ with a signed correlation polynomial of degree $51$. A sufficient inverse-majorant inequality is certified by enclosing the scalar coefficient head through degree $301$ and bounding the entire remaining tail using a weighted Gaussian trace estimate. The finite integral is bounded on a complete interval partition; its spatial exterior is controlled analytically. Exact parameters and source code that regenerate all accepted numerical inputs accompany the paper. The bound improves both the explicit bound $1.7818666069360661$ in the recent literature and the subsequently reported, system-tested value $1.7813319810625639$.

</details>


### [168] [Solutions to some open problems in matrix analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20094)
*Mohamed Amine Aouichaoui,Eun-Young Lee*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文解决了矩阵分析中的若干公开问题，包括加强Aujla--Bourin次可加性不等式、推广互反Lie--Trotter定理、确定收缩与正块矩阵和不等式中的最佳常数，并证明涉及对称模的特征值不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Audenaert、Kittaneh、Bourin等学者提出的若干公开问题，推进矩阵分析中不等式理论的发展。

Method: 通过加强Aujla--Bourin次可加性不等式、将互反Lie--Trotter定理推广到涉及第三个矩阵的乘积形式，并借助不等式分析与最佳常数确定等方法。

Result: 加强了Aujla--Bourin次可加性不等式；将互反Lie--Trotter定理推广到三矩阵乘积；确定收缩和与正块矩阵和不等式的最佳常数，证明三角不等式|A+B+C|≤3/4 I+|A|+|B|+|C|中3/4为最佳；证明涉及对称模(|X|+|X^*|)/2的特征值不等式。

Conclusion: 本文完整回答了Audenaert、Kittaneh及Bourin等人提出的多个公开问题，给出了相关矩阵不等式的最佳形式与最佳常数。

Abstract: This paper deals with several open questions in matrix analysis. We answer a question of Audenaert and Kittaneh by strengthening the Aujla--Bourin subadditivity inequality. We also answer questions posed by Bourin and the second author: (1) we extend the reciprocal Lie--Trotter theorem of Audenaert and Hiai to a product involving a third matrix; (2) we determine the best constants in inequalities for sums of contractions and positive block matrices, including the triangle inequality $$ |A+B+C| \leq \frac{3}{4} I + |A|+|B|+|C| $$ for three contractions $A,B,C$, where $3/4$ cannot be replaced by a smaller constant; and (3) we prove an eigenvalue inequality involving the symmetric modulus $(|X|+|X^*|)/2$ studied by Bourin, Lee, and Zhang.

</details>


### [169] [Grothendieck and $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck subspaces of $\ell_\infty$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20170)
*Manuel González,Tomasz Kania*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明 $\ell_\infty$ 含有 $2^{\mathfrak{c}}$ 个两两不同构的非自反 Grothendieck 子空间，并研究相对 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 性质的相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 给出 2021 年作者所提问题的最优解答，并探究相对 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 性质是否为子空间同构不变量，以及推广到一般闭子空间 $E$ 的情形。

Method: 构造 $\ell_\infty$ 中大量两两不同构的非自反 Grothendieck 子空间，并证明其可同时包含 $c_0$ 的典范拷贝且具有无穷维自反商；进一步构造与 $\ell_\infty\oplus_\infty E$ 同构的 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 子空间。

Result: $\ell_\infty$ 含有 $2^{\mathfrak{c}}$ 个两两不同构的非自反 Grothendieck 子空间，均为最优结果；相对 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 性质依赖于所指定的 $c_0$ 拷贝，非同构不变量；对任意闭子空间 $E\subseteq\ell_\infty$，存在与 $\ell_\infty\oplus_\infty E$ 同构的 $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck 子空间。

Conclusion: 本文解决了作者 2021 年的问题，并回答了 González 等人的问题（取 $E=c_0$ 情形），揭示了相对 Grothendieck 性质的非同构不变性。

Abstract: Let $\mathfrak{c}=2^{\aleph_0}$. We prove that $\ell_\infty$ contains $2^{\mathfrak{c}}$ pairwise non-isomorphic non-reflexive Grothendieck subspaces. They may all be chosen to contain the canonical copy of $c_0$ and to have infinite-dimensional reflexive quotient. This is optimal and answers by the authors in 2021. We also show that the relative $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck property depends on the specified copy of $c_0$ and is not an isomorphic invariant of the subspace. More generally, for every closed $E\subseteq\ell_\infty$ there is an $\ell_\infty$-Grothendieck subspace isomorphic to $\ell_\infty\oplus_\infty E$; taking $E=c_0$ answers a problem by González et al.

</details>


### [170] [A logarithmically monotone symmetric norm which is not fully symmetric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20270)
*Jinghao Huang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 构造了关于对数次多数化单调、对称但非强对称的Banach函数空间，并构造了强对称、关于对数次多数化单调但无等价完全对称范数的空间，回答了Dodds等人的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答Dodds、Dodds、Sukochev与Zanin提出的问题，厘清对数次多数化单调性与（强/完全）对称范数之间的关系。

Method: 在$(0,\infty)$上显式构造Banach函数空间及其对称范数，通过具体反例验证对称性层级与单调性条件。

Result: 构造出关于对数次多数化单调的对称Banach范数，但它不是强对称的，因而也不是完全对称的；同时构造出关于对数次多数化单调的强对称Banach函数空间，其不具有等价的完全对称范数。

Conclusion: 对称性层级严格分明：对数次多数化单调性不足以保证强对称或完全对称，且强对称空间未必能赋予等价的完全对称范数；从而给出原问题的否定性答案。

Abstract: We construct an explicit symmetric Banach norm on a function space over $(0,\infty)$ which is monotone with respect to logarithmic submajorization but is not strongly symmetric, and hence is not fully symmetric. This answers a question raised by Dodds, Dodds, Sukochev, and Zanin. We also construct a strongly symmetric Banach function space over $(0,\infty)$ which is monotone with respect to logarithmic submajorization and admits no equivalent fully symmetric norm.

</details>


### [171] [The $A$-optimal design problem and the $S$-matrix conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20306)
*Teng Zhang*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文证明了Sloane与Harwit于1976年提出的$S$-矩阵猜想：对任意元素在$[0,1]$中的非奇异实$n\times n$矩阵$A$，有$\|A^{-1}\|_F\ge\frac{2n}{n+1}$，且等号成立当且仅当$A$为$S$-矩阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该猜想源于光谱学中的$A$-最优设计问题，自1976年提出以来长期未获解决，具有重要的理论意义。

Method: 将问题按维度奇偶分治：在奇数维情形下结合一个变分迹不等式（variational trace inequality），在偶数维情形下利用关于中心化矩阵Moore–Penrose逆的一个精确的范围约束估计（sharp range-constrained estimate）。

Result: 成功证明了$S$-矩阵猜想，同时给出了取等条件——等号成立当且仅当$A$是$S$-矩阵。

Conclusion: $S$-矩阵猜想获得完整证明，解决了该领域一个长期悬而未决的问题。

Abstract: The $S$-matrix conjecture was formulated by Sloane and Harwit in 1976, motivated by an $A$-optimal design problem arising in spectroscopy. It states that if $A$ is a nonsingular real $n\times n$ matrix whose entries lie in $[0,1]$, then $$\left\|A^{-1}\right\|_F\ge\frac{2n}{n+1}.$$ Moreover, equality holds if and only if $A$ is an $S$-matrix. In this paper, we prove the conjecture by combining a variational trace inequality in odd dimensions with a sharp range-constrained estimate for the Moore--Penrose inverse of a centered matrix in even dimensions.

</details>


### [172] [Lévy measures for Dirichlet-type spaces on the unit bidisc](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20346)
*Santu Bera,Shanola S. Sequeira*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文给出单位双圆盘上Dirichlet型空间$\mathcal D(\rho^{(1)},\rho^{(2)})$的矩序列所关联的完全交替多序列的Lévy测度$\\nu_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}$的显式表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Dirichlet型空间上坐标乘子相关的完全交替多序列的Lévy测度表示，揭示其与边界测度经$S(z)=|z|^2$推前测度之间的联系。

Method: 在单位双圆盘$\mathbb D^2$上考虑由有限正Borel测度$\rho^{(1)},\rho^{(2)}$定义的Dirichlet型空间$\mathcal D(\rho^{(1)},\rho^{(2)})$，考察矩序列$\{\|z^\alpha\|^2\}_{\alpha\in\mathbb Z_+^2}$对应的完全交替多序列及其Lévy测度，利用推前测度$S_*\rho^{(i)}$与Dirac测度$\delta_1$进行分解表示。

Result: 在$[0,1]^2\backslash\{(1,1)\}$上得到显式表示：$d\nu_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}(x)=\frac{1}{1-x_1}d((S_*\rho^{(1)})\times\delta_1)(x)+\frac{1}{1-x_2}d(\delta_1\times(S_*\rho^{(2)}))(x)$，其中$S_*\rho^{(i)}$为$\rho^{(i)}$经$S(z)=|z|^2$的推前测度。

Conclusion: Lévy测度可完全由两个单变量推前测度$S_*\rho^{(1)}$、$S_*\rho^{(2)}$与权函数$\frac{1}{1-x_i}$的乘积测度显式刻画，除对角点$(1,1)$外成立。

Abstract: For the Dirichlet-type space $\mathcal D(ρ^{(1)},ρ^{(2)})$ on the unit bidisc $\mathbb D^2,$ where $ρ^{(1)}$ and $ρ^{(2)}$ are finite positive Borel measures on the closed unit disc $\overline{\mathbb D},$ we show that the Lévy measure $ν_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}$ associated with the completely alternating multisequence $\left\{\|z^α\|_{\mathcal D(ρ^{(1)},ρ^{(2)})}^2 \right\}_{α\in\mathbb Z_+^2}$ admits the explicit representation
  \begin{equation*}
  dν_{(\mathscr M_z,1)}(x)=\frac{1}{1-x_1}d((S_{*}ρ^{(1)})\timesδ_1)(x)+\frac{1}{1-x_2}d(δ_1\times (S_{*}ρ^{(2)}))(x)
  \end{equation*} 
  on $[0,1]^2\backslash\{(1,1)\},$ where $S_{*}ρ^{(i)},$ $i=1,2$, denotes the pushforward measure of $ρ^{(i)}$ by the map $S:\overline{\mathbb D}\rightarrow [0,1]$ defined by $S(z)=|z|^2,$ $z\in \overline{\mathbb D},$ and $δ_1$ denotes the Dirac measure at 1.

</details>


### [173] [The best Musielak-Orlicz approximation by linear subspaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20466)
*Juan Costa Ponce,Sergio Favier,Fabián Levis*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 研究 Musielak-Orlicz 空间中的最佳模逼近问题，引入 Orlicz 函数在原点的几何分类，在极小假设下建立存在性、基于侧导数的变分刻画，并给出唯一性准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Musielak-Orlicz 空间中刻画“类加权 L^1”行为并研究相应的最佳模逼近。目标是弱化假设以推广已有逼近结论，并借助几何分类统一处理存在性与唯一性。

Method: 引入 Orlicz 函数在原点的几何分类以隔离类加权 L^1 行为；在极小假设下证明最佳模逼近的存在性；利用侧导数给出极小元的变分刻画（最优性条件）；建立唯一性准则，包括一维 Haar 型定理，以及高维情形下 0- 与 1- 空间中的特征刻画。

Result: 在极小假设下得到了最佳模逼近的存在性结果；给出了极大元（极小元）的侧导数变分刻画；建立了一维 Haar 型唯一性定理，并在高维 0- 与 1- 空间中给出了唯一性的特征刻画。

Conclusion: 研究表明，Orlicz 函数在原点的几何分类是分析 Musielak-Orlicz 空间中类加权 L^1 逼近行为的有效工具，可统一处理最佳模逼近的存在性、变分刻画与唯一性。

Abstract: We study best modular approximation in Musielak-Orlicz spaces. A geometric classification near the origin of Orlicz functions is introduced to isolate weighted $L^1$-like behavior. We establish existence results under minimal hypotheses and provide a variational characterization of minimizers via lateral derivatives. Finally, we establish uniqueness criteria, including a one-dimensional Haar-type theorem and characterizations in $0$- and $1$-spaces in higher dimensions.

</details>


### [174] [Zero-product problem for Toeplitz operators on the Fock space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20555)
*Jie Qin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 本文解答了Bauer与Le关于Fock空间上Toeplitz算子零积的公开问题，通过构造非径向Schwartz符号，分别在n≥2和n=1情形下给出Toeplitz算子非零但乘积为零的具体例子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bauer和Le在[JFA, 261 (2011)]中提出了关于Fock空间F^2(C^n)上Toeplitz算子零积的问题，即是否存在两个非零Toeplitz算子其乘积为零。该问题涉及算子代数结构与符号函数性质的深刻联系，对此前缺乏完整构造。

Method: 针对n≥2，构造两个有界非径向Schwartz符号，使其Toeplitz算子在F^2(C^n)上非零但乘积为零；针对n=1及每个c∈(1/2,1)，构造两个光滑非径向符号，其增长至多被Ce^{c|z|^2}控制，对应的扩展Toeplitz算子在F^2(C)上非零且乘积在所有全纯多项式上为零；当c≥3/4时第二个符号有界。证明工具包括高斯核计算、矩阵恒等式、theta函数及Fourier变换。

Result: 在n≥2时，成功构造出两个有界非径向Schwartz符号，其Toeplitz算子非零且在F^2(C^n)上乘积为零。在n=1时，对每个c∈(1/2,1)构造出两个增长速度至多Ce^{c|z|^2}的光滑非径向符号，其扩展Toeplitz算子非零且乘积在所有全纯多项式上为零；当c≥3/4时第二个符号有界。

Conclusion: 该工作完整回答了Bauer和Le的问题，表明在Fock空间上确实存在非零Toeplitz算子其乘积为零的实例，且在高维与一维情形下分别给出不同增长条件的构造，深化了对Toeplitz算子代数结构的理解。

Abstract: We answer Bauer and Le's question on zero products of Toeplitz operators on the Fock space $F^2(\mathbb C^n)$[JFA, 261 (2011), 9, 2617--2640]. For $n\ge2$, we construct two bounded nonradial Schwartz symbols on $\mathbb C^n$ whose Toeplitz operators are nonzero and have zero product on $F^2(\mathbb C^n)$. For $n=1$ and each $c\in(1/2,1)$, we construct two smooth nonradial symbols of growth at most $Ce^{c|z|^2}$ for some constant $C>0$. Their extended Toeplitz operators in $F^2(\mathbb C)$ are nonzero and have zero product on all holomorphic polynomials. Moreover, the second symbol is bounded when $c\geq3/4$. Our proofs use Gaussian kernel calculations, matrix identities, theta functions and Fourier transform.

</details>


### [175] [Normal-Direction Energy and Fourier Restriction for Convex Planar Curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20643)
*Vicente Vergara*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 通过Gauss映射组织质量测度,引入转折测度与终端切向尺度,建立局部L^4傅里叶延拓估计及加权版本;对单项曲线给出显式终端尺度与多尺度能量表示,在Frostman条件下得到临界阈值s_c(k)=(k-2)/(3k-4)及尖锐增长率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究紧致凸C^2平面曲线上的傅里叶延拓问题,需要一种能刻画曲线在不同位置、不同方向上的"可线性化尺度"的工具,从而获得与位置相关的角分辨率,并据此统一处理局部L^4延拓估计与加权估计。

Method: 用Gauss映射N将质量测度|f|^2 dσ推前为法向分布测度ν_f;由转折测度dμ_κ=κ dσ定义终端切向尺度r_R(ξ)(满足r_R(ξ)μ_κ(B_Γ(ξ,r_R(ξ)))≍R^{-1})及位置相关角分辨率ρ_R(ξ)=R^{-1}/r_R(ξ);在μ_κ的加倍假设下识别其为曲线线性化到精度R^{-1}的最大尺度;由此构造法向能量与终端分解、横向几何;并对单项曲线γ_k(t)=(t,t^k)显式求解终端尺度,用ν_f的角相关给出能量的多尺度表示。

Result: 得到局部L^4傅里叶延拓估计及其加权变体;对γ_k(t)=(t,t^k),k≥3实,在ν_f满足s维Frostman条件下给出增长图与临界阈值s_c(k)=(k-2)/(3k-4),区分平坦点区域与非退化区域,所得增长率(含临界对数修正)在能量层面是尖锐的。

Conclusion: 以Gauss映射推前和转折测度诱导的终端尺度为核心,构建了一套位置自适应的法向能量框架,统一处理平面凸曲线上的L^4延拓估计;对单项曲线给出显式可算的尺度与尖锐的临界指数s_c(k)=(k-2)/(3k-4)。

Abstract: We study Fourier extension from compact convex $C^2$ planar curves by organizing the mass $|f|^2\,\mathrm dσ$ through the Gauss map. The pushforward measure \[ ν_f= N_\#\bigl(|f|^2\,\mathrm dσ\bigr) \] records its distribution in normal directions. The turning measure $\mathrm dμ_κ=κ\,\mathrm dσ$ determines an intrinsic terminal tangential scale $r_R(ξ)$ through \[ r_R(ξ)\, μ_κ\bigl(B_Γ(ξ,r_R(ξ))\bigr) \asymp R^{-1}, \] and hence a position-dependent angular resolution $ρ_R(ξ)=R^{-1}/r_R(ξ)$. Under a doubling hypothesis on $μ_κ$, $r_R$ is, up to structural constants, the largest scale on which the curve can be linearized to precision $R^{-1}$. These scales define a normal-direction energy, and the associated terminal decomposition and transverse geometry yield local $L^4$ Fourier extension estimates and weighted variants.
  For the monomial curves $γ_k(t)=(t,t^k)$, $k\geq3$ real, the terminal scales are explicit and the energy admits a multiscale representation in terms of angular correlations of $ν_f$. Under an $s$-dimensional Frostman condition on $ν_f$, this yields a growth diagram with critical threshold \[ s_c(k)=\frac{k-2}{3k-4}, \] separating the flat-point and nondegenerate regimes. The resulting rates, including the critical logarithmic correction, are sharp at the energy level.

</details>


### [176] [A Framework for Supervised and Unsupervised Learning via Reproducing Kernel Hilbert Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20792)
*Frédéric Protin*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 提出基于嵌套再生核希尔伯特空间(RKHS)的统一监督/无监督学习框架，将有限层RKHS的计算结构与全L²空间的逼近能力结合，并在密度估计与二分类中建立几乎必然收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一固定RKHS难以兼顾计算可行性与对整体L²空间的逼近能力；需要一个既能利用RKHS有限维计算结构、又能达到环境L²空间逼近精度的统一统计学习框架。

Method: 定义嵌套RKHS：一族递增的RKHS，其并集在环境L²空间中稠密。先在固定RKHS内建立连续嵌入L²时随机元素经验平均的强收敛结论；再借助并集稠密性，将有限层统计收敛与整体逼近结合。用于概率密度估计（对目标密度在RKHS上的正交投影构造显式经验估计，并用对角选择得到收敛序列）和二元贝叶斯分类（将贝叶斯目标的逼近表述为总体/经验风险泛函最小化）。

Result: 在固定RKHS下，无监督与监督情形均获得随机元素经验平均的强收敛；密度估计中对正交投影的经验估计几乎必然收敛，对角选择得到几乎必然L²收敛到目标密度的估计序列；二分类中证明经验最小化子的相合性，并借助嵌套结构获得向贝叶斯目标的L²收敛，同时给出超平面与加权距离的几何解释及总体风险对应误分类概率的概率解释。

Conclusion: 嵌套RKHS为监督与无监督学习提供统一框架，兼顾RKHS计算结构与L²逼近能力，在密度估计与贝叶斯二分类中得到一致的收敛与相合性保证，并揭示了几何与概率层面的解释。

Abstract: We introduce a unified framework for supervised and unsupervised learning based on reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHSs). We introduce the notion of a nested RKHS, namely an increasing sequence of RKHSs whose union is dense in an ambient $L^2$-space. This construction makes it possible to exploit the computational structure of RKHS methods at each finite approximation level while retaining the approximation power of the full $L^2$-space.
  We first study statistical learning in a fixed RKHS. In both unsupervised and supervised settings, we establish strong convergence results for empirical averages of random elements taking values in an RKHS continuously embedded in $L^2$. We then use the nested RKHS structure to combine these statistical convergence results with the density of the union of the RKHSs in the ambient $L^2$-space. We apply this construction to probability density estimation and binary Bayesian classification. For density estimation, we construct explicit empirical estimators of the orthogonal projections of the target density onto the RKHSs and establish their almost sure convergence. A diagonal selection procedure then yields a sequence of estimators converging almost surely to the target density in $L^2$.
  For binary Bayesian classification, we formulate the approximation of the Bayes target as the minimization of suitable population and empirical risk functionals. We prove consistency of the empirical minimizers and use the nested structure to obtain convergence towards the Bayes target in $L^2$. We further provide a geometric interpretation in terms of hyperplanes and weighted distances in the ambient $L^2$-space. Finally, we show that the population risk admits a direct probabilistic interpretation in terms of the probability of misclassification.

</details>
